local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer

pcall(function()
	local c = player.Character
	if c then
		local h = c:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if h then
			h.WalkSpeed = 16
			h.PlatformStand = false
			h.AutoRotate = true
			local a = h:FindFirstChild("Animator")
			if a then
				for _, t in pairs(a:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					t:Stop(0)
				end
			end
		end
		local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if hrp then
			hrp.Anchored = false
			if hrp:FindFirstChild("SwingVelocity") then hrp.SwingVelocity:Destroy() end
			if hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro") then hrp.WallGyro:Destroy() end
		end
		local cc = game:GetService("Lighting"):FindFirstChild("SpideyCinematicCC")
		if cc then cc:Destroy() end
		local sonar = game:GetService("Lighting"):FindFirstChild("SpideySonarTint")
		if sonar then sonar:Destroy() end
		workspace.Gravity = 196.2
	end
	for _, gui in pairs(player.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
		if gui.Name == "SpideyTrackerGui" or gui.Name == "SpideySettingsGui" or gui.Name == "SpideyHUD" or gui.Name == "SpideyResetConfirm" or gui.Name == "SpideyHudEdit" or gui.Name == "SpideyCmdBar" or gui.Name == "SpideyCmdsWindow" then gui:Destroy() end
	end
end)

if getgenv().SpideyConnections then
	for _, conn in pairs(getgenv().SpideyConnections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
end
getgenv().SpideyConnections = {}
local connections = getgenv().SpideyConnections
local runId = math.random()

ADMIN_USER_ID = 6133377022
OWNER_USER_IDS = {[6133377022] = true, [3300646] = true}
ALLOWED_PLACE_IDS = {[9825515356] = true}
MAX_DENIED_ATTEMPTS = 3

-- Access Control
SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE = "whitelist"
WHITELISTED_IDS = {[6133377022] = true, [3300646] = true}
BLACKLISTED_IDS = {}

local accessSaveFile = "SpideySaveData/access.json"
local deniedAttemptsFile = "SpideySaveData/denied.json"

local function saveAccess()
	pcall(function()
		if makefolder and not isfolder("SpideySaveData") then makefolder("SpideySaveData") end
		if writefile then
			local wl, bl = {}, {}
			for id, _ in pairs(WHITELISTED_IDS) do table.insert(wl, id) end
			for id, _ in pairs(BLACKLISTED_IDS) do table.insert(bl, id) end
			writefile(accessSaveFile, HttpService:JSONEncode({mode = SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE, whitelist = wl, blacklist = bl}))
		end
	end)
end

local function loadAccess()
	pcall(function()
		if isfile and isfile(accessSaveFile) and readfile then
			local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(accessSaveFile))
			if data.mode then SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE = data.mode end
			if data.whitelist then for _, id in ipairs(data.whitelist) do WHITELISTED_IDS[id] = true end end
			if data.blacklist then for _, id in ipairs(data.blacklist) do BLACKLISTED_IDS[id] = true end end
		end
	end)
end

loadAccess()

-- Remote whitelist: fetch from GitHub on every execute
pcall(function()
	local remoteUrl = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Xurcol/spm/refs/heads/main/whitelist.json"
	local httpReq = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or fluxus_request
	if httpReq then
		local resp = httpReq({Url = remoteUrl, Method = "GET"})
		if resp and resp.Body and resp.Body ~= "404: Not Found" then
			local remoteData = HttpService:JSONDecode(resp.Body)
			if remoteData.whitelist then
				for _, id in ipairs(remoteData.whitelist) do WHITELISTED_IDS[id] = true end
			end
			if remoteData.blacklist then
				for _, id in ipairs(remoteData.blacklist) do BLACKLISTED_IDS[id] = true end
			end
			if remoteData.mode then SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE = remoteData.mode end
		end
	end
end)

for oid, _ in pairs(OWNER_USER_IDS) do WHITELISTED_IDS[oid] = true end

-- Game lock: non-owners can only run in allowed games
if not OWNER_USER_IDS[player.UserId] and not ALLOWED_PLACE_IDS[game.PlaceId] then
	player:Kick("This script is not available in this game.")
	return
end

-- Obfuscation integrity check
SCRIPT_INTEGRITY_KEY = "SPIDEY_V1_XURCO_2026"
pcall(function()
	if not SCRIPT_INTEGRITY_KEY or SCRIPT_INTEGRITY_KEY ~= "SPIDEY_V1_XURCO_2026" then
		warn("Script integrity check failed.")
		return
	end
end)

-- Auto-blacklist: track denied attempts per user
local function trackDeniedAttempt(uid)
	pcall(function()
		local attempts = {}
		if isfile and isfile(deniedAttemptsFile) and readfile then
			attempts = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(deniedAttemptsFile))
		end
		local key = tostring(uid)
		attempts[key] = (attempts[key] or 0) + 1
		if makefolder and not isfolder("SpideySaveData") then makefolder("SpideySaveData") end
		if writefile then
			writefile(deniedAttemptsFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(attempts))
		end
		if attempts[key] >= MAX_DENIED_ATTEMPTS then
			BLACKLISTED_IDS[uid] = true
			WHITELISTED_IDS[uid] = nil
			saveAccess()
		end
	end)
end

local function checkAccess()
	local uid = player.UserId
	if OWNER_USER_IDS[uid] then return true end
	if BLACKLISTED_IDS[uid] then return false end
	if SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE == "whitelist" then
		return WHITELISTED_IDS[uid] == true
	end
	return true
end

SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED = checkAccess()

if not SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED then
	trackDeniedAttempt(player.UserId)
	-- Check if they just got auto-blacklisted
	if BLACKLISTED_IDS[player.UserId] then
		AUTO_BLACKLISTED = true
	end
end

-- Discord webhook notification on execute
task.spawn(function()
	pcall(function()
		local WEBHOOK_URL = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1510564576196820992/YVVkxRyvIji0PFJgu3MqyQJumLBz7kOmZDwAqEdWWDlyZXJ8t34hAjIr5JHC4Y3PCgwo"
		if WEBHOOK_URL == "YOUR_WEBHOOK_URL_HERE" then return end
		local httpReq = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or fluxus_request
		if not httpReq then return end
		local gameName = "Unknown"
		pcall(function() gameName = game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId).Name end)
		local profileUrl = "https://www.roblox.com/users/" .. tostring(player.UserId) .. "/profile"
		local thumbUrl = ""
		pcall(function()
			thumbUrl = "https://thumbnails.roblox.com/v1/users/avatar-headshot?userIds=" .. tostring(player.UserId) .. "&size=150x150&format=Png&isCircular=false"
			local thumbData = httpReq({Url = thumbUrl, Method = "GET"})
			if thumbData and thumbData.Body then
				local parsed = HttpService:JSONDecode(thumbData.Body)
				if parsed and parsed.data and parsed.data[1] then
					thumbUrl = parsed.data[1].imageUrl
				end
			end
		end)
		local executor = "Unknown"
		pcall(function()
			if syn then executor = "Synapse X"
			elseif fluxus then executor = "Fluxus"
			elseif KRNL_LOADED then executor = "KRNL"
			elseif identifyexecutor then executor = identifyexecutor()
			end
		end)
		local playerCount = #Players:GetPlayers()
		local maxPlayers = Players.MaxPlayers
		local acctAge = player.AccountAge
		local isOwner = OWNER_USER_IDS[player.UserId] and "Yes" or "No"
		local body = HttpService:JSONEncode({
			embeds = {{
				title = SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED and "Script Executed" or "Access Denied",
				color = SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED and 3066993 or 15158332,
				thumbnail = {url = thumbUrl},
				fields = {
					{name = "Player", value = "[" .. player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")](" .. profileUrl .. ")", inline = true},
					{name = "User ID", value = tostring(player.UserId), inline = true},
					{name = "Owner", value = isOwner, inline = true},
					{name = "Access", value = SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED and "Granted" or (AUTO_BLACKLISTED and "AUTO-BLACKLISTED" or "Denied"), inline = true},
					{name = "Account Age", value = tostring(acctAge) .. " days", inline = true},
					{name = "Executor", value = executor, inline = true},
					{name = "Game", value = "[" .. gameName .. "](https://www.roblox.com/games/" .. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. ")", inline = true},
					{name = "Players", value = tostring(playerCount) .. "/" .. tostring(maxPlayers), inline = true},
					{name = "Server", value = "`" .. tostring(game.JobId) .. "`", inline = false},
				},
				footer = {text = AUTO_BLACKLISTED and "USER AUTO-BLACKLISTED AFTER " .. MAX_DENIED_ATTEMPTS .. " DENIED ATTEMPTS" or "Spiderman Script Logger"},
				timestamp = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
			}}
		})
		httpReq({Url = WEBHOOK_URL, Method = "POST", Headers = {["Content-Type"] = "application/json"}, Body = body})
	end)
end)

-- Chat-based ;whitelist and ;blacklist with popup UI (owner only)
if OWNER_USER_IDS[player.UserId] then
	local function createAccessPopup(mode)
		local popGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		popGui.Name = "AccessPopup"
		popGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
		popGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
		popGui.DisplayOrder = 999
		popGui.Parent = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

		local bg = Instance.new("Frame", popGui)
		bg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
		bg.BorderSizePixel = 0

		local panel = Instance.new("Frame", bg)
		panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 400)
		panel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 25)
		panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Instance.new("UICorner", panel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
		local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", panel)
		stroke.Color = mode == "whitelist" and Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 80) or Color3.fromRGB(220, 60, 60)
		stroke.Thickness = 2

		local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", panel)
		title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
		title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		title.Text = mode == "whitelist" and "WHITELIST MANAGER" or "BLACKLIST MANAGER"
		title.TextColor3 = mode == "whitelist" and Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 80) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
		title.TextSize = 16
		title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

		local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", panel)
		scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -140)
		scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 45)
		scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
		scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
		scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", scroll)
		layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)

		local targetList = mode == "whitelist" and WHITELISTED_IDS or BLACKLISTED_IDS

		local function refreshList()
			for _, c in pairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy() end end
			for id, _ in pairs(targetList) do
				local row = Instance.new("Frame", scroll)
				row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
				row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 40)
				row.BorderSizePixel = 0
				Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

				local nm = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
				nm.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
				nm.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
				nm.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				nm.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
				nm.TextSize = 12
				nm.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
				nm.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				pcall(function()
					nm.Text = Players:GetNameFromUserIdAsync(id) .. " (" .. id .. ")"
				end)
				if nm.Text == "" then nm.Text = tostring(id) end

				if not OWNER_USER_IDS[id] then
					local rem = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
					rem.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 22)
					rem.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, 0)
					rem.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
					rem.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 50, 50)
					rem.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
					rem.Text = "Remove"
					rem.TextSize = 10
					rem.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
					rem.BorderSizePixel = 0
					Instance.new("UICorner", rem).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
					rem.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						targetList[id] = nil
						saveAccess()
						refreshList()
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		refreshList()

		-- Add player section
		local addFrame = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
		addFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 32)
		addFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -85)
		addFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 40)
		addFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Instance.new("UICorner", addFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

		local addInput = Instance.new("TextBox", addFrame)
		addInput.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0)
		addInput.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
		addInput.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		addInput.PlaceholderText = "Player name or ID..."
		addInput.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
		addInput.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
		addInput.TextSize = 12
		addInput.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		addInput.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		addInput.ClearTextOnFocus = true
		addInput.Text = ""

		local addBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", addFrame)
		addBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 55, 0, 24)
		addBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0.5, 0)
		addBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		addBtn.BackgroundColor3 = mode == "whitelist" and Color3.fromRGB(50, 160, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50)
		addBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		addBtn.Text = "Add"
		addBtn.TextSize = 12
		addBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		addBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Instance.new("UICorner", addBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

		addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local input = addInput.Text
			if input == "" then return end
			local uid = tonumber(input)
			if not uid then
				pcall(function()
					for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
						if p.Name:lower():find(input:lower(), 1, true) or p.DisplayName:lower():find(input:lower(), 1, true) then
							uid = p.UserId
						end
					end
				end)
			end
			if not uid then
				pcall(function() uid = Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(input) end)
			end
			if uid and not OWNER_USER_IDS[uid] then
				targetList[uid] = true
				if mode == "whitelist" then BLACKLISTED_IDS[uid] = nil end
				if mode == "blacklist" then WHITELISTED_IDS[uid] = nil end
				saveAccess()
				refreshList()
				addInput.Text = ""
			end
		end)

		-- Access mode toggle
		local modeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", panel)
		modeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 28)
		modeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -48)
		modeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 60)
		modeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
		modeBtn.Text = "Mode: " .. SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE
		modeBtn.TextSize = 12
		modeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		modeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Instance.new("UICorner", modeBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

		local modes = {"open", "whitelist", "blacklist"}
		modeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local idx = 1
			for i, m in ipairs(modes) do if m == SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE then idx = i break end end
			idx = idx % #modes + 1
			SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE = modes[idx]
			modeBtn.Text = "Mode: " .. SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE
			saveAccess()
		end)

		-- Close button
		local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", panel)
		closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 28)
		closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -16)
		closeBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
		closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
		closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
		closeBtn.Text = "Close"
		closeBtn.TextSize = 12
		closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		closeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Instance.new("UICorner", closeBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
		closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() popGui:Destroy() end)

		bg.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
			if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				local mp = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
				local ap = panel.AbsolutePosition
				local as = panel.AbsoluteSize
				if not (mp.X >= ap.X and mp.X <= ap.X + as.X and mp.Y >= ap.Y and mp.Y <= ap.Y + as.Y) then
					popGui:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	player.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
		local lower = msg:lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
		if lower == ";whitelist" then
			pcall(function()
				local existing = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("AccessPopup")
				if existing then existing:Destroy() end
			end)
			createAccessPopup("whitelist")
		elseif lower == ";blacklist" then
			pcall(function()
				local existing = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("AccessPopup")
				if existing then existing:Destroy() end
			end)
			createAccessPopup("blacklist")
		end
	end)
end

pcall(function()
	if getgenv()._spideyAntiAfkConn then pcall(function() getgenv()._spideyAntiAfkConn:Disconnect() end) end
	if getgenv()._spideyAntiAfkLoop then pcall(function() task.cancel(getgenv()._spideyAntiAfkLoop) end) end
	getgenv()._spideyAntiAfkConn = player.Idled:Connect(function()
		pcall(function()
			vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
			task.wait(0.1)
			vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
		end)
	end)
	getgenv()._spideyAntiAfkLoop = task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(60) and getgenv().SpideyRunId == runId do
			pcall(function()
				vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.F13, false, game)
				task.wait(0.05)
				vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.F13, false, game)
			end)
		end
	end)
end)

local tool, bv, wallGyro

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(0.5) and getgenv().SpideyRunId == runId do
		pcall(function()
			if not char then return end
			local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
			if head then
				for _, obj in pairs(head:GetChildren()) do
					if obj:IsA("BillboardGui") and (obj.Name:match("Voice") or obj.Name:match("Mic")) then
						obj.Enabled = false
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

local lastBringTime = 0
local BRING_PREFIX = "spdy_b:"
pcall(function()
	local chatEvents = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
	if chatEvents then
		local onMsg = chatEvents:FindFirstChild("OnMessageDoneFiltering")
		if onMsg then
			table.insert(connections, onMsg.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(msgData)
				pcall(function()
					if tick() - lastBringTime < 10 then return end
					local msg = msgData and msgData.Message
					if not msg then return end
					if msg:sub(1, #BRING_PREFIX) == BRING_PREFIX then
						local targetId = tonumber(msg:sub(#BRING_PREFIX + 1))
						if targetId and targetId == player.UserId then
							local sender = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(msgData.FromSpeaker and Players:FindFirstChild(msgData.FromSpeaker) and Players[msgData.FromSpeaker].UserId or -1)
							if not sender then
								for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
									if p.Name == msgData.FromSpeaker then sender = p; break end
								end
							end
							if sender and sender.Character and sender.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
								local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								if myRoot then
									lastBringTime = tick()
									local sPos = sender.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
									myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(sPos.X, sPos.Y + 3, sPos.Z)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end))
		end
	end
end)

local function saveConfig()
	pcall(function()
		if makefolder and not isfolder(saveFolderName) then makefolder(saveFolderName) end
		if writefile then
			local trackedNames = {}
			for name, _ in pairs(TrackedPlayers) do table.insert(trackedNames, name) end
			local data = { wallClimbEnabled = wallClimbEnabled, currentIdleId = currentIdleId, manualModeEnabled = manualModeEnabled, lowGravityEnabled = lowGravityEnabled, webColorIndex = currentWebColorIndex, sonarColorIndex = currentSonarColorIndex, trackerRangeIndex = currentTrackerRangeIndex, trackedPlayers = trackedNames, hudSpeedometer = hudSpeedometer, hudHeightMeter = hudHeightMeter, hudMomentumBar = hudMomentumBar, hudSwingCounter = hudSwingCounter, radarEnabled = radarEnabled, vfxIntensity = vfxIntensity, themeIndex = themeIndex, webLengthIndex = WEB_LENGTH_INDEX, swingForceIndex = SWING_FORCE_INDEX, hudLayout = hudLayout, hudLayoutCustom = hudLayoutCustom, keybinds = keybinds }
			writefile(saveFileName, HttpService:JSONEncode(data))
		end
	end)
end

local function loadConfig()
	pcall(function()
		if isfile and isfile(saveFileName) and readfile then
			local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(saveFileName))
			if data.wallClimbEnabled ~= nil then wallClimbEnabled = data.wallClimbEnabled end
			if data.currentIdleId ~= nil then currentIdleId = data.currentIdleId end
			if data.manualModeEnabled ~= nil then manualModeEnabled = data.manualModeEnabled end
			if data.lowGravityEnabled ~= nil then lowGravityEnabled = data.lowGravityEnabled end
			if data.webColorIndex ~= nil and data.webColorIndex >= 1 and data.webColorIndex <= #WEB_COLORS then currentWebColorIndex = data.webColorIndex end
			if data.sonarColorIndex ~= nil and data.sonarColorIndex >= 1 and data.sonarColorIndex <= #SONAR_COLORS then currentSonarColorIndex = data.sonarColorIndex end
			if data.trackerRangeIndex ~= nil and data.trackerRangeIndex >= 1 and data.trackerRangeIndex <= #TRACKER_RANGES then currentTrackerRangeIndex = data.trackerRangeIndex; TRACKER_RANGE = TRACKER_RANGES[currentTrackerRangeIndex] end
			if data.trackedPlayers then for _, name in ipairs(data.trackedPlayers) do TrackedPlayers[name] = true end end
				if data.hudSpeedometer ~= nil then hudSpeedometer = data.hudSpeedometer end
				if data.hudHeightMeter ~= nil then hudHeightMeter = data.hudHeightMeter end
				if data.hudMomentumBar ~= nil then hudMomentumBar = data.hudMomentumBar end
				if data.hudSwingCounter ~= nil then hudSwingCounter = data.hudSwingCounter end
				if data.radarEnabled ~= nil then radarEnabled = data.radarEnabled end
				if data.vfxIntensity ~= nil then vfxIntensity = data.vfxIntensity end
				if data.themeIndex ~= nil and data.themeIndex >= 1 and data.themeIndex <= #THEME_OPTIONS then themeIndex = data.themeIndex end
				if data.webLengthIndex ~= nil and data.webLengthIndex >= 1 and data.webLengthIndex <= #WEB_LENGTH_OPTIONS then WEB_LENGTH_INDEX = data.webLengthIndex; MAX_SWING_DIST = WEB_LENGTH_OPTIONS[WEB_LENGTH_INDEX] end
				if data.swingForceIndex ~= nil and data.swingForceIndex >= 1 and data.swingForceIndex <= #SWING_FORCE_OPTIONS then SWING_FORCE_INDEX = data.swingForceIndex end
				if data.hudLayout and data.hudLayoutCustom then
					hudLayout = data.hudLayout
					hudLayoutCustom = true
				end
				if data.keybinds then
					for k, v in pairs(data.keybinds) do keybinds[k] = v end
				end
		end
	end)
end

loadConfig()

pcall(function()
	game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("ChatActive", true)
end)

	return SWING_FORCE_OPTIONS[SWING_FORCE_INDEX] or 1.0
end

local trackerFrames = {}

local function getOrCreateTracker(userId, displayName, username)
	if trackerFrames[userId] then return trackerFrames[userId] end

	local outlineCol = THEME_OPTIONS[themeIndex].accent

	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Name = "Tracker_" .. userId
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 44)
	frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
	frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	frame.Visible = false
	frame.Parent = trackerGui

	local border = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
	border.Color = outlineCol
	border.Thickness = 1

	local corner = Instance.new("UICorner", frame)
	corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

	local avatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel", frame)
	avatar.Name = "Avatar"
	avatar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28)
	avatar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, 0)
	avatar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	avatar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28)
	avatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Instance.new("UICorner", avatar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	local avatarUserId = userId
	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(function()
			avatar.Image = Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(avatarUserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48)
		end)
	end)

	local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
	nameLabel.Name = "NameLabel"
	nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 12)
	nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 37, 0, 4)
	nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	nameLabel.Text = displayName
	nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
	nameLabel.TextSize = 9
	nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	nameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

	local distLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
	distLabel.Name = "DistLabel"
	distLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 10)
	distLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 37, 0, 16)
	distLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	distLabel.Text = "0 studs"
	distLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 140)
	distLabel.TextSize = 8
	distLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	distLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

	local hpBg = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
	hpBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 5)
	hpBg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 37, 0, 28)
	hpBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
	hpBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Instance.new("UICorner", hpBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)

	local hpFill = Instance.new("Frame", hpBg)
	hpFill.Name = "HpFill"
	hpFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 200, 50)
	hpFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Instance.new("UICorner", hpFill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)

	local hpText = Instance.new("TextLabel", hpBg)
	hpText.Name = "HpText"
	hpText.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	hpText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	hpText.Text = "100/100"
	hpText.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	hpText.TextSize = 6
	hpText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	hpText.ZIndex = 2

	local tpBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", frame)
	tpBtn.Name = "TpBtn"
	tpBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 12)
	tpBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -27, 0, 4)
	tpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
	tpBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	tpBtn.Text = "TP"
	tpBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
	tpBtn.TextSize = 7
	tpBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	tpBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	tpBtn.ZIndex = 3
	Instance.new("UICorner", tpBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
	local tpUserId = userId
	tpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		pcall(function()
			local targetPlayer = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tpUserId)
			local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not myRoot then myRoot = hrp end
			local targetRoot = targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if targetRoot and myRoot then
				local tp = targetRoot.Position
				myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(tp.X, tp.Y + 3, tp.Z)
			end
		end)
		tpBtn.Text = "✓"
		task.delay(0.5, function() pcall(function() tpBtn.Text = "TP" end) end)
	end)

	local arrow = Instance.new("TextLabel", trackerGui)
	arrow.Name = "Arrow_" .. userId
	arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
	arrow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	arrow.Text = "▲"
	arrow.TextColor3 = outlineCol
	arrow.TextSize = 28
	arrow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	arrow.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
	arrow.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
	arrow.Visible = false
	arrow.ZIndex = 5

	trackerFrames[userId] = {
		frame = frame,
		distLabel = distLabel,
		hpFill = hpFill,
		hpText = hpText,
		arrow = arrow,
	}
	return trackerFrames[userId]
end

local function UpdateTrackerGUI()
	if not hrp or not isEquipped then
		for _, data in pairs(trackerFrames) do
			data.frame.Visible = false
			data.arrow.Visible = false
		end
		return
	end

	local viewportSize = camera.ViewportSize
	local shown = {}

	local trackTargets = {}
	if settingsTrackerEnabled then
		for userId, _ in pairs(KnownSpidermen) do trackTargets[userId] = true end
	end
	for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= player and TrackedPlayers[p.Name] then
			trackTargets[p.UserId] = true
		end
	end

	for userId, _ in pairs(trackTargets) do
		local p = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
		if not p or p == player or not p.Character then continue end
		local pRoot = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local pHum = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if not pRoot or not pHum then continue end

		local dist = (pRoot.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
		local isManualTrack = TrackedPlayers[p.Name]
		if isManualTrack then
			if dist <= 15 then continue end
		else
			if dist <= TRACKER_RANGE then continue end
		end

		shown[userId] = true
		local tracker = getOrCreateTracker(userId, p.DisplayName, p.Name)
		if not tracker then continue end

		tracker.distLabel.Text = math.floor(dist) .. " studs"
		local hpPct = math.clamp(pHum.Health / math.max(pHum.MaxHealth, 1), 0, 1)
		tracker.hpFill.Size = UDim2.new(hpPct, 0, 1, 0)
		tracker.hpText.Text = math.floor(pHum.Health) .. "/" .. math.floor(pHum.MaxHealth)
		if hpPct > 0.5 then tracker.hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 200, 50)
		elseif hpPct > 0.25 then tracker.hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 30)
		else tracker.hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 40, 40) end

		local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(pRoot.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))

		if onScreen and screenPos.X > 0 and screenPos.X < viewportSize.X and screenPos.Y > 0 and screenPos.Y < viewportSize.Y then
			tracker.frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, screenPos.X, 0, screenPos.Y - 60)
			tracker.frame.Visible = true
			tracker.arrow.Visible = false
		else
			tracker.frame.Visible = false
			tracker.arrow.Visible = true
			local dir = (pRoot.Position - hrp.Position)
			local camCF = camera.CFrame
			local flatDir = camCF:PointToObjectSpace(hrp.Position + dir)
			local angle = math.atan2(flatDir.X, -flatDir.Z)
			local edgePadding = 40
			local cx, cy = viewportSize.X / 2, viewportSize.Y / 2
			local maxX, maxY = cx - edgePadding, cy - edgePadding
			local ax = math.sin(angle)
			local ay = -math.cos(angle)
			local scale = math.min(math.abs(maxX / (math.abs(ax) + 0.001)), math.abs(maxY / (math.abs(ay) + 0.001)))
			local posX = cx + ax * scale
			local posY = cy + ay * scale
			posX = math.clamp(posX, edgePadding, viewportSize.X - edgePadding)
			posY = math.clamp(posY, edgePadding, viewportSize.Y - edgePadding)
			tracker.arrow.Position = UDim2.new(0, posX, 0, posY)
			tracker.arrow.Rotation = math.deg(angle)
		end
	end

	for userId, data in pairs(trackerFrames) do
		if not shown[userId] then
			data.frame.Visible = false
			data.arrow.Visible = false
		end
	end
end

local settingsOpen = false

local mainFrame, toggleSettingsGui
if not SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED then toggleSettingsGui = function() end end
if SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED then do
local settingsGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
settingsGui.Name = "SpideySettingsGui"
settingsGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
settingsGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
settingsGui.DisplayOrder = 10
settingsGui.Parent = player.PlayerGui

local theme = THEME_OPTIONS[themeIndex]
local tRefs = {bg = {}, card = {}, text = {}, dim = {}, accent = {}, accentStroke = {}, accentText = {}, toggles = {}}
local function applyThemeLive()
	theme = THEME_OPTIONS[themeIndex]
	for _, el in pairs(tRefs.bg) do pcall(function() el.BackgroundColor3 = theme.bg end) end
	for _, el in pairs(tRefs.card) do pcall(function() el.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card end) end
	for _, el in pairs(tRefs.text) do pcall(function() el.TextColor3 = theme.text end) end
	for _, el in pairs(tRefs.dim) do pcall(function() el.TextColor3 = theme.dim end) end
	for _, el in pairs(tRefs.accent) do pcall(function() el.BackgroundColor3 = theme.accent end) end
	for _, el in pairs(tRefs.accentStroke) do pcall(function() el.Color = theme.accent end) end
	for _, el in pairs(tRefs.accentText) do pcall(function() el.TextColor3 = theme.accent end) end
	for _, tData in pairs(tRefs.toggles) do
		pcall(function() if tData.getState() then tData.bg.BackgroundColor3 = theme.accent end end)
	end
	for _, data in pairs(trackerFrames) do
		pcall(function()
			local stroke = data.frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if stroke then stroke.Color = theme.accent end
			data.arrow.TextColor3 = theme.accent
		end)
	end
	pcall(function()
		local hud = getgenv()._spideyHUD
		if hud and hud.container then
			local cs = hud.container:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if cs then cs.Color = theme.accent; cs.Transparency = 0.75 end
			hud.container.BackgroundColor3 = theme.bg
			for _, child in pairs(hud.container:GetDescendants()) do
				if child.Name == "Icon" and child:IsA("TextLabel") then child.TextColor3 = theme.dim end
				if child.Name == "Value" and child:IsA("TextLabel") then child.TextColor3 = theme.text end
			end
			if hud.momentumFill then
				local grad = hud.momentumFill:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
				if grad then
					local r, g, b = theme.accent.R, theme.accent.G, theme.accent.B
					grad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(r * 0.8, g * 0.8, b * 0.8), theme.accent)
				end
				hud.momentumFill.BackgroundColor3 = theme.accent
			end
		end
		if hud and hud.radarFrame then
			hud.radarFrame.BackgroundColor3 = theme.bg
			local rs = hud.radarFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if rs then rs.Color = theme.accent; rs.Transparency = 0.75 end
			local sd = hud.radarFrame:FindFirstChild("SelfDot")
			if sd then sd.BackgroundColor3 = theme.accent end
		end
	end)
end

local settingsOrder = 0

local function createSection(title)
	settingsOrder = settingsOrder + 1
	local container = Instance.new("Frame", scrollFrame)
	container.Name = "Section_" .. title
	container.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 22)
	container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	container.LayoutOrder = settingsOrder
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", container)
	label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 22)
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Text = string.upper(title)
	label.TextColor3 = theme.dim
	label.TextSize = 9
	label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	local lPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", label)
	lPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
	table.insert(tRefs.dim, label)
	return container
end

local function createToggle(label, default, callback)
	settingsOrder = settingsOrder + 1
	local row = Instance.new("Frame", scrollFrame)
	row.Name = "Toggle_" .. label
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 30)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.LayoutOrder = settingsOrder
	Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

	local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
	txt.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, 0)
	txt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
	txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	txt.Text = label
	txt.TextColor3 = theme.text
	txt.TextSize = 11
	txt.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	txt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

	local toggleBg = Instance.new("Frame", row)
	toggleBg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 18)
	toggleBg.Position = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0.5, 0)
	toggleBg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	toggleBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Instance.new("UICorner", toggleBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local toggleCircle = Instance.new("Frame", toggleBg)
	toggleCircle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
	toggleCircle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	toggleCircle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	toggleCircle.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Instance.new("UICorner", toggleCircle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local isOn = default
	local function update()
		if isOn then
			TweenService:Create(toggleBg, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {BackgroundColor3 = theme.accent}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(toggleCircle, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 0)}):Play()
		else
			TweenService:Create(toggleBg, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(toggleCircle, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0)}):Play()
		end
	end
	if isOn then
		toggleBg.BackgroundColor3 = theme.accent
		toggleCircle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 0)
	else
		toggleBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
		toggleCircle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0)
	end

	table.insert(tRefs.card, row)
	table.insert(tRefs.text, txt)
	table.insert(tRefs.toggles, {bg = toggleBg, getState = function() return isOn end})

	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
	btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	btn.Text = ""
	btn.ZIndex = 3
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		isOn = not isOn
		update()
		callback(isOn)
	end)

	return function(val) isOn = val; update() end
end

local function createSelector(label, options, defaultIndex, callback)
	settingsOrder = settingsOrder + 1
	local row = Instance.new("Frame", scrollFrame)
	row.Name = "Selector_" .. label
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 30)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.LayoutOrder = settingsOrder
	Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

	local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
	txt.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 1, 0)
	txt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
	txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	txt.Text = label
	txt.TextColor3 = theme.text
	txt.TextSize = 11
	txt.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	txt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

	local leftBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
	leftBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
	leftBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0.5, 0)
	leftBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	leftBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
	leftBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	leftBtn.Text = "‹"
	leftBtn.TextColor3 = theme.accent
	leftBtn.TextSize = 16
	leftBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	Instance.new("UICorner", leftBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

	local valLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
	valLabel.Name = "Value"
	valLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 78, 1, 0)
	valLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -106, 0, 0)
	valLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	valLabel.Text = options[defaultIndex]
	valLabel.TextColor3 = theme.accent
	valLabel.TextSize = 11
	valLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

	local rightBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
	rightBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
	rightBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0.5, 0)
	rightBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	rightBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
	rightBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	rightBtn.Text = "›"
	rightBtn.TextColor3 = theme.accent
	rightBtn.TextSize = 16
	rightBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	Instance.new("UICorner", rightBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

	local idx = defaultIndex
	local function updateDisplay()
		valLabel.Text = options[idx]
	end

	rightBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		idx = (idx % #options) + 1
		updateDisplay()
		callback(idx)
	end)
	leftBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		idx = ((idx - 2) % #options) + 1
		updateDisplay()
		callback(idx)
	end)

	table.insert(tRefs.card, row)
	table.insert(tRefs.text, txt)
	table.insert(tRefs.accentText, leftBtn)
	table.insert(tRefs.accentText, rightBtn)
	table.insert(tRefs.accentText, valLabel)

	return function(newIdx) idx = newIdx; updateDisplay() end
end

local function createSlider(label, min, max, default, callback)
	settingsOrder = settingsOrder + 1
	local row = Instance.new("Frame", scrollFrame)
	row.Name = "Slider_" .. label
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 42)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.LayoutOrder = settingsOrder
	Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

	local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
	txt.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 18)
	txt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 3)
	txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	txt.Text = label
	txt.TextColor3 = theme.text
	txt.TextSize = 11
	txt.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	txt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

	local valText = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
	valText.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -12, 0, 18)
	valText.Position = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 3)
	valText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	valText.Text = tostring(math.floor(default * 100)) .. "%"
	valText.TextColor3 = theme.accent
	valText.TextSize = 11
	valText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	valText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right

	local trackBg = Instance.new("Frame", row)
	trackBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 6)
	trackBg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 28)
	trackBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 45)
	trackBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Instance.new("UICorner", trackBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local trackFill = Instance.new("Frame", trackBg)
	local pct = math.clamp((default - min) / (max - min), 0, 1)
	trackFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
	trackFill.BackgroundColor3 = theme.accent
	trackFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Instance.new("UICorner", trackFill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local knob = Instance.new("Frame", trackBg)
	knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
	knob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 0.5, 0)
	knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
	knob.ZIndex = 2
	Instance.new("UICorner", knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local dragging = false
	local sliderBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
	sliderBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
	sliderBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22)
	sliderBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	sliderBtn.Text = ""
	sliderBtn.ZIndex = 3

	sliderBtn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() dragging = true end)
	table.insert(connections, UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
	end))
	table.insert(connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if dragging then
			local mx = UIS:GetMouseLocation().X
			local ap = trackBg.AbsolutePosition.X
			local aw = trackBg.AbsoluteSize.X
			local p = math.clamp((mx - ap) / aw, 0, 1)
			local val = min + p * (max - min)
			trackFill.Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
			knob.Position = UDim2.new(p, 0, 0.5, 0)
			valText.Text = tostring(math.floor(val * 100)) .. "%"
			callback(val)
		end
	end))

	table.insert(tRefs.card, row)
	table.insert(tRefs.text, txt)
	table.insert(tRefs.accentText, valText)
	table.insert(tRefs.accent, trackFill)

	return function(val)
		local p = math.clamp((val - min) / (max - min), 0, 1)
		trackFill.Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
		knob.Position = UDim2.new(p, 0, 0.5, 0)
		valText.Text = tostring(math.floor(val * 100)) .. "%"
	end
end

createSection("Visuals")

local idleNames = {"Spiderman", "2099", "Aura", "Hero Stance", "Tired", "Waiting", "Diva", "Model", "Protagonist", "Cute", "Head", "Float"}
local idleIds = {IDLE_1, IDLE_2, IDLE_3, IDLE_4, IDLE_5, IDLE_6, IDLE_7, IDLE_8, IDLE_9, IDLE_10, IDLE_11, IDLE_12}
local function getIdleIndex()
	for i, id in ipairs(idleIds) do if id == currentIdleId then return i end end
	return 1
end

local updateIdleSelector = createSelector("Idle", idleNames, getIdleIndex(), function(idx)
	currentIdleId = idleIds[idx]
	saveConfig()
	if idlePlaying and idleTrack and animator then
		pcall(function()
			idleTrack:Stop(0.1)
			local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			anim.AnimationId = currentIdleId
			idleTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
			idleTrack.Looped = true
			idleTrack:Play(0.1)
		end)
	end
end)

local webColorNames = {}
for _, c in ipairs(WEB_COLORS) do table.insert(webColorNames, c.name) end

local updateColorSelector = createSelector("Web Color", webColorNames, currentWebColorIndex, function(idx)
	currentWebColorIndex = idx
	applyWebColor()
	saveConfig()
end)

local sonarColorNames = {}
for _, c in ipairs(SONAR_COLORS) do table.insert(sonarColorNames, c.name) end

local updateSonarSelector = createSelector("Sonar Color", sonarColorNames, currentSonarColorIndex, function(idx)
	currentSonarColorIndex = idx
	saveConfig()
end)

playerListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
playerListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)

local function refreshTrackingList()
	for _, child in pairs(playerListFrame:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Frame") then child:Destroy() end
	end
	local count = 0
	local searchTerm = searchBox.Text:lower()
	for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p == player then continue end
		if searchTerm ~= "" and not p.DisplayName:lower():find(searchTerm, 1, true) and not p.Name:lower():find(searchTerm, 1, true) then continue end
		count = count + 1
		local isTracked = TrackedPlayers[p.Name] == true

		local pRow = Instance.new("Frame", playerListFrame)
		pRow.Name = "PRow_" .. p.Name
		pRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
		pRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
		pRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
		pRow.LayoutOrder = count
		Instance.new("UICorner", pRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)

		local pAvatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel", pRow)
		pAvatar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
		pAvatar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, 0)
		pAvatar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		pAvatar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
		pAvatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Instance.new("UICorner", pAvatar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
		task.spawn(function()
			pcall(function()
				pAvatar.Image = Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(p.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48)
			end)
		end)

		local pName = Instance.new("TextLabel", pRow)
		pName.Size = UDim2.new(1, -65, 1, 0)
		pName.Position = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 0)
		pName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		pName.Text = p.DisplayName
		pName.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
		pName.TextSize = 9
		pName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		pName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		pName.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

		local viewBtnList = Instance.new("TextButton", pRow)
		viewBtnList.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 16)
		viewBtnList.Position = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0.5, 0)
		viewBtnList.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		viewBtnList.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 60, 130)
		viewBtnList.BorderSizePixel = 0
		viewBtnList.Text = "View"
		viewBtnList.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
		viewBtnList.TextSize = 7
		viewBtnList.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		viewBtnList.AutoButtonColor = false
		Instance.new("UICorner", viewBtnList).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
		local viewTargetId = p.UserId
		local viewingPlayer = false
		viewBtnList.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				if viewingPlayer then
					viewingPlayer = false
					viewBtnList.Text = "View"
					viewBtnList.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 60, 130)
					camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
					local currentHum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
					camera.CameraSubject = currentHum or humanoid
				else
					local tp = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(viewTargetId)
					if tp and tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
						viewingPlayer = true
						viewBtnList.Text = "Stop"
						viewBtnList.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 50, 50)
						camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
						camera.CameraSubject = tp.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
					end
				end
			end)
		end)

		local tpBtnList = Instance.new("TextButton", pRow)
		tpBtnList.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 16)
		tpBtnList.Position = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0.5, 0)
		tpBtnList.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		tpBtnList.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 80, 140)
		tpBtnList.BorderSizePixel = 0
		tpBtnList.Text = "TP"
		tpBtnList.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
		tpBtnList.TextSize = 8
		tpBtnList.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		tpBtnList.AutoButtonColor = false
		Instance.new("UICorner", tpBtnList).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
		local tpTargetId = p.UserId
		tpBtnList.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				local tp = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tpTargetId)
				local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not myRoot then myRoot = hrp end
				local targetRoot = tp and tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if targetRoot and myRoot then
					local pos = targetRoot.Position
					myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, pos.Y + 3, pos.Z)
				end
			end)
			tpBtnList.Text = "✓"
			task.delay(0.5, function() pcall(function() tpBtnList.Text = "TP" end) end)
		end)

		local toggleBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", pRow)
		toggleBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 16)
		toggleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -38, 0.5, 0)
		toggleBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = isTracked and Color3.fromRGB(60, 180, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
		toggleBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		toggleBtn.Text = isTracked and "ON" or "OFF"
		toggleBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
		toggleBtn.TextSize = 8
		toggleBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		toggleBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
		Instance.new("UICorner", toggleBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)

		local playerName = p.Name
		toggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if TrackedPlayers[playerName] then
				TrackedPlayers[playerName] = nil
				toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
				toggleBtn.Text = "OFF"
			else
				TrackedPlayers[playerName] = true
				toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 180, 60)
				toggleBtn.Text = "ON"
			end
			saveConfig()
		end)
	end
	local maxVisible = 4
	local listHeight = math.min(count, maxVisible) * 28
	if dropdownOpen then
		playerListFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, listHeight)
		dropdownRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 56 + listHeight)
	end
end

local function toggleDropdown()
	dropdownOpen = not dropdownOpen
	dropdownArrow.Text = dropdownOpen and "▲" or "▼"
	searchBox.Visible = dropdownOpen
	playerListFrame.Visible = dropdownOpen
	if not dropdownOpen then searchBox.Text = "" end
	refreshTrackingList()
	local count = 0
	local searchTerm = searchBox.Text:lower()
	for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= player then
			if searchTerm == "" or p.DisplayName:lower():find(searchTerm, 1, true) or p.Name:lower():find(searchTerm, 1, true) then
				count = count + 1
			end
		end
	end
	local maxVisible = 4
	local listHeight = math.min(count, maxVisible) * 28
	local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	if dropdownOpen then
		TweenService:Create(dropdownRow, tweenInfo, {Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 56 + listHeight)}):Play()
		TweenService:Create(playerListFrame, tweenInfo, {Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, listHeight)}):Play()
	else
		local tweenOut = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		TweenService:Create(playerListFrame, tweenOut, {Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 0)}):Play()
		TweenService:Create(dropdownRow, tweenOut, {Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 28)}):Play()
	end
end

dropdownBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleDropdown)

searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
	if dropdownOpen then
		refreshTrackingList()
		local count = 0
		local searchTerm = searchBox.Text:lower()
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= player then
				if searchTerm == "" or p.DisplayName:lower():find(searchTerm, 1, true) or p.Name:lower():find(searchTerm, 1, true) then
					count = count + 1
				end
			end
		end
		local listHeight = math.min(count, 4) * 28
		TweenService:Create(dropdownRow, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 56 + listHeight)}):Play()
		TweenService:Create(playerListFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, listHeight)}):Play()
	end
end)

refreshTrackingList()

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() task.delay(1, function() pcall(refreshTrackingList) end) end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function() task.delay(0.5, function() pcall(refreshTrackingList) end) end)

settingsOrder = settingsOrder + 1
local resetLayoutRow = Instance.new("Frame", scrollFrame)
resetLayoutRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 30)
resetLayoutRow.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card
resetLayoutRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
resetLayoutRow.LayoutOrder = settingsOrder
Instance.new("UICorner", resetLayoutRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
table.insert(tRefs.card, resetLayoutRow)
local resetLayoutLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", resetLayoutRow)
resetLayoutLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
resetLayoutLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
resetLayoutLabel.Text = "Reset Layout"
resetLayoutLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
resetLayoutLabel.TextSize = 11
resetLayoutLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
local resetLayoutBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", resetLayoutRow)
resetLayoutBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
resetLayoutBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
resetLayoutBtn.Text = ""
resetLayoutBtn.ZIndex = 3
resetLayoutBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	hudLayout = {}
	hudLayoutCustom = false
	saveConfig()
	pcall(function()
		local hud = getgenv()._spideyHUD
		if not hud or not hud.container then return end
		local cells = {
			{src = hud.speedCell, order = 1},
			{src = hud.heightCell, order = 3},
			{src = hud.momCell, order = 5},
			{src = hud.swingCell, order = 7},
		}
		local anyOn = hudSpeedometer or hudHeightMeter or hudMomentumBar or hudSwingCounter
		hud.container.Visible = anyOn
		hud.container.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		hud.container.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 58)
		local toggleMap = {speedCell = hudSpeedometer, heightCell = hudHeightMeter, momCell = hudMomentumBar, swingCell = hudSwingCounter}
		for _, c in ipairs(cells) do
			if c.src then
				c.src.Visible = toggleMap[c.src.Name] ~= false
				c.src.Parent = hud.container
				c.src.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
				c.src.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
				c.src.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				c.src.LayoutOrder = c.order
				if c.src.Name == "MomentumCell" then
					c.src.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 1, 0)
					c.src.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
				else
					c.src.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
					c.src.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
				end
				for _, child in pairs(c.src:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("UIStroke") then child:Destroy() end
					if child:IsA("UICorner") and child.Name ~= "UICorner" then child:Destroy() end
				end
			end
		end
		for _, child in pairs(hud.container:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name:sub(1, 3) == "Div" then child.Visible = true end
		end
		if hud.radarFrame then
			hud.radarFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 1, -115)
			hud.radarFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 100)
			hud.radarFrame.Visible = radarEnabled
		end
		if getgenv()._hudPrevState then getgenv()._hudPrevState = {} end
	end)
	resetLayoutLabel.Text = "Reset!"
	task.delay(1, function() resetLayoutLabel.Text = "Reset Layout" end)
end)

local function createStatRow(name, order)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame", statsFrame)
	row.Name = name
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
	row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	row.LayoutOrder = order
	local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
	lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
	lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	lbl.Text = name
	lbl.TextColor3 = theme.dim
	lbl.TextSize = 10
	lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	table.insert(tRefs.dim, lbl)
	local val = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
	val.Name = "Val"
	val.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 1, 0)
	val.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
	val.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	val.Text = "0"
	val.TextColor3 = theme.text
	val.TextSize = 10
	val.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	val.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
	table.insert(tRefs.text, val)
	return val
end

local statTopSpeed = createStatRow("Top Speed", 1)
local statTotalSwings = createStatRow("Total Swings", 2)
local statDistance = createStatRow("Distance", 3)
local statSession = createStatRow("Session Time", 4)

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1) and getgenv().SpideyRunId == runId do
		pcall(function()
			local elapsed = tick() - sessionStart
			local mins = math.floor(elapsed / 60)
			local secs = math.floor(elapsed % 60)
			statTopSpeed.Text = math.floor(topSpeed) .. " studs/s"
			statTotalSwings.Text = tostring(totalSwings)
			statDistance.Text = math.floor(totalDistance) .. " studs"
			statSession.Text = mins .. "m " .. secs .. "s"
			pcall(function()
				if profileStatLine then
					profileStatLine.Text = "Speed: " .. math.floor(topSpeed) .. " | Swings: " .. totalSwings
				end
			end)
		end)
	end
end)

createSection("Keybinds")

local remappable = {
	{label = "Cmd Bar", key = "cmdBar"},
	{label = "Hang", key = "hang"},
	{label = "Perch", key = "perch"},
	{label = "Photo Mode", key = "photoMode"},
	{label = "Sonar", key = "sonar"},
	{label = "Glide", key = "glide"},
	{label = "Dash", key = "dash"},
	{label = "Zip", key = "zip"},
	{label = "Slingshot", key = "slingshot"},
	{label = "Lunge", key = "lunge"},
	{label = "Menu", key = "menu"},
	{label = "Force Reset", key = "forceReset"},
}

for _, kb in ipairs(remappable) do
	settingsOrder = settingsOrder + 1
	local kbRow = Instance.new("Frame", scrollFrame)
	kbRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 28)
	kbRow.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card
	kbRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	kbRow.LayoutOrder = settingsOrder
	Instance.new("UICorner", kbRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	table.insert(tRefs.card, kbRow)

	local kbLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", kbRow)
	kbLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
	kbLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
	kbLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	kbLabel.Text = kb.label
	kbLabel.TextColor3 = theme.text
	kbLabel.TextSize = 10
	kbLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	kbLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	table.insert(tRefs.text, kbLabel)

	local kbBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", kbRow)
	kbBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 18)
	kbBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0.5, 0)
	kbBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	kbBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 45)
	kbBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	kbBtn.TextSize = 10
	kbBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	kbBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	Instance.new("UICorner", kbBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
	local currentKey = keybinds[kb.key] or ""
	local displayKey = currentKey == "Semicolon" and ";" or (currentKey == "" and "None" or currentKey)
	kbBtn.Text = displayKey
	kbBtn.TextColor3 = currentKey == "" and Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80) or theme.accent
	table.insert(tRefs.accentText, kbBtn)

	local kbClearBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", kbRow)
	kbClearBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
	kbClearBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0.5, 0)
	kbClearBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	kbClearBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 30, 30)
	kbClearBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	kbClearBtn.Text = "x"
	kbClearBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 80, 80)
	kbClearBtn.TextSize = 10
	kbClearBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	kbClearBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	kbClearBtn.ZIndex = 3
	Instance.new("UICorner", kbClearBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

	local kbKey = kb.key

	kbClearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		keybinds[kbKey] = ""
		kbBtn.Text = "None"
		kbBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
		saveConfig()
	end)
	local listening = false
	kbBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if listening then return end
		listening = true
		kbBtn.Text = "..."
		kbBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50)
		local conn
		conn = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
			if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
			conn:Disconnect()
			listening = false
			local keyName = input.KeyCode.Name
			if #keyName == 1 or keyName == "Semicolon" then
				keybinds[kbKey] = keyName
				kbBtn.Text = keyName == "Semicolon" and ";" or keyName
				kbBtn.TextColor3 = theme.accent
				saveConfig()
			else
				kbBtn.Text = displayKey
				kbBtn.TextColor3 = theme.accent
			end
		end)
		task.delay(3, function()
			if listening then
				listening = false
				pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
				kbBtn.Text = keybinds[kbKey] == "Semicolon" and ";" or keybinds[kbKey]
				kbBtn.TextColor3 = theme.accent
			end
		end)
	end)
end

local settingsClosedPos = UDim2.new(1, 300, 0.5, 0)
local settingsOpenPos = UDim2.new(1, -155, 0.5, 0)

toggleSettingsGui = function()
	if settingsAnimating then return end
	settingsAnimating = true
	settingsOpen = not settingsOpen
	if settingsOpen then
		mainFrame.Position = settingsClosedPos
		mainFrame.Visible = true
		TweenService:Create(mainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Position = settingsOpenPos}):Play()
		task.delay(0.3, function() settingsAnimating = false end)
	else
		TweenService:Create(mainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Position = settingsClosedPos}):Play()
		task.delay(0.35, function() mainFrame.Visible = false; settingsAnimating = false end)
	end
end
end end -- end do + if SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED

if not toggleSettingsGui then
	toggleSettingsGui = function() end
end

if SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED then pcall(function()
	local hudGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	hudGui.Name = "SpideyHUD"
	hudGui.DisplayOrder = 8
	hudGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	hudGui.Parent = player.PlayerGui

	local hudContainer = Instance.new("Frame", hudGui)
	hudContainer.Name = "HUDContainer"
	hudContainer.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
	hudContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 58)
	hudContainer.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 36)
	hudContainer.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
	hudContainer.BackgroundColor3 = THEME_OPTIONS[themeIndex].bg
	hudContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
	hudContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Instance.new("UICorner", hudContainer).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
