function getIgnoreParams()
	local ignoreList = {char}
	for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p.Character then table.insert(ignoreList, p.Character) end
	end
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreList
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	return params
end

function playSound(id, volume, pitch, startTime, customParent)
	local p = customParent or hrp
	if not p or not id then return end
	local s = Instance.new("Sound")
	s.SoundId = id
	s.Volume = (volume or 1) * 0.4
	local basePitch = pitch or 1
	s:SetAttribute("BasePitch", basePitch)
	local speedScale = isCinematic and 0.3 or 1.0
	s.PlaybackSpeed = basePitch * speedScale
	if id == SWING_SOUND_ID then
		s.RollOffMaxDistance = 100
		s.RollOffMinDistance = 10
		s.RollOffMode = Enum.RollOffMode.Linear
	end
	if startTime then s.TimePosition = startTime end
	s.Parent = p
	s:Play()
	s.Ended:Connect(function() s:Destroy() end)
	return s
end

function emitWebBurst(handStr, targetChar)
	targetChar = targetChar or char
	local handAtt = nil
	if targetChar == char then
		handAtt = (handStr == "Right") and handAttachmentR or handAttachmentL
	else
		local handPart = targetChar:FindFirstChild(handStr .. "Hand") or targetChar:FindFirstChild(handStr .. " Arm")
		if handPart then
			handAtt = handPart:FindFirstChild("PeerWebAtt")
			if not handAtt then
				handAtt = Instance.new("Attachment", handPart)
				handAtt.Name = "PeerWebAtt"
				handAtt.Position = Vector3.new(0, -0.1, 0)
			end
		end
	end
	if not handAtt then return end
	local burst = handAtt:FindFirstChild("WebBurstParticle")
	if burst then
		burst:Emit(20)
		burst.Enabled = true
		task.delay(0.05, function() burst.Enabled = false end)
	end
end

function playInstantWebSound(handStr, customPitch)
	if #webSoundsPool > 0 then
		local s = webSoundsPool[currentWebSoundIdx]
		local bp = customPitch or (math.random(95, 105) / 100)
		s:SetAttribute("BasePitch", bp)
		local speedScale = isCinematic and 0.3 or 1.0
		s.PlaybackSpeed = bp * speedScale
		s.TimePosition = 0.2
		s:Play()
		currentWebSoundIdx = (currentWebSoundIdx % #webSoundsPool) + 1
	end
	emitWebBurst(handStr or activeWebHand)
end

function spawnImpactDust(pos, normal)
	local dustPart = Instance.new("Part")
	dustPart.Anchored = true
	dustPart.CanCollide = false
	dustPart.Transparency = 1
	dustPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + normal)
	dustPart.Parent = workspace
	local att = Instance.new("Attachment", dustPart)
	local dust = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	dust.Texture = "rbxassetid://283437152"
	dust.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
	dust.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 5)})
	dust.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.5), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
	dust.Speed = NumberRange.new(15, 25)
	dust.Drag = 4
	dust.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.6, 1.2)
	dust.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(60, 60)
	dust.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Front
	dust.Rate = 0
	dust.Enabled = false
	dust.Parent = att
	dust:Emit(25)
	dust.Enabled = true
	task.delay(0.1, function() dust.Enabled = false end)
	Debris:AddItem(dustPart, 2)
end

function getGroundColor(rootPart)
	local rp = RaycastParams.new()
	rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {rootPart.Parent}
	rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	local ray = workspace:Raycast(rootPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, -10, 0), rp)
	if ray and ray.Instance then
		return ray.Instance.Color
	end
	return Color3.fromRGB(170, 160, 140)
end

function createChargeVFX(rootPart)
	for _, c in pairs(rootPart:GetChildren()) do if c.Name == "SpideyChargeAtt" then c:Destroy() end end
	local groundCol = getGroundColor(rootPart)
	local att = Instance.new("Attachment", rootPart)
	att.Name = "SpideyChargeAtt"
	att.Position = Vector3.new(0, -3, 0)

	local dust = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", att)
	dust.Name = "Dust"
	dust.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
	dust.Color = ColorSequence.new(groundCol)
	dust.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.8), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 5)})
	dust.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 0.6), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
	dust.Speed = NumberRange.new(6, 15)
	dust.Drag = 4
	dust.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 1.0)
	dust.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(80, 80)
	dust.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
	dust.Rate = 25
	dust.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-100, 100)
	dust.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)

	local pebbles = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", att)
	pebbles.Name = "Pebbles"
	pebbles.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
	local darkCol = Color3.new(groundCol.R * 0.5, groundCol.G * 0.5, groundCol.B * 0.5)
	pebbles.Color = ColorSequence.new(darkCol)
	pebbles.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.4), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.15)})
	pebbles.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, 0.2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
	pebbles.Speed = NumberRange.new(10, 25)
	pebbles.Drag = 2
	pebbles.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.7)
	pebbles.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(60, 60)
	pebbles.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
	pebbles.Rate = 12
	pebbles.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, -50, 0)

	local glow = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", att)
	glow.Name = "Glow"
	glow.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/fire_main.dds"
	glow.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 60), Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 20))
	glow.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 1.8), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})
	glow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
	glow.Speed = NumberRange.new(3, 10)
	glow.Drag = 5
	glow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.2, 0.5)
	glow.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(360, 40)
	glow.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
	glow.Rate = 15
	glow.LightEmission = 1
	glow.LightInfluence = 0

	return att
end

function updateChargeVFX(att, powerPct)
	if not att or not att.Parent then return end
	for _, emitter in pairs(att:GetChildren()) do
		if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			if emitter.Name == "Dust" then
				emitter.Rate = 15 + (powerPct * 80)
				emitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(6 + powerPct * 15, 15 + powerPct * 30)
			elseif emitter.Name == "Pebbles" then
				emitter.Rate = 8 + (powerPct * 40)
			elseif emitter.Name == "Glow" then
				emitter.Rate = 5 + (powerPct * 55)
			end
		end
	end
end

function destroyChargeVFX(att)
	if not att or not att.Parent then return end
	for _, emitter in pairs(att:GetChildren()) do
		if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then emitter.Rate = 0; emitter.Enabled = false end
	end
	Debris:AddItem(att, 1.5)
end

function spawnLungeBurst(rootPart)
	local gCol = getGroundColor(rootPart)
	local att = Instance.new("Attachment", rootPart)
	att.Name = "SpideyLungeBurst"
	att.Position = Vector3.new(0, -3, 0)
	local burst = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", att)
	burst.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
	burst.Color = ColorSequence.new(gCol)
	burst.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1.5), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 5), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 8)})
	burst.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.5), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
	burst.Speed = NumberRange.new(15, 35)
	burst.Drag = 5
	burst.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8)
	burst.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(70, 70)
	burst.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
	burst.Rate = 0
	burst.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-80, 80)
	burst.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
	burst:Emit(math.floor(35 * (vfxIntensity or 1)))
	local sparks = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", att)
	sparks.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
	sparks.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 80))
	sparks.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.5), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})
	sparks.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
	sparks.Speed = NumberRange.new(20, 45)
	sparks.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.6)
	sparks.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(80, 80)
	sparks.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
	sparks.Rate = 0
	sparks.LightEmission = 1
	sparks.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, -40, 0)
	sparks:Emit(math.floor(20 * (vfxIntensity or 1)))
	Debris:AddItem(att, 2)
end
function spawnSonicBoom(pos, dir)
	local shockwave = Instance.new("Part")
	shockwave.Name = "SonicBoomRing"
	shockwave.Anchored = true
	shockwave.CanCollide = false
	shockwave.CastShadow = false
	shockwave.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
	shockwave.Color = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	shockwave.Transparency = 0.2
	shockwave.Size = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
	local spawnPos = pos - (dir * 5)
	shockwave.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(spawnPos, spawnPos + dir) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
	shockwave.Parent = workspace
	local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
	mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
	mesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://3270017"
	mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(10,10,2)
	mesh.Parent = shockwave
	local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Exponential, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	TweenService:Create(shockwave, tweenInfo, {Transparency = 1}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(mesh, tweenInfo, {Scale = Vector3.new(80,80,5)}):Play()
	Debris:AddItem(shockwave, 0.4)
end

function buildWallFrame(pos, normal, moveDir)
	local up = normal.Unit
	local look = moveDir - up * moveDir:Dot(up)
	if look.Magnitude < 0.01 then
		local fallback = Vector3.new(0,1,0) - up * up.Y
		if fallback.Magnitude < 0.01 then fallback = Vector3.new(0,0,1) - up * up.Z end
		if fallback.Magnitude < 0.01 then fallback = Vector3.new(1,0,0) - up * up.X end
		look = fallback
	end
	look = look.Unit
	local right = look:Cross(up)
	if right.Magnitude < 0.01 then right = Vector3.new(1,0,0) end
	right = right.Unit
	local newLook = up:Cross(right).Unit
	return CFrame.fromMatrix(pos, right, up, -newLook)
end

function ensureCrawlTrack()
	if not crawlTrack or not crawlTrack.IsPlaying then
		local a = Instance.new("Animation")
		a.AnimationId = CRAWL_ANIM_ID
		crawlTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(a)
		crawlTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
		crawlTrack.Looped = true
		crawlTrack:Play(0.2)
	end
end

function stopCrawlTrack()
	if crawlTrack then crawlTrack:Stop(0.2) end
end

function setCrawlSpeed(speed)
	if crawlTrack then crawlTrack:AdjustSpeed(speed > 0.1 and 1 or 0) end
end

OtherPlayersData = {}

function getPeerWebColor(character)
	local col = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	pcall(function()
		for _, handName in pairs({"RightHand", "Right Arm", "LeftHand", "Left Arm"}) do
			local hand = character:FindFirstChild(handName)
			if hand then
				for _, child in pairs(hand:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Trail") then
						local cs = child.Color
						if cs then
							local c = cs.Keypoints[1].Value
							if c ~= Color3.new(1,1,1) then col = c; return end
						end
					end
				end
				for _, child in pairs(hand:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Beam") and child.Name ~= "WebBurst" then
						local cs = child.Color
						if cs then
							local c = cs.Keypoints[1].Value
							if c ~= Color3.new(1,1,1) then col = c; return end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for _, desc in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
			if desc:IsA("Trail") then
				local cs = desc.Color
				if cs then
					local c = cs.Keypoints[1].Value
					if c ~= Color3.new(1,1,1) then col = c; return end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	return col
end

function CreatePeerBeam(character, targetPos, isStraight, handName)
	local handStr = handName or "RightHand"
	local hand = character:FindFirstChild(handStr) or character:FindFirstChild(string.gsub(handStr, "Hand", " Arm"))
	if not hand and not handName then hand = character:FindFirstChild("LeftHand") or character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") end
	if not hand then return nil end
	local attHand = Instance.new("Attachment", hand)
	local attWorld = Instance.new("Attachment", workspace.Terrain)
	attWorld.WorldPosition = targetPos
	local peerColor = getPeerWebColor(character)
	local beam = Instance.new("Beam", workspace.Terrain)
	beam.Texture = WEB_BEAM_TEXTURE
	beam.Width0 = 0.08
	beam.Width1 = 0.08
	beam.Attachment0 = attHand
	beam.Attachment1 = attWorld
	beam.FaceCamera = true
	beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(peerColor)
	beam.LightEmission = (peerColor == Color3.new(1,1,1)) and 0 or 0.4
	beam.Segments = 20
	if isStraight then beam.CurveSize0 = 0; beam.CurveSize1 = 0 end
	return {beam = beam, worldAtt = attWorld, handAtt = attHand}
end

function UpdatePeers(dt)
	for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= player and p.Character then
			local pChar = p.Character
			local pHum = pChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			local pRoot = pChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local pAnimator = pHum and pHum:FindFirstChild("Animator")
			if pAnimator and pRoot then
				local isSwinging, isZipping, isWallCrawling, isPerching = false, false, false, false
				local isCharging, isHanging, isWebShooting = false, false, false
				local isWebShootingRight, isWebShootingLeft = false, false
				local isVaultingPeer, isDoubleWebbing = false, false
				local hasExecuted = false
				local tracks = pAnimator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
				for _, track in pairs(tracks) do
					local id = track.Animation.AnimationId
					if id == SWING_ANIM_ID or id == SWING_ANIM_ID_2 then isSwinging = true; hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == ZIP_ANIM_ID then isZipping = true; hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == CRAWL_ANIM_ID or id == WALL_RIGHT_ID or id == WALL_LEFT_ID or id == WALL_RUN_RIGHT_ID then isWallCrawling = true; hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == SLINGSHOT_PERCH_ID then isPerching = true; hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == CHARGE_JUMP_ID then isCharging = true; hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == WEB_SHOOT_RIGHT_ID then isWebShootingRight = true; hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == WEB_SHOOT_LEFT_ID then isWebShootingLeft = true; hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == N_ANIM_ID or id == N_ANIM_ID_3 then isHanging = true; hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == N_ANIM_ID_2 then hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == VAULT_ANIM_ID or id == MANTLE_ANIM_ID then isVaultingPeer = true; hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == X_ANIM_ID then hasExecuted = true
					elseif id == FLAG_DOUBLE_DASH then isDoubleWebbing = true; hasExecuted = true
					end
				end
				isWebShooting = isDoubleWebbing and false or (isWebShootingRight or isWebShootingLeft)
				local hasSpideyIdle = false
				for _, track in pairs(tracks) do
					local id = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId
					if id == IDLE_1 or id == IDLE_2 or id == IDLE_3 or id == IDLE_4 or id == IDLE_5 or id == IDLE_6 or id == IDLE_7 or id == IDLE_8 or id == IDLE_9 or id == IDLE_10 or id == IDLE_11 or id == IDLE_12 then
						hasSpideyIdle = true
						break
					end
				end
				if hasExecuted then KnownSpidermen[p.UserId] = true end
				if not hasExecuted then
					if OtherPlayersData[p.UserId] then
						local d = OtherPlayersData[p.UserId]
						if d.fx then if d.fx.beam then d.fx.beam:Destroy() end; if d.fx.worldAtt then d.fx.worldAtt:Destroy() end end
						if d.fxDoubleR then if d.fxDoubleR.beam then d.fxDoubleR.beam:Destroy() end; if d.fxDoubleR.worldAtt then d.fxDoubleR.worldAtt:Destroy() end end
						if d.fxDoubleL then if d.fxDoubleL.beam then d.fxDoubleL.beam:Destroy() end; if d.fxDoubleL.worldAtt then d.fxDoubleL.worldAtt:Destroy() end end
						if d.fxHang then if d.fxHang.beam then d.fxHang.beam:Destroy() end; if d.fxHang.worldAtt then d.fxHang.worldAtt:Destroy() end end
						OtherPlayersData[p.UserId] = nil
					end
					continue
				end
				if not OtherPlayersData[p.UserId] then
					OtherPlayersData[p.UserId] = {
						swingActive = false, zipActive = false, wallActive = false, perchActive = false, hangActive = false, chargeActive = false, webShootActive = false, doubleWebActive = false, vaultActive = false,
						lastPos = pRoot.Position, fx = nil, fxDoubleR = nil, fxDoubleL = nil, wasSwinging = false, wasZipping = false, lastActionEnd = 0
					}
				end
				local data = OtherPlayersData[p.UserId]
				if (data.wasSwinging and not isSwinging) or (data.wasZipping and not isZipping) then data.lastActionEnd = tick() end
				local timeStep = math.max(dt, 0.001)
				local velocity = (pRoot.Position - data.lastPos).Magnitude / timeStep
				if velocity > 200 and not isSwinging and not isZipping and not isDoubleWebbing and (tick() - (data.lastActionEnd or 0) > 1.0) then
					if not data.dashCooldown or (tick() - data.dashCooldown > 1.5) then
						spawnSonicBoom(pRoot.Position, pRoot.CFrame.LookVector)
						playSound(DASH_SOUND_ID, 2.5, 1, 0, pRoot)
						data.dashCooldown = tick()
					end
				end
				data.lastPos = pRoot.Position
				if isVaultingPeer then
					if not data.vaultActive then
						data.vaultActive = true
						playSound(WALL_LUNGE_SOUND_ID, 1.0, 1, 0, pRoot)
						emitWebBurst("Right", pChar)
						emitWebBurst("Left", pChar)
					end
				else
					data.vaultActive = false
				end
				if isWebShooting then
					if not data.webShootActive then
						data.webShootActive = true
						local handStr = isWebShootingRight and "Right" or "Left"
						emitWebBurst(handStr, pChar)
						playSound(SWING_SOUND_ID, 1.0, 1.2, 0.2, pRoot)
					end
				else
					data.webShootActive = false
				end
				if isZipping then
					if not data.zipActive then
						data.zipActive = true
						playSound(ZIP_SOUND_ID, 1.2, 1.1, 0, pRoot)
						emitWebBurst("Right", pChar)
						emitWebBurst("Left", pChar)
					end
				else
					data.zipActive = false
				end
				if isDoubleWebbing then
					if not data.doubleWebActive then
						data.doubleWebActive = true
						playSound(DASH_SOUND_ID, 1.5, 1.2, 0, pRoot)
						spawnSonicBoom(pRoot.Position, pRoot.CFrame.LookVector)
						local targetR = pRoot.Position + pRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 150 + pRoot.CFrame.RightVector * 40
						local targetL = pRoot.Position + pRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 150 - pRoot.CFrame.RightVector * 40
						if data.fxDoubleR then if data.fxDoubleR.beam then data.fxDoubleR.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fxDoubleR.worldAtt then data.fxDoubleR.worldAtt:Destroy() end end
						if data.fxDoubleL then if data.fxDoubleL.beam then data.fxDoubleL.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fxDoubleL.worldAtt then data.fxDoubleL.worldAtt:Destroy() end end
						data.fxDoubleR = CreatePeerBeam(pChar, targetR, true, "RightHand")
						data.fxDoubleL = CreatePeerBeam(pChar, targetL, true, "LeftHand")
					end
				else
					if data.doubleWebActive then
						data.doubleWebActive = false
						if data.fxDoubleR then if data.fxDoubleR.beam then data.fxDoubleR.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fxDoubleR.worldAtt then data.fxDoubleR.worldAtt:Destroy() end; data.fxDoubleR = nil end
						if data.fxDoubleL then if data.fxDoubleL.beam then data.fxDoubleL.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fxDoubleL.worldAtt then data.fxDoubleL.worldAtt:Destroy() end; data.fxDoubleL = nil end
					end
				end
				if isSwinging and not isDoubleWebbing then
					local now = tick()
					if not data.swingActive then
						data.swingActive = true
						data.lastSwingWebTime = 0
					end
					if now - (data.lastSwingWebTime or 0) >= 1.0 then
						data.lastSwingWebTime = now
						local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
						rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {pChar}
						local aimDir = (pRoot.CFrame.LookVector + Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0)).Unit
						local ray = workspace:Spherecast(pRoot.Position, 3, aimDir * MAX_SWING_DIST, rayParams)
						if ray and ray.Normal.Y >= 0.7 then
							ray = workspace:Spherecast(pRoot.Position + Vector3.new(0,5,0), 3, (aimDir + Vector3.new(0,0.4,0)).Unit * MAX_SWING_DIST, rayParams)
						end
						local target = ray and ray.Position or (pRoot.Position + aimDir * 60 + Vector3.new(0,25,0))
						if data.fx then if data.fx.beam then data.fx.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fx.worldAtt then data.fx.worldAtt:Destroy() end end
						data.fx = CreatePeerBeam(pChar, target, false)
						playSound(SWING_SOUND_ID, 1, 1, 0.2, pRoot)
					end
					if data.fx and data.fx.beam then
						local peerCol = getPeerWebColor(pChar)
						data.fx.beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(peerCol)
						data.fx.beam.LightEmission = (peerCol == Color3.new(1,1,1)) and 0 or 0.4
						local dist = (data.fx.handAtt.WorldPosition - data.fx.worldAtt.WorldPosition).Magnitude
						local sag = math.clamp(dist * 0.12, 1, 15)
						data.fx.beam.CurveSize0 = -sag
						data.fx.beam.CurveSize1 = sag * 0.5
					end
				else
					if data.swingActive then
						data.swingActive = false
						data.lastSwingWebTime = 0
						if data.fx then if data.fx.beam then data.fx.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fx.worldAtt then data.fx.worldAtt:Destroy() end; data.fx = nil end
					end
				end
				if isHanging then
					if not data.hangActive then
						data.hangActive = true
						local rayP2 = RaycastParams.new()
						rayP2.FilterDescendantsInstances = {pChar}
						rayP2.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
						local rayUp2 = workspace:Raycast(pRoot.Position, Vector3.new(0, HANG_MAX_DIST + 10, 0), rayP2)
						local ceilPos = rayUp2 and rayUp2.Position or (pRoot.Position + Vector3.new(0, HANG_MAX_DIST, 0))
						if data.fxHang then if data.fxHang.beam then data.fxHang.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fxHang.worldAtt then data.fxHang.worldAtt:Destroy() end end
						data.fxHang = CreatePeerBeam(pChar, ceilPos, true)
					end
					if data.fxHang and data.fxHang.worldAtt then
						local rayP3 = RaycastParams.new()
						rayP3.FilterDescendantsInstances = {pChar}
						rayP3.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
						local rayUp3 = workspace:Raycast(pRoot.Position, Vector3.new(0, HANG_MAX_DIST + 10, 0), rayP3)
						data.fxHang.worldAtt.WorldPosition = rayUp3 and rayUp3.Position or (pRoot.Position + Vector3.new(0, HANG_MAX_DIST, 0))
						local peerCol = getPeerWebColor(pChar)
						data.fxHang.beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(peerCol)
					end
				else
					if data.hangActive then
						data.hangActive = false
						if data.fxHang then if data.fxHang.beam then data.fxHang.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fxHang.worldAtt then data.fxHang.worldAtt:Destroy() end; data.fxHang = nil end
					end
				end
				data.wasSwinging = isSwinging
				data.wasZipping = isZipping
			end
		end
	end
end

function ResetSonar()
	pcall(function()
		sonarActive = false
		if ccSonar then ccSonar.TintColor = Color3.new(1,1,1) end
		for _, v in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if v.Character then
				pcall(function()
					for _, c in pairs(v.Character:GetDescendants()) do
						if c.Name == "SonarHL" or c.Name == "SonarTag" then c:Destroy() end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end
getgenv()._resetSonar = ResetSonar

function ActivateSonar()
	if sonarActive then
		ResetSonar()
		return
	end
	sonarActive = true
	playSound(SONAR_PING_ID, 2, 1)
	if not ccSonar then
		ccSonar = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
		ccSonar.Name = "SpideySonarTint"
		ccSonar.Parent = Lighting
	end
	ccSonar.TintColor = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	local targetTint = SONAR_COLORS[currentSonarColorIndex].tint
	local fadeInInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	TweenService:Create(ccSonar, fadeInInfo, {TintColor = targetTint}):Play()
	ResetSonar()
	sonarActive = true
	local highlights = {}
	for _, v in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if v ~= player and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			pcall(function()
				local isSpider = KnownSpidermen[v.UserId]
				local color = isSpider and Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0) or SONAR_COLORS[currentSonarColorIndex].highlight
				local h = Instance.new("Highlight", v.Character)
				h.Name = "SonarHL"
				h.FillColor = color
				h.OutlineColor = Color3.new(1,1,1)
				h.FillTransparency = 1
				h.OutlineTransparency = 1
				TweenService:Create(h, fadeInInfo, {FillTransparency = 0.5, OutlineTransparency = 0}):Play()
				local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui", v.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
				bb.Name = "SonarTag"
				bb.Size = UDim2.new(0,200,0,50)
				bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,3,0)
				bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
				local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
				txt.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
				txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				txt.Text = v.DisplayName .. "\n(@" .. v.Name .. ")"
				txt.TextColor3 = color
				txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
				txt.TextSize = 14
				txt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				TweenService:Create(txt, fadeInInfo, {TextTransparency = 0, TextStrokeTransparency = 0}):Play()
				table.insert(highlights, {h, bb, txt})
			end)
		end
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(2.0)
		pcall(function()
			local fadeOutInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			if ccSonar then TweenService:Create(ccSonar, fadeOutInfo, {TintColor = Color3.new(1,1,1)}):Play() end
			for _, obj in pairs(highlights) do
				pcall(function()
					if obj[1] then TweenService:Create(obj[1], fadeOutInfo, {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}):Play() end
					if obj[3] then TweenService:Create(obj[3], fadeOutInfo, {TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1}):Play() end
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.6)
			for _, obj in pairs(highlights) do
				pcall(function()
					if obj[1] then obj[1]:Destroy() end
					if obj[2] then obj[2]:Destroy() end
				end)
			end
		end)
		sonarActive = false
	end)
	task.delay(5, function()
		if sonarActive then ResetSonar() end
	end)
end

function StopAllAnimations()
	local tracks = {idleTrack, swingTrack, wallTrack, wallLeftTrack, wallRunTrack, wallRunLeftTrack, fallTrack, startFallTrack, slingshotTrack, glideTrack, chargeTrack, xTrack, emoteTrack, zipTrack, crawlTrack, trickTrack, rollTrack, vaultTrack, mantleTrack, runTrack, backDashTrack, stepFallTrack, swingReleaseTrack}
	for _, track in pairs(tracks) do if track then track:Stop(0.1) end end
	idlePlaying, fallingAnimPlaying, startFallPlaying, stepFallPlaying = false, false, false, false
	isEmoting, isHangingEmote, isWallRunning, isTricking, runPlaying = false, false, false, false, false
	if hangBeam then hangBeam:Destroy(); hangBeam = nil end
	if hangWorldAtt then hangWorldAtt:Destroy(); hangWorldAtt = nil end
	if hangMidAtt then hangMidAtt:Destroy(); hangMidAtt = nil end
	if hrp and hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro") then hrp.WallGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.zero end
	if isFootHanging then isFootHanging = false; if hrp then hrp.Anchored = false; hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) end; humanoid.PlatformStand = false; humanoid.AutoRotate = true; bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero end
	if isMantling then if hrp then hrp.Anchored = false end; isMantling = false end
end

function applyShake(amt) shakeIntensity = amt end

function spawnWebResidue(anchorPos, handPos, surfaceNormal)
	for i = 1, 3 do
		local anchorPart = Instance.new("Part")
		anchorPart.Name = "WebAnchorClump"
		anchorPart.Size = Vector3.new(1.3,1.3,0.05)
		anchorPart.Transparency = 1
		anchorPart.Anchored = true
		anchorPart.CanCollide = false
		anchorPart.CastShadow = false
		anchorPart.Parent = workspace
		anchorPart.Position = anchorPos + (surfaceNormal * 0.05)
		local decal = Instance.new("Decal")
		decal.Texture = WEB_ANCHOR_TEXTURE_ID
		decal.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
		decal.Parent = anchorPart
		anchorPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(anchorPart.Position, anchorPart.Position + surfaceNormal) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(math.random(0,360)))
		Debris:AddItem(anchorPart, 3)
	end
	local fallingPart = Instance.new("Part")
	fallingPart.Name = "WebEnd"
	fallingPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.1,0.1,0.1)
	fallingPart.Position = handPos
	fallingPart.Anchored = false
	fallingPart.CanCollide = false
	fallingPart.Massless = true
	fallingPart.CastShadow = false
	fallingPart.Transparency = 1
	fallingPart.Parent = workspace
	task.delay(0.2, function() if fallingPart and fallingPart.Parent then fallingPart.CanCollide = true end end)
	local att0 = Instance.new("Attachment", workspace.Terrain)
	att0.WorldPosition = anchorPos
	local att1 = Instance.new("Attachment", fallingPart)
	local rope = Instance.new("RopeConstraint")
	rope.Attachment0 = att0
	rope.Attachment1 = att1
	rope.Visible = false
	rope.Length = (anchorPos - handPos).Magnitude * 1.1
	rope.Parent = fallingPart
	local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
	beam.Attachment0 = att0
	beam.Attachment1 = att1
	beam.Texture = WEB_BEAM_TEXTURE
	beam.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Wrap
	beam.TextureLength = 12
	beam.Width0 = 0.08
	beam.Width1 = 0.02
	local wc = WEB_COLORS[currentWebColorIndex]
	beam.LightEmission = wc.emission
	beam.LightInfluence = wc.emission > 0 and 0 or 1
	beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(wc.color)
	beam.FaceCamera = true
	beam.CurveSize0 = 0.5
	beam.CurveSize1 = -0.5
	beam.Segments = 10
	beam.Parent = fallingPart
	Debris:AddItem(att0, 3)
	Debris:AddItem(fallingPart, 3)
end

function createSonicBoom(customPos)
	local pos = customPos or (hrp and hrp.Position)
	if pos then spawnSonicBoom(pos, camera.CFrame.LookVector) end
end

function createTrail(parent)
	local att0 = Instance.new("Attachment", parent)
	att0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
	local att1 = Instance.new("Attachment", parent)
	att1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -0.1, 0)
	local t = Instance.new("Trail")
	t.Parent = parent
	t.Attachment0 = att0
	t.Attachment1 = att1
	t.Lifetime = 0.3
	t.Enabled = false
	t.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.8), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
	t.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1))
	return t
end

function applyWebColor()
	local c = WEB_COLORS[currentWebColorIndex]
	local col = ColorSequence.new(c.color)
	local em = c.emission
	local beams = {webBeamR, webBeamL, webLoopBeamR, webLoopBeamL}
	for _, b in pairs(beams) do
		if b then b.Color = col; b.LightEmission = em end
	end
	if handTrailR then handTrailR.Color = col end
	if handTrailL then handTrailL.Color = col end
end

function hideAllBeams()
	if webBeamR then webBeamR.Enabled = false end
	if webBeamL then webBeamL.Enabled = false end
	if webLoopBeamR then webLoopBeamR.Enabled = false end
	if webLoopBeamL then webLoopBeamL.Enabled = false end
end

function launchWebCoil(mainBeam, loopBeam, midAtt, handAtt, worldPos)
	if webLaunchConnections[mainBeam] then
		webLaunchConnections[mainBeam]:Disconnect()
		webLaunchConnections[mainBeam] = nil
	end
	mainBeam.Enabled = true
	loopBeam.Enabled = true
	midAtt.WorldPosition = handAtt.WorldPosition
	local startTime = tick()
	local COIL_DURATION = 0.32
	local MAX_COIL = 14
	local conn
	conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not mainBeam.Enabled then
			conn:Disconnect()
			webLaunchConnections[mainBeam] = nil
			return
		end
		local elapsed = tick() - startTime
		local alpha = math.min(elapsed / COIL_DURATION, 1)
		local eased = 1 - (1 - alpha)^3

		midAtt.WorldPosition = handAtt.WorldPosition:Lerp(worldPos, eased)
		local coilAmount = MAX_COIL * (1 - eased) + 2

		mainBeam.CurveSize0 = math.sin(elapsed * 60) * coilAmount
		mainBeam.CurveSize1 = math.cos(elapsed * 60) * coilAmount

		local extDist = (midAtt.WorldPosition - worldPos).Magnitude
		loopBeam.CurveSize0 = math.sin(elapsed * 60) * (extDist * 0.12)
		loopBeam.CurveSize1 = math.cos(elapsed * 60) * (extDist * 0.12)

		if alpha >= 1 then
			conn:Disconnect()
			webLaunchConnections[mainBeam] = nil
			mainBeam.CurveSize0 = 0
			mainBeam.CurveSize1 = 0
			loopBeam.Enabled = false 
			midAtt.WorldPosition = worldPos
		end
	end)
	webLaunchConnections[mainBeam] = conn
end

function setupWebBeams(terrain)
	for _, name in pairs({"SpiderWebBeamR","SpiderWebBeamL","WebWorldAttR","WebWorldAttL","WebMidAttR","WebMidAttL","WebLoopBeamR","WebLoopBeamL"}) do
		if terrain:FindFirstChild(name) then terrain[name]:Destroy() end
	end

	worldAttR = Instance.new("Attachment", terrain); worldAttR.Name = "WebWorldAttR"
	worldAttL = Instance.new("Attachment", terrain); worldAttL.Name = "WebWorldAttL"
	webMidAttR = Instance.new("Attachment", terrain); webMidAttR.Name = "WebMidAttR"
	webMidAttL = Instance.new("Attachment", terrain); webMidAttL.Name = "WebMidAttL"

	local function makeBeam(name, att0, att1)
		local b = Instance.new("Beam", terrain)
		b.Name = name
		b.Attachment0 = att0
		b.Attachment1 = att1
		b.Texture = WEB_BEAM_TEXTURE
		b.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Wrap
		b.TextureLength = 12
		b.TextureSpeed = 0
		b.Width0 = 0.13
		b.Width1 = 0.07
		b.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1))
		b.LightEmission = 0
		b.LightInfluence = 0
		b.FaceCamera = true
		b.Segments = 20
		b.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
		b.CurveSize0 = 0
		b.CurveSize1 = 0
		b.Enabled = false
		return b
	end

	webBeamR = makeBeam("SpiderWebBeamR", handAttachmentR, webMidAttR)
	webBeamL = makeBeam("SpiderWebBeamL", handAttachmentL, webMidAttL)
	webLoopBeamR = makeBeam("WebLoopBeamR", webMidAttR, worldAttR)
	webLoopBeamL = makeBeam("WebLoopBeamL", webMidAttL, worldAttL)
	applyWebColor()
end

getgenv()._hudEditActive = false

getgenv()._enterHudEditMode = function()
	pcall(function()
		local existing = player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SpideyHudEdit")
		if existing then existing:Destroy() end

		local hud = getgenv()._spideyHUD
		if not hud then return end
		getgenv()._hudEditActive = true

		local hudGui = hud.container and hud.container.Parent
		if not hudGui then return end
		hud.container.Visible = false

		local cellMap = {
			{key = "speed", src = hud.speedCell, baseW = 80, baseH = 36},
			{key = "height", src = hud.heightCell, baseW = 80, baseH = 36},
			{key = "momentum", src = hud.momCell, baseW = 120, baseH = 36},
			{key = "swings", src = hud.swingCell, baseW = 80, baseH = 36},
		}

		local vps = camera.ViewportSize
		local totalW = 0
		for _, c in ipairs(cellMap) do totalW = totalW + c.baseW + 4 end
		local startX = (vps.X - totalW) / 2
		local defaultY = 60
		local curX = startX

		local toggleMap = {speed = hudSpeedometer, height = hudHeightMeter, momentum = hudMomentumBar, swings = hudSwingCounter}
		for _, c in ipairs(cellMap) do
			if c.src then
				local shouldShow = toggleMap[c.key]
				if shouldShow == nil then shouldShow = true end
				c.src.Visible = shouldShow
				c.src.Parent = hudGui
				c.src.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
				c.src.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
				local constraint = c.src:FindFirstChild("SizeLimit")
				if constraint then constraint.MaxSize = Vector2.new(400, 400) end
				local sc = (hudLayout[c.key] and hudLayout[c.key].scale) or 1
				local posX = (hudLayout[c.key] and hudLayout[c.key].xOff) or curX
				local posY = (hudLayout[c.key] and hudLayout[c.key].yOff) or defaultY
				c.src.Position = UDim2.new(0, posX, 0, posY)
				c.src.Size = UDim2.new(0, c.baseW * sc, 0, c.baseH * sc)
				c.src.BackgroundColor3 = THEME_OPTIONS[themeIndex].bg
				c.src.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
				if not c.src:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner") then
					Instance.new("UICorner", c.src).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
				end
				if not c.src:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") then
					local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", c.src)
					s.Color = THEME_OPTIONS[themeIndex].accent
					s.Transparency = 0.75
					s.Thickness = 1
				end
				curX = curX + c.baseW * sc + 4
			end
		end

		hudGui.DisplayOrder = 110

		local editGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		editGui.Name = "SpideyHudEdit"
		editGui.DisplayOrder = 100
		editGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
		editGui.Parent = player.PlayerGui

		local dim = Instance.new("TextButton", editGui)
		dim.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		dim.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		dim.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		dim.BorderSizePixel = 0
		dim.Text = ""
		dim.ZIndex = 1
		TweenService:Create(dim, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5}):Play()

		local titleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", editGui)
		titleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 350, 0, 22)
		titleLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8)
		titleLbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		titleLbl.Text = "Drag to move · Right edge to resize · Drop cells close to snap"
		titleLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
		titleLbl.TextSize = 10
		titleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		titleLbl.ZIndex = 10

		local doneBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", editGui)
		doneBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 32)
		doneBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -50)
		doneBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		doneBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 80)
		doneBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		doneBtn.Text = "Done"
		doneBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		doneBtn.TextSize = 14
		doneBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		doneBtn.ZIndex = 10
		Instance.new("UICorner", doneBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

		local editConns = {}
		local allDraggables = {}

		local function makeDraggable(key, frame, baseW, baseH, canSnap)
			if not frame or not frame.Parent then return end
			frame.ZIndex = 5

			local dragHandle = Instance.new("TextButton", editGui)
			dragHandle.Size = UDim2.new(0, frame.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, frame.AbsoluteSize.Y)
			dragHandle.Position = UDim2.new(0, frame.AbsolutePosition.X, 0, frame.AbsolutePosition.Y)
			dragHandle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			dragHandle.Text = ""
			dragHandle.ZIndex = 6

			local resizeHandle = Instance.new("TextButton", editGui)
			resizeHandle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, frame.AbsoluteSize.Y)
			resizeHandle.Position = UDim2.new(0, frame.AbsolutePosition.X + frame.AbsoluteSize.X - 5, 0, frame.AbsolutePosition.Y)
			resizeHandle.BackgroundTransparency = 0.85
			resizeHandle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			resizeHandle.Text = ""
			resizeHandle.ZIndex = 7
			resizeHandle.BorderSizePixel = 0

			local dragging = false
			local resizing = false
			local dragOff = Vector2.new(0, 0)

			dragHandle.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
				dragging = true
				local m = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
				dragOff = Vector2.new(m.X - frame.AbsolutePosition.X, m.Y - frame.AbsolutePosition.Y)
			end)

			resizeHandle.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
				resizing = true
			end)

			local endConn = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
				if dragging then
					dragging = false
					if canSnap then
						for _, other in pairs(allDraggables) do
							if other.key ~= key and other.canSnap then
								local myR = frame.AbsolutePosition.X + frame.AbsoluteSize.X
								local otherL = other.frame.AbsolutePosition.X
								local otherR = other.frame.AbsolutePosition.X + other.frame.AbsoluteSize.X
								local myL = frame.AbsolutePosition.X
								local yClose = math.abs(frame.AbsolutePosition.Y - other.frame.AbsolutePosition.Y) < 25
								if yClose and math.abs(myR - otherL) < 35 then
									local snapPos = UDim2.new(0, otherL - frame.AbsoluteSize.X - 2, 0, other.frame.AbsolutePosition.Y)
									TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Position = snapPos}):Play()
								elseif yClose and math.abs(myL - otherR) < 35 then
									local snapPos = UDim2.new(0, otherR + 2, 0, other.frame.AbsolutePosition.Y)
									TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Position = snapPos}):Play()
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
				resizing = false
			end)
			table.insert(editConns, endConn)

			local moveConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				if dragging then
					local m = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
					local nx = m.X - dragOff.X
					local ny = m.Y - dragOff.Y
					frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, nx, 0, ny)
				end
				if resizing then
					local m = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
					local newW = math.max(50, m.X - frame.AbsolutePosition.X)
					local sc = newW / baseW
					sc = math.clamp(sc, 0.5, 3)
					frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, baseW * sc, 0, baseH * sc)
					for _, desc in pairs(frame:GetDescendants()) do
						if desc:IsA("TextLabel") or desc:IsA("TextButton") then
							local base = desc:GetAttribute("BaseTextSize")
							if not base then base = desc.TextSize; desc:SetAttribute("BaseTextSize", base) end
							desc.TextSize = math.floor(base * sc)
						end
					end
				end
				dragHandle.Size = UDim2.new(0, frame.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, frame.AbsoluteSize.Y)
				dragHandle.Position = UDim2.new(0, frame.AbsolutePosition.X, 0, frame.AbsolutePosition.Y)
				resizeHandle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, frame.AbsoluteSize.Y)
				resizeHandle.Position = UDim2.new(0, frame.AbsolutePosition.X + frame.AbsoluteSize.X - 5, 0, frame.AbsolutePosition.Y)
			end)
			table.insert(editConns, moveConn)

			table.insert(allDraggables, {key = key, frame = frame, canSnap = canSnap, baseW = baseW, baseH = baseH, dragHandle = dragHandle, resizeHandle = resizeHandle})
		end

		for _, c in ipairs(cellMap) do
			if c.src and c.src.Visible then
				makeDraggable(c.key, c.src, c.baseW, c.baseH, true)
			end
		end
		if hud.radarFrame then
			hud.radarFrame.Visible = true
			makeDraggable("radar", hud.radarFrame, 100, 100, false)
		end

		doneBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			getgenv()._hudEditActive = false
			hudGui.DisplayOrder = 8
			hudLayoutCustom = true
			for _, d in pairs(allDraggables) do
				local sc = d.frame.AbsoluteSize.X / d.baseW
				hudLayout[d.key] = {xOff = d.frame.AbsolutePosition.X, yOff = d.frame.AbsolutePosition.Y, scale = math.clamp(sc, 0.5, 3)}
			end
			local toggleMap = {speed = hudSpeedometer, height = hudHeightMeter, momentum = hudMomentumBar, swings = hudSwingCounter}
			for _, c in ipairs(cellMap) do
				if c.src then
					local shouldShow = toggleMap[c.key]
					if shouldShow == nil then shouldShow = true end
					c.src.Visible = shouldShow
				end
			end
			if hud.radarFrame then hud.radarFrame.Visible = radarEnabled end
			saveConfig()
			for _, c in pairs(editConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
			editGui:Destroy()
		end)
	end)
end

task.defer(function()
	if hudLayoutCustom and next(hudLayout) then
		pcall(function()
			local hud = getgenv()._spideyHUD
			if not hud or not hud.container then return end
			local hudGui = hud.container.Parent
			hud.container.Visible = false
			local cells = {
				{key = "speed", src = hud.speedCell, baseW = 80, baseH = 36},
				{key = "height", src = hud.heightCell, baseW = 80, baseH = 36},
				{key = "momentum", src = hud.momCell, baseW = 120, baseH = 36},
				{key = "swings", src = hud.swingCell, baseW = 80, baseH = 36},
			}
			for _, c in ipairs(cells) do
				if c.src and hudLayout[c.key] then
					local l = hudLayout[c.key]
					local sc = l.scale or 1
					c.src.Parent = hudGui
					c.src.Position = UDim2.new(0, l.xOff, 0, l.yOff)
					c.src.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
					c.src.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
					c.src.Size = UDim2.new(0, c.baseW * sc, 0, c.baseH * sc)
					c.src.BackgroundColor3 = THEME_OPTIONS[themeIndex].bg
					c.src.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
					if not c.src:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner") then
						Instance.new("UICorner", c.src).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
					end
					if not c.src:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") then
						local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", c.src)
						s.Color = THEME_OPTIONS[themeIndex].accent
						s.Transparency = 0.75
						s.Thickness = 1
					end
					for _, desc in pairs(c.src:GetDescendants()) do
						if desc:IsA("TextLabel") then
							local base = desc:GetAttribute("BaseTextSize")
							if not base then base = desc.TextSize; desc:SetAttribute("BaseTextSize", base) end
							desc.TextSize = math.floor(base * sc)
						end
					end
				end
			end
			if hud.radarFrame and hudLayout.radar then
				local l = hudLayout.radar
				local sz = 100 * (l.scale or 1)
				hud.radarFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, l.xOff, 0, l.yOff)
				hud.radarFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, sz, 0, sz)
			end
		end)
	end
end)

pcall(function()
	local cmdGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	cmdGui.Name = "SpideyCmdBar"
	cmdGui.DisplayOrder = 120
	cmdGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	cmdGui.Parent = player.PlayerGui

	local cmdBar = Instance.new("Frame", cmdGui)
	cmdBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 340, 0, 36)
	cmdBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 50)
	cmdBar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
	cmdBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 18)
	cmdBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	cmdBar.Visible = false
	Instance.new("UICorner", cmdBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
	local cmdStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", cmdBar)
	cmdStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
	cmdStroke.Thickness = 1
	cmdStroke.Transparency = 0.5

	local cmdPrefix = Instance.new("TextLabel", cmdBar)
	cmdPrefix.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0)
	cmdPrefix.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
	cmdPrefix.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	cmdPrefix.Text = ">"
	cmdPrefix.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120)
	cmdPrefix.TextSize = 14
	cmdPrefix.Font = Enum.Font.Code

	local cmdInput = Instance.new("TextBox", cmdBar)
	getgenv()._cmdInput = cmdInput
	cmdInput.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
	cmdInput.Position = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 0)
	cmdInput.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	cmdInput.Text = ""
	cmdInput.PlaceholderText = "Type a command..."
	cmdInput.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
	cmdInput.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
	cmdInput.TextSize = 13
	cmdInput.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	cmdInput.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	cmdInput.ClearTextOnFocus = true

	local notifContainer = Instance.new("Frame", cmdGui)
	notifContainer.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 300)
	notifContainer.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -60)
	notifContainer.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
	notifContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	notifContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
	local notifLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", notifContainer)
	notifLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	notifLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
	notifLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)

	local cmdBarOpen = false

	local notifOrder = 0
	local function showOutput(text, duration)
		notifOrder = notifOrder + 1
		local notif = Instance.new("Frame", notifContainer)
		notif.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		notif.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		notif.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 18)
		notif.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
		notif.BorderSizePixel = 0
		notif.LayoutOrder = notifOrder
		notif.ClipsDescendants = true
		Instance.new("UICorner", notif).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
		local nStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", notif)
		nStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
		nStroke.Thickness = 1
		nStroke.Transparency = 0.5
		local nTxt = Instance.new("TextLabel", notif)
		nTxt.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 0)
		nTxt.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		nTxt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 0)
		nTxt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		nTxt.Text = text
		nTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 190)
		nTxt.TextSize = 10
		nTxt.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		nTxt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		nTxt.TextWrapped = true
		local nPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", notif)
		nPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5)
		nPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 5)
		notif.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		TweenService:Create(notif, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)}):Play()
		task.delay(duration or 3, function()
			pcall(function()
				TweenService:Create(notif, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)}):Play()
				task.delay(0.2, function() notif:Destroy() end)
			end)
		end)
	end

	local function findPlayer(query)
		query = query:lower()
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p.Name:lower() == query or p.DisplayName:lower() == query then return p end
		end
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p.Name:lower():find(query, 1, true) or p.DisplayName:lower():find(query, 1, true) then return p end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local commands = {}
	commands["tp"] = {desc = "tp [player/random] - Teleport to player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: tp [player name] or tp random") end
		if args[1]:lower() == "random" then commands["random"].fn({}); return end
		local target = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not target then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not myRoot then myRoot = hrp end
		local targetRoot = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if targetRoot and myRoot then
			local targetPos = targetRoot.Position
			myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos.X, targetPos.Y + 3, targetPos.Z)
			showOutput("Teleported to " .. target.DisplayName)
		else
			showOutput("Can't teleport - character not found")
		end
	end}
	commands["goto"] = commands["tp"]
	commands["to"] = commands["tp"]

	commands["view"] = {desc = "view [player/random] - Spectate player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			local currentHum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			camera.CameraSubject = currentHum or humanoid
			return showOutput("Stopped viewing")
		end
		if args[1]:lower() == "random" then
			local others = {}
			for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= player and p.Character then table.insert(others, p) end end
			if #others == 0 then return showOutput("No players") end
			local t = others[math.random(1, #others)]
			if t.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
				camera.CameraSubject = t.Character.Humanoid
				return showOutput("Viewing " .. t.DisplayName .. " (random)")
			end
		end
		local target = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not target then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		if target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			camera.CameraSubject = target.Character.Humanoid
			showOutput("Viewing " .. target.DisplayName .. " (type 'unview' to stop)")
		end
	end}
	commands["spectate"] = commands["view"]

	commands["unview"] = {desc = "unview - Stop spectating", fn = function()
		camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		local currentHum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		camera.CameraSubject = currentHum or humanoid
		showOutput("Stopped viewing")
	end}
	commands["unspectate"] = commands["unview"]

	commands["fly"] = {desc = "fly - Toggle fly mode", fn = function()
		cmdBarFlying = not cmdBarFlying
		if cmdBarFlying then
			showOutput("Fly ON (WASD + Space/Ctrl) | Speed: " .. cmdBarFlySpeed)
		else
			showOutput("Fly OFF")
			if bv then bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero end
		end
	end}

	commands["flyspeed"] = {desc = "flyspeed [number] - Set fly speed", fn = function(args)
		local spd = tonumber(args[1])
		if not spd then return showOutput("Usage: flyspeed [number]") end
		cmdBarFlySpeed = math.clamp(spd, 10, 500)
		showOutput("Fly speed set to " .. cmdBarFlySpeed)
	end}
	commands["fspeed"] = commands["flyspeed"]

	commands["speed"] = {desc = "speed [number] - Set walk speed", fn = function(args)
		local spd = tonumber(args[1])
		if not spd then return showOutput("Usage: speed [number]") end
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(spd, 0, 500)
		showOutput("Walk speed set to " .. spd)
	end}
	commands["ws"] = commands["speed"]

	commands["jp"] = {desc = "jp [number] - Set jump power", fn = function(args)
		local pow = tonumber(args[1])
		if not pow then return showOutput("Usage: jp [number]") end
		humanoid.JumpPower = math.clamp(pow, 0, 500)
		showOutput("Jump power set to " .. pow)
	end}
	commands["jumppower"] = commands["jp"]

	commands["fov"] = {desc = "fov [number] - Set field of view", fn = function(args)
		local f = tonumber(args[1])
		if not f then return showOutput("Usage: fov [number]") end
		camera.FieldOfView = math.clamp(f, 20, 120)
		showOutput("FOV set to " .. f)
	end}

	commands["freecam"] = {desc = "freecam - Toggle free camera", fn = function()
		if not photoModeActive then
			photoModeActive = true
			photoModeChar = char
			if hrp then hrp.Anchored = true end
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
			showOutput("Freecam ON (WASD + Space/Ctrl, Shift = fast, press L to exit)")
		else
			photoModeActive = false
			if photoModeChar and photoModeChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				photoModeChar.HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
			end
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
			showOutput("Freecam OFF")
		end
	end}

	commands["noclip"] = {desc = "noclip - Toggle noclip", fn = function()
		cmdBarNoclip = not cmdBarNoclip
		showOutput("Noclip " .. (cmdBarNoclip and "ON" or "OFF"))
	end}
	commands["clip"] = commands["noclip"]

	commands["unfly"] = {desc = "unfly - Turn off fly", fn = function()
		cmdBarFlying = false
		if bv then bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero end
		showOutput("Fly OFF")
	end}

	commands["random"] = {desc = "random - Teleport to random player", fn = function()
		local others = {}
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= player and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				table.insert(others, p)
			end
		end
		if #others == 0 then return showOutput("No other players found") end
		local target = others[math.random(1, #others)]
		local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if myRoot then
			myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
			showOutput("Teleported to " .. target.DisplayName .. " (random)")
		end
	end}

	commands["reset"] = {desc = "reset - Force reset character", fn = function()
		pcall(function() getgenv()._doForceReset() end)
		showOutput("Force resetting...")
	end}

	commands["rejoin"] = {desc = "rejoin - Rejoin the server", fn = function()
		showOutput("Rejoining...")
		task.delay(0.5, function()
			pcall(function()
				game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId)
			end)
		end)
	end}

	commands["rj"] = commands["rejoin"]

	commands["sit"] = {desc = "sit - Make character sit", fn = function()
		humanoid.Sit = true
		showOutput("Sitting")
	end}

	commands["unsit"] = {desc = "unsit - Stand up", fn = function()
		humanoid.Sit = false
		humanoid.PlatformStand = false
		showOutput("Standing")
	end}

	commands["gravity"] = {desc = "gravity [number] - Set gravity", fn = function(args)
		local g = tonumber(args[1])
		if not g then return showOutput("Usage: gravity [number] (default 196.2)") end
		workspace.Gravity = g
		showOutput("Gravity set to " .. g)
	end}

	commands["time"] = {desc = "time [number] - Set lighting time", fn = function(args)
		local t = tonumber(args[1])
		if not t then return showOutput("Usage: time [0-24]") end
		Lighting.ClockTime = t
		showOutput("Time set to " .. t)
	end}

	commands["fogend"] = {desc = "fogend [number] - Set fog distance", fn = function(args)
		local f = tonumber(args[1])
		if not f then return showOutput("Usage: fogend [number]") end
		Lighting.FogEnd = f
		showOutput("Fog end set to " .. f)
	end}

	commands["hidename"] = {desc = "hidename - Hide your display name overhead", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
			if head then
				for _, obj in pairs(head:GetChildren()) do
					if obj:IsA("BillboardGui") then obj.Enabled = false end
				end
			end
		end)
		showOutput("Name hidden")
	end}

	commands["showname"] = {desc = "showname - Show your display name", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
			if head then
				for _, obj in pairs(head:GetChildren()) do
					if obj:IsA("BillboardGui") then obj.Enabled = true end
				end
			end
		end)
		showOutput("Name shown")
	end}

	commands["esp"] = {desc = "esp - Activate sonar pulse", fn = function()
		pcall(ActivateSonar)
		showOutput("ESP pulse activated")
	end}

	commands["cmds"] = {desc = "cmds - Show all commands", fn = function()
		local list = {}
		local seen = {}
		for name, cmd in pairs(commands) do
			if not seen[cmd] then
				seen[cmd] = true
				table.insert(list, cmd.desc)
			end
		end
		table.sort(list)

		local existing = player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SpideyCmdsWindow")
		if existing then existing:Destroy(); return end

		local cmdsGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		cmdsGui.Name = "SpideyCmdsWindow"
		cmdsGui.DisplayOrder = 130
		cmdsGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
		cmdsGui.Parent = player.PlayerGui

		local win = Instance.new("Frame", cmdsGui)
		win.Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 480)
		win.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		win.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		win.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 18)
		win.BorderSizePixel = 0
		win.ClipsDescendants = true
		Instance.new("UICorner", win).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
		local winStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", win)
		winStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
		winStroke.Thickness = 1

		win.Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 0)
		TweenService:Create(win, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 480)}):Play()

		local titleBar = Instance.new("Frame", win)
		titleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
		titleBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 25)
		titleBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Instance.new("UICorner", titleBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

		local dragBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", titleBar)
		dragBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -65, 1, 0)
		dragBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		dragBtn.Text = ""
		dragBtn.ZIndex = 2
		local draggingWin = false
		local dragOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		dragBtn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
			draggingWin = true
			local m = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
			dragOffset = Vector2.new(m.X - win.AbsolutePosition.X, m.Y - win.AbsolutePosition.Y)
		end)
		UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then draggingWin = false end
		end)
		RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			if draggingWin then
				local m = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
				win.Position = UDim2.new(0, m.X - dragOffset.X + win.AbsoluteSize.X / 2, 0, m.Y - dragOffset.Y + win.AbsoluteSize.Y / 2)
			end
		end)

		local titleTxt = Instance.new("TextLabel", titleBar)
		titleTxt.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
		titleTxt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
		titleTxt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		titleTxt.Text = "Commands (" .. #list .. ")"
		titleTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
		titleTxt.TextSize = 14
		titleTxt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		titleTxt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

		local minBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", titleBar)
		minBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
		minBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0.5, 0)
		minBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		minBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
		minBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		minBtn.Text = "−"
		minBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
		minBtn.TextSize = 16
		minBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		Instance.new("UICorner", minBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
		local minimized = false
		minBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			minimized = not minimized
			if minimized then
				TweenService:Create(win, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 36)}):Play()
				minBtn.Text = "+"
			else
				TweenService:Create(win, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 480)}):Play()
				minBtn.Text = "−"
			end
		end)

		local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", titleBar)
		closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
		closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0.5, 0)
		closeBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 50, 50)
		closeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		closeBtn.Text = "✕"
		closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		closeBtn.TextSize = 12
		closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		Instance.new("UICorner", closeBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
		closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			TweenService:Create(win, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 0)}):Play()
			task.delay(0.2, function() cmdsGui:Destroy() end)
		end)

		local tooltip = Instance.new("Frame", cmdsGui)
		tooltip.Name = "Tooltip"
		tooltip.Size = UDim2.new(0, 250, 0, 0)
		tooltip.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		tooltip.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 35)
		tooltip.BorderSizePixel = 0
		tooltip.Visible = false
		tooltip.ZIndex = 20
		Instance.new("UICorner", tooltip).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
		local tooltipStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", tooltip)
		tooltipStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
		tooltipStroke.Thickness = 1
		local tooltipPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", tooltip)
		tooltipPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
		tooltipPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
		tooltipPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
		tooltipPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
		local tooltipTxt = Instance.new("TextLabel", tooltip)
		tooltipTxt.Name = "Text"
		tooltipTxt.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		tooltipTxt.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		tooltipTxt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		tooltipTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
		tooltipTxt.TextSize = 12
		tooltipTxt.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		tooltipTxt.TextWrapped = true
		tooltipTxt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		tooltipTxt.ZIndex = 20

		local searchBar = Instance.new("TextBox", win)
		searchBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 26)
		searchBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 39)
		searchBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 28)
		searchBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
		searchBar.Text = ""
		searchBar.PlaceholderText = "Search commands..."
		searchBar.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 70)
		searchBar.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
		searchBar.TextSize = 11
		searchBar.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		searchBar.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		searchBar.ClearTextOnFocus = false
		Instance.new("UICorner", searchBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
		local searchPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", searchBar)
		searchPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)

		local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", win)
		scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -72)
		scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 68)
		scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
		scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
		scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
		scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

		local scrollLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", scroll)
		scrollLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		scrollLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)

		local allCmdRows = {}

		for i, desc in ipairs(list) do
			local cmdName = desc
			local cmdDesc = ""
			local dashPos = desc:find(" %- ")
			if dashPos then
				cmdName = desc:sub(1, dashPos - 1)
				cmdDesc = desc:sub(dashPos + 3)
			end
			local searchKey = desc:lower()

			local cmdRow = Instance.new("Frame", scroll)
			cmdRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 28)
			cmdRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 28)
			cmdRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
			cmdRow.LayoutOrder = i
			Instance.new("UICorner", cmdRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

			local cmdTxt = Instance.new("TextLabel", cmdRow)
			cmdTxt.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0)
			cmdTxt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
			cmdTxt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			cmdTxt.Text = cmdName
			cmdTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
			cmdTxt.TextSize = 12
			cmdTxt.Font = Enum.Font.Code
			cmdTxt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			cmdTxt.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

			table.insert(allCmdRows, {row = cmdRow, key = searchKey})

			local hoverBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", cmdRow)
			hoverBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			hoverBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			hoverBtn.Text = ""
			hoverBtn.ZIndex = 5

			hoverBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				cmdRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 45)
				cmdTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				if cmdDesc ~= "" then
					tooltipTxt.Text = cmdDesc
					tooltip.Visible = true
				end
			end)

			hoverBtn.MouseMoved:Connect(function()
				local m = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
				tooltip.Position = UDim2.new(0, m.X + 15, 0, m.Y - 30)
			end)

			hoverBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				cmdRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 28)
				cmdTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
				tooltip.Visible = false
			end)
		end

		searchBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			local term = searchBar.Text:lower()
			for _, entry in pairs(allCmdRows) do
				if term == "" or entry.key:find(term, 1, true) then
					entry.row.Visible = true
				else
					entry.row.Visible = false
				end
			end
		end)
	end}
	commands["help"] = commands["cmds"]
	commands["commands"] = commands["cmds"]

	commands["print"] = {desc = "print [text] - Print to console", fn = function(args)
		local msg = table.concat(args, " ")
		print("[Spidey] " .. msg)
		showOutput("Printed: " .. msg)
	end}

	commands["players"] = {desc = "players - List all players", fn = function()
		local list = {}
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			table.insert(list, p.DisplayName .. " (@" .. p.Name .. ")")
		end
		showOutput(table.concat(list, "\n"), 6)
	end}

	commands["re"] = {desc = "re - Reset character", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			local h = (player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")) or humanoid
			if h then h.Health = 0 end
		end)
		showOutput("Resetting...")
	end}

	commands["age"] = {desc = "age [player] - Show account age", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: age [player]") end
		if args[1]:lower() == "random" then
			local others = {} for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= player then table.insert(others, p) end end
			if #others > 0 then local t = others[math.random(1,#others)]; return showOutput(t.DisplayName .. " account age: " .. t.AccountAge .. " days") end
		end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		showOutput(t.DisplayName .. " account age: " .. t.AccountAge .. " days")
	end}

	commands["id"] = {desc = "id [player] - Show user ID", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Your ID: " .. player.UserId) end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		showOutput(t.DisplayName .. " ID: " .. t.UserId)
	end}

	commands["ping"] = {desc = "ping - Show your ping", fn = function()
		local ping = math.floor(game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue())
		showOutput("Ping: " .. ping .. "ms")
	end}

	commands["fps"] = {desc = "fps - Show current FPS", fn = function()
		local fps = math.floor(1 / RunService.RenderStepped:Wait())
		showOutput("FPS: ~" .. fps)
	end}

	commands["serverhop"] = {desc = "serverhop - Join a different server", fn = function()
		showOutput("Server hopping...")
		task.delay(0.5, function()
			pcall(function()
				game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId)
			end)
		end)
	end}
	commands["sh"] = commands["serverhop"]

	commands["copyid"] = {desc = "copyid [player] - Copy user ID to clipboard", fn = function(args)
		local t = player
		if args[1] then t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " ")) end
		if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		pcall(function() setclipboard(tostring(t.UserId)) end)
		showOutput("Copied ID: " .. t.UserId)
	end}

	commands["copyname"] = {desc = "copyname [player] - Copy username to clipboard", fn = function(args)
		local t = player
		if args[1] then t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " ")) end
		if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		pcall(function() setclipboard(t.Name) end)
		showOutput("Copied: " .. t.Name)
	end}

	commands["clearall"] = {desc = "clearall - Reset all visual effects", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			workspace.Gravity = 196.2
			Lighting.Brightness = 1
			Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
			Lighting.ClockTime = 14
			Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10
			Lighting.FogStart = 1e10
			cmdBarFlying = false
			cmdBarNoclip = false
			if bv then bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero end
			if humanoid then humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16; humanoid.JumpPower = 50; humanoid.HipHeight = 0 end
			camera.FieldOfView = 70
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			if humanoid then camera.CameraSubject = humanoid end
			player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 128
			pcall(function() getgenv()._resetSonar() end)
		end)
		showOutput("Everything reset to default")
	end}

	commands["spin"] = {desc = "spin [speed] - Spin your character", fn = function(args)
		local spd = tonumber(args[1]) or 20
		if getgenv()._spinConn then getgenv()._spinConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._spinConn = nil; showOutput("Spin OFF"); return end
		getgenv()._spinConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			if hrp then hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(spd), 0) end
		end)
		showOutput("Spin ON (speed " .. spd .. ") - type spin to stop")
	end}

	commands["unspin"] = {desc = "unspin - Stop spinning", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._spinConn then getgenv()._spinConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._spinConn = nil end
		showOutput("Spin OFF")
	end}

	commands["headsize"] = {desc = "headsize [number] - Scale head size", fn = function(args)
		local s = tonumber(args[1]) or 1
		pcall(function() char.Head.Size = Vector3.new(s, s, s) end)
		showOutput("Head size: " .. s)
	end}

	commands["platform"] = {desc = "platform - Create platform under you", fn = function()
		local p = Instance.new("Part")
		p.Size = Vector3.new(15, 1, 15); p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = true
		p.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
		p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon; p.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50); p.Transparency = 0.3
		p.Parent = workspace; Debris:AddItem(p, 30)
		showOutput("Platform created (30s)")
	end}

	commands["stair"] = {desc = "stair [count] - Create stairs ahead", fn = function(args)
		local count = tonumber(args[1]) or 10
		local look = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
		for i = 1, count do
			local s = Instance.new("Part")
			s.Size = Vector3.new(6, 0.5, 3); s.Anchored = true; s.CanCollide = true
			s.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + look * (i * 3) + Vector3.new(0, i * 2, 0))
			s.Material = Enum.Material.Neon; s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40); s.Transparency = 0.4
			s.Parent = workspace; Debris:AddItem(s, 60)
		end
		showOutput(count .. " stairs created (60s)")
	end}
	commands["stairs"] = commands["stair"]

	commands["trail"] = {desc = "trail - Toggle body trail", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._cmdTrail then
			getgenv()._cmdTrail:Destroy(); getgenv()._cmdTrail = nil; showOutput("Trail OFF"); return
		end
		pcall(function()
			local att0 = Instance.new("Attachment", hrp); att0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
			local att1 = Instance.new("Attachment", hrp); att1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0)
			local t = Instance.new("Trail", hrp); t.Attachment0 = att0; t.Attachment1 = att1
			t.Lifetime = 0.8; t.MinLength = 0.1; t.FaceCamera = true
			t.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50), Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 50))
			t.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
			getgenv()._cmdTrail = t
		end)
		showOutput("Trail ON")
	end}

	commands["untrail"] = {desc = "untrail - Remove trail", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._cmdTrail then getgenv()._cmdTrail:Destroy(); getgenv()._cmdTrail = nil end
		showOutput("Trail OFF")
	end}

	commands["size"] = {desc = "size [number] - Scale character size", fn = function(args)
		local s = tonumber(args[1]) or 1
		pcall(function()
			local hDesc = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("HumanoidDescription")
			if hDesc then hDesc.HeightScale = s; hDesc.WidthScale = s; hDesc.DepthScale = s; hDesc.HeadScale = s end
		end)
		showOutput("Size set to " .. s .. " (may need respawn)")
	end}

	commands["hat"] = {desc = "hat - Remove all hats", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			for _, a in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do if a:IsA("Accessory") then a:Destroy() end end
		end)
		showOutput("Hats removed (client-side)")
	end}
	commands["nohat"] = commands["hat"]
	commands["nohats"] = commands["hat"]

	commands["face"] = {desc = "face - Remove face", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			for _, d in pairs(char.Head:GetChildren()) do if d:IsA("Decal") then d:Destroy() end end
		end)
		showOutput("Face removed")
	end}

	commands["mesh"] = {desc = "mesh - Remove all meshes", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			for _, d in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("SpecialMesh") or d:IsA("MeshPart") then pcall(function() d.Transparency = 1 end) end end
		end)
		showOutput("Meshes hidden")
	end}

	commands["dist"] = {desc = "dist [player] - Distance to player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: dist [player]") end
		if args[1]:lower() == "random" then
			local others = {} for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= player and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then table.insert(others, p) end end
			if #others == 0 then return showOutput("No players") end
			local t = others[math.random(1,#others)]
			return showOutput(t.DisplayName .. ": " .. math.floor((t.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude) .. " studs")
		end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t or not t.Character or not t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		showOutput(t.DisplayName .. ": " .. math.floor((t.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude) .. " studs")
	end}
	commands["distance"] = commands["dist"]

	commands["closest"] = {desc = "closest - Find nearest player", fn = function()
		local minDist = math.huge; local nearest = nil
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= player and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				local d = (p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
				if d < minDist then minDist = d; nearest = p end
			end
		end
		if nearest then showOutput("Closest: " .. nearest.DisplayName .. " (" .. math.floor(minDist) .. " studs)") else showOutput("No players found") end
	end}
	commands["near"] = commands["closest"]

	commands["tpclosest"] = {desc = "tpclosest - TP to nearest player", fn = function()
		local minDist = math.huge; local nearest = nil
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= player and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				local d = (p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
				if d < minDist then minDist = d; nearest = p end
			end
		end
		if nearest and nearest.Character then
			hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(nearest.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
			showOutput("TP to " .. nearest.DisplayName)
		end
	end}

	commands["count"] = {desc = "count - Player count", fn = function()
		showOutput("Players: " .. #Players:GetPlayers() .. "/" .. Players.MaxPlayers)
	end}

	commands["health"] = {desc = "health - Show your health", fn = function()

	commands["teams"] = {desc = "teams - List all teams", fn = function()
		local list = {}
		for _, t in pairs(game:GetService("Teams"):GetTeams()) do table.insert(list, t.Name .. " (" .. #t:GetPlayers() .. ")") end
		if #list == 0 then return showOutput("No teams") end
		showOutput(table.concat(list, "\n"), 5)
	end}

	commands["tools"] = {desc = "tools - List your tools", fn = function()
		local list = {}
		for _, t in pairs(player.Backpack:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(list, t.Name) end end
		for _, t in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(list, t.Name .. " (held)") end end
		if #list == 0 then return showOutput("No tools") end
		showOutput(table.concat(list, ", "))
	end}

	commands["equip"] = {desc = "equip [tool] - Equip a tool by name", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: equip [tool name]") end
		local toolName = table.concat(args, " "):lower()
		for _, t in pairs(player.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
			if t:IsA("Tool") and t.Name:lower():find(toolName, 1, true) then
				humanoid:EquipTool(t); showOutput("Equipped " .. t.Name); return
			end
		end
		showOutput("Tool not found")
	end}

	commands["unequip"] = {desc = "unequip - Unequip current tool", fn = function()
		humanoid:UnequipTools()
		showOutput("Unequipped all tools")
	end}

	commands["fullbright"] = {desc = "fullbright - Max lighting brightness", fn = function()
		Lighting.Brightness = 2; Lighting.Ambient = Color3.new(1,1,1); Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.new(1,1,1)
		Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10; Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
		showOutput("Fullbright ON")
	end}

	commands["blur"] = {desc = "blur [amount] - Add screen blur", fn = function(args)
		local amt = tonumber(args[1]) or 10
		local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChild("SpideyBlur")
		if existing then existing:Destroy() end
		local b = Instance.new("BlurEffect"); b.Name = "SpideyBlur"; b.Size = amt; b.Parent = Lighting
		showOutput("Blur set to " .. amt)
	end}

	commands["unblur"] = {desc = "unblur - Remove blur", fn = function()

		showOutput("Blur removed")

	commands["bloom"] = {desc = "bloom [intensity] - Add bloom effect", fn = function(args)
		local amt = tonumber(args[1]) or 1.5
		local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChild("SpideyBloom")
		if existing then existing:Destroy() end
		local b = Instance.new("BloomEffect"); b.Name = "SpideyBloom"; b.Intensity = amt; b.Threshold = 0.8; b.Size = 24; b.Parent = Lighting
		showOutput("Bloom set to " .. amt)
	end}
