	commands["cc"] = {desc = "cc [sat] [contrast] [bright] - Color correction", fn = function(args)
		local sat = tonumber(args[1]) or 0
		local con = tonumber(args[2]) or 0
		local bri = tonumber(args[3]) or 0
		local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChild("SpideyCC")
		if existing then existing:Destroy() end
		local c = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect"); c.Name = "SpideyCC"
		c.Saturation = sat; c.Contrast = con; c.Brightness = bri; c.Parent = Lighting
		showOutput("Color correction: sat=" .. sat .. " con=" .. con .. " bri=" .. bri)
	end}

		showOutput("Color correction removed")
	end}

	commands["sunrays"] = {desc = "sunrays - Toggle sun rays", fn = function()
		local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChild("SpideySunRays")
		if existing then existing:Destroy(); showOutput("Sun rays OFF"); return end
		local s = Instance.new("SunRaysEffect"); s.Name = "SpideySunRays"; s.Intensity = 0.15; s.Spread = 0.8; s.Parent = Lighting
		showOutput("Sun rays ON")
	end}

	commands["dof"] = {desc = "dof [distance] - Depth of field", fn = function(args)
		local d = tonumber(args[1]) or 30
		local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChild("SpideyDOF")
		if existing then existing:Destroy() end
		local dof = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect"); dof.Name = "SpideyDOF"; dof.FocusDistance = d; dof.InFocusRadius = d * 0.5; dof.NearIntensity = 0.5; dof.FarIntensity = 0.3; dof.Parent = Lighting
		showOutput("DOF focus: " .. d)
	end}

	commands["undof"] = {desc = "undof - Remove depth of field", fn = function()

		showOutput("DOF removed")

	commands["vignette"] = {desc = "vignette - Toggle vignette effect", fn = function()
		local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChild("SpideyVignette")
		if existing then existing:Destroy(); showOutput("Vignette OFF"); return end
		local c = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect"); c.Name = "SpideyVignette"; c.Contrast = 0.3; c.Saturation = -0.2; c.Parent = Lighting
		showOutput("Vignette ON")
	end}

	commands["tint"] = {desc = "tint [r] [g] [b] - Tint screen color", fn = function(args)

		showOutput("Tint set")

	commands["removeeffects"] = {desc = "removeeffects - Remove all custom effects", fn = function()
		for _, name in pairs({"SpideyBlur", "SpideyBloom", "SpideyCC", "SpideySunRays", "SpideyDOF", "SpideyVignette", "SpideyTint"}) do
			local e = Lighting:FindFirstChild(name); if e then e:Destroy() end
		end
		showOutput("All effects removed")
	end}
	commands["noeffects"] = commands["removeeffects"]

	commands["sensitivity"] = {desc = "sensitivity [number] - Set mouse sensitivity", fn = function(args)

		showOutput("Sensitivity set to " .. s)
	end}
	commands["sens"] = commands["sensitivity"]

	commands["fp"] = commands["lockfirstperson"]

		showOutput("Camera unlocked")

	commands["unfp"] = commands["unlockfirstperson"]

	commands["cammove"] = {desc = "cammove [speed] - Camera smooth speed", fn = function(args)
		showOutput("Use freecam command + Shift for fast camera movement")
	end}

	commands["savepos"] = {desc = "savepos [name] - Save current position", fn = function(args)
		if not getgenv()._savedPositions then getgenv()._savedPositions = {} end
		local name = args[1] or "default"
		getgenv()._savedPositions[name] = hrp.CFrame
		showOutput("Position '" .. name .. "' saved")
	end}

	commands["loadpos"] = {desc = "loadpos [name] - Load saved position", fn = function(args)
		if not getgenv()._savedPositions then return showOutput("No saved positions") end
		local name = args[1] or "default"
		local cf = getgenv()._savedPositions[name]
		if not cf then return showOutput("Position '" .. name .. "' not found") end
		hrp.CFrame = cf
		showOutput("Loaded position '" .. name .. "'")
	end}
	commands["sp"] = commands["savepos"]
	commands["lp"] = commands["loadpos"]

	commands["listpos"] = {desc = "listpos - List saved positions", fn = function()
		if not getgenv()._savedPositions or not next(getgenv()._savedPositions) then return showOutput("No saved positions") end
		local list = {}
		for name, _ in pairs(getgenv()._savedPositions) do table.insert(list, name) end
		showOutput("Saved: " .. table.concat(list, ", "))
	end}

	commands["looptp"] = {desc = "looptp [player] - Loop teleport to player", fn = function(args)
		if getgenv()._loopTpConn then getgenv()._loopTpConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._loopTpConn = nil; showOutput("Loop TP OFF"); return end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: looptp [player]") end
		local target = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not target then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		getgenv()._loopTpConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				if target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and hrp then
					hrp.CFrame = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 3)
				end
			end)
		end)
		showOutput("Loop TP to " .. target.DisplayName .. " - type looptp to stop")
	end}
	commands["unlooptp"] = {desc = "unlooptp - Stop loop TP", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._loopTpConn then getgenv()._loopTpConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._loopTpConn = nil end
		showOutput("Loop TP OFF")
	end}

	commands["servertime"] = {desc = "servertime - Show server time", fn = function()
		showOutput("Server time: " .. math.floor(workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) .. "s | " .. os.date("%H:%M:%S"))
	end}

	commands["uptime"] = {desc = "uptime - Session uptime", fn = function()
		local elapsed = tick() - sessionStart
		local mins = math.floor(elapsed / 60)
		local secs = math.floor(elapsed % 60)
		showOutput("Session: " .. mins .. "m " .. secs .. "s")
	end}

	commands["tpall"] = {desc = "tpall - TP to all players one by one", fn = function()
		task.spawn(function()
			for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if p ~= player and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and hrp then
					hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
					showOutput("Visiting " .. p.DisplayName)
					task.wait(1.5)
				end
			end
			showOutput("Tour complete!")
		end)
	end}

	commands["follow"] = {desc = "follow [player] - Follow a player", fn = function(args)
		if getgenv()._followConn then
			getgenv()._followConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._followConn = nil
			local myHum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			if myHum then myHum.WalkSpeed = 16 end
			showOutput("Follow OFF")
			return
		end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: follow [player]") end
		if args[1]:lower() == "random" then
			local others = {} for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= player and p.Character then table.insert(others, p) end end
			if #others == 0 then return showOutput("No players") end
			args = {others[math.random(1,#others)].Name}
		end
		local target = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not target then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		getgenv()._followConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				local myChar = player.Character
				local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				if not myRoot or not myHum then return end
				if not target.Character or not target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
				local tPos = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
				local dist = (myRoot.Position - tPos).Magnitude
				if dist > 6 then
					myHum:MoveTo(tPos)
					if dist > 50 then
						myHum.WalkSpeed = 80
					elseif dist > 25 then
						myHum.WalkSpeed = 50
					elseif dist > 12 then
						myHum.WalkSpeed = 30
					else
						myHum.WalkSpeed = 16
					end
				else
					myHum.WalkSpeed = 16
				end
			end)
		end)
		showOutput("Following " .. target.DisplayName .. " - type follow or unfollow to stop")
	end}

	commands["unfollow"] = {desc = "unfollow - Stop following", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._followConn then getgenv()._followConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._followConn = nil end
		local myHum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if myHum then myHum.WalkSpeed = 16 end
		showOutput("Follow OFF")
	end}

	commands["orbit"] = {desc = "orbit [player] - Orbit around a player", fn = function(args)
		if getgenv()._orbitConn then getgenv()._orbitConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._orbitConn = nil; showOutput("Orbit OFF"); return end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: orbit [player]") end
		local target = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not target then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		local angle = 0
		getgenv()._orbitConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			pcall(function()
				if target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and hrp then
					angle = angle + dt * 2
					local radius = 12
					local tPos = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
					hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(tPos + Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, 5, math.sin(angle) * radius), tPos)
				end
			end)
		end)
		showOutput("Orbiting " .. target.DisplayName .. " - type orbit to stop")
	end}

	commands["unorbit"] = {desc = "unorbit - Stop orbiting", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._orbitConn then getgenv()._orbitConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._orbitConn = nil end
		showOutput("Orbit OFF")
	end}

	commands["annoy"] = {desc = "annoy [player] - TP to player repeatedly", fn = function(args)
		if getgenv()._annoyConn then task.cancel(getgenv()._annoyConn); getgenv()._annoyConn = nil; showOutput("Annoy OFF"); return end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: annoy [player]") end
		local target = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not target then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		getgenv()._annoyConn = task.spawn(function()
			while task.wait(0.5) do
				pcall(function()
					if target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and hrp then
						hrp.CFrame = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(math.random(-5,5), 3, math.random(-5,5))
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
		showOutput("Annoying " .. target.DisplayName .. " - type annoy to stop")
	end}

	commands["unannoy"] = {desc = "unannoy - Stop annoying", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._annoyConn then task.cancel(getgenv()._annoyConn); getgenv()._annoyConn = nil end
		showOutput("Annoy OFF")
	end}

	commands["antiafk"] = {desc = "antiafk - Anti-AFK status (always on)", fn = function()
		showOutput("Anti-AFK is always ON (built-in)")
	end}

	commands["nofall"] = {desc = "nofall - Toggle no fall damage", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._noFallConn then getgenv()._noFallConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._noFallConn = nil; showOutput("No fall OFF"); return end
		getgenv()._noFallConn = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, new)
			if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed then
				pcall(function() humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
			end
		end)
		showOutput("No fall damage ON")
	end}

	commands["xray"] = {desc = "xray - Toggle see through walls", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._xrayActive then
			getgenv()._xrayActive = false
			for _, p in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") and p:GetAttribute("XrayOrig") then
					p.Transparency = p:GetAttribute("XrayOrig"); p:SetAttribute("XrayOrig", nil)
				end
			end
			showOutput("X-Ray OFF")
		else
			getgenv()._xrayActive = true
			for _, p in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") and not p:IsDescendantOf(char) and p.Transparency < 0.5 then
					p:SetAttribute("XrayOrig", p.Transparency); p.Transparency = 0.7
				end
			end
			showOutput("X-Ray ON")
		end
	end}

	commands["highlight"] = {desc = "highlight [player] - Highlight a player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: highlight [player]") end
		if args[1]:lower() == "random" then
			local others = {} for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= player and p.Character then table.insert(others, p) end end
			if #others == 0 then return showOutput("No players") end
			local t = others[math.random(1,#others)]
			local h = Instance.new("Highlight", t.Character); h.Name = "CmdHL"; h.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
			Debris:AddItem(h, 15)
			return showOutput("Highlighted " .. t.DisplayName .. " (15s)")
		end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t or not t.Character then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		local h = Instance.new("Highlight", t.Character); h.Name = "CmdHL"; h.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
		Debris:AddItem(h, 15)
		showOutput("Highlighted " .. t.DisplayName .. " (15s)")
	end}
	commands["hl"] = commands["highlight"]

	commands["unhighlight"] = {desc = "unhighlight - Remove all highlights", fn = function()
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p.Character then for _, c in pairs(p.Character:GetChildren()) do if c.Name == "CmdHL" then c:Destroy() end end end
		end
		showOutput("Highlights removed")
	end}
	commands["unhl"] = commands["unhighlight"]

	commands["highlightall"] = {desc = "highlightall - Highlight all players", fn = function()
		local ct = 0
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= player and p.Character then
				for _, c in pairs(p.Character:GetChildren()) do if c.Name == "CmdHL" then c:Destroy() end end
				local h = Instance.new("Highlight", p.Character); h.Name = "CmdHL"; h.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
				Debris:AddItem(h, 30); ct = ct + 1
			end
		end
		showOutput("Highlighted " .. ct .. " players (30s)")
	end}
	commands["hlall"] = commands["highlightall"]

	commands["place"] = {desc = "place - Show game info", fn = function()
		showOutput("Place: " .. game.PlaceId .. " | Server: " .. game.JobId:sub(1, 8) .. "... | Players: " .. #Players:GetPlayers())
	end}

	commands["clear"] = {desc = "clear - Clear all notifications", fn = function()
		for _, c in pairs(notifContainer:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy() end
		end
	end}

	commands["fix"] = {desc = "fix - Fix all stuck webs, effects, and glitches", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			for _, name in pairs({"SpiderWebBeamR","SpiderWebBeamL","WebWorldAttR","WebWorldAttL","WebMidAttR","WebMidAttL","WebLoopBeamR","WebLoopBeamL"}) do
				local obj = workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChild(name)
				if obj then obj:Destroy() end
			end
			if hangBeam then hangBeam:Destroy(); hangBeam = nil end
			if hangWorldAtt then hangWorldAtt:Destroy(); hangWorldAtt = nil end
			if hangMidAtt then hangMidAtt:Destroy(); hangMidAtt = nil end
			for userId, data in pairs(OtherPlayersData) do
				pcall(function()
					if data.fx then if data.fx.beam then data.fx.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fx.worldAtt then data.fx.worldAtt:Destroy() end; if data.fx.handAtt then data.fx.handAtt:Destroy() end; data.fx = nil end
					if data.fxDoubleR then if data.fxDoubleR.beam then data.fxDoubleR.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fxDoubleR.worldAtt then data.fxDoubleR.worldAtt:Destroy() end; data.fxDoubleR = nil end
					if data.fxDoubleL then if data.fxDoubleL.beam then data.fxDoubleL.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fxDoubleL.worldAtt then data.fxDoubleL.worldAtt:Destroy() end; data.fxDoubleL = nil end
					if data.fxHang then if data.fxHang.beam then data.fxHang.beam:Destroy() end; if data.fxHang.worldAtt then data.fxHang.worldAtt:Destroy() end; data.fxHang = nil end
				end)
			end
			OtherPlayersData = {}
			local ct = 0
			for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				pcall(function()
					if obj.Name == "WebAnchorClump" or obj.Name == "WebEnd" or obj.Name == "ImpactCrater" or obj.Name == "ImpactDebris" or obj.Name == "SonicBoomRing" or obj.Name == "SpideyChargeAtt" or obj.Name == "SpideyLungeBurst" or obj.Name == "SpideyChargeVFX" then
						obj:Destroy(); ct = ct + 1
					end
				end)
			end
			for _, obj in pairs(workspace.Terrain:GetChildren()) do
				pcall(function()
					if obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Attachment") then obj:Destroy(); ct = ct + 1 end
				end)
			end
			camera.FieldOfView = 70
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			local myHum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			if myHum then
				camera.CameraSubject = myHum
				myHum.PlatformStand = false
				myHum.AutoRotate = true
				myHum.WalkSpeed = 16
			end
			local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if myRoot then myRoot.Anchored = false; myRoot.CanCollide = true end
			if ccCinematic then ccCinematic.Saturation = 0; ccCinematic.Contrast = 0 end
			if ccSonar then ccSonar:Destroy(); ccSonar = nil end
			isCinematic = false; slowMoAlpha = 0
			swinging = false; stuckToWall = false; isZipping = false; isDashing = false
			gliding = false; isSlingshotting = false; isChargingJump = false; isLaunching = false
			isEmoting = false; isHangingEmote = false; isVaulting = false; isMantling = false
			isHoldingMouse = false; wallDebounce = false; slideLocked = false
			currentMomentum = 0; shakeIntensity = 0; cameraRoll = 0; fovKick = 0
			workspace.Gravity = 196.2
			hideAllBeams()
			if handTrailR then handTrailR.Enabled = false end
			if handTrailL then handTrailL.Enabled = false end
			if bv then bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero end
			StopAllAnimations()
			pcall(function() setupWebBeams(workspace.Terrain) end)
			pcall(function() applyWebColor() end)
			showOutput("Fixed! Cleared " .. ct .. " objects")
		end)
	end}

	commands["invisible"] = {desc = "invisible - Toggle character invisibility", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			local myChar = player.Character
			if not myChar then return end
			local isInvis = myChar:GetAttribute("SpideyInvis")
			for _, part in pairs(myChar:GetDescendants()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then
					if isInvis then
						local orig = part:GetAttribute("OrigTransparency")
						if orig then part.Transparency = orig; part:SetAttribute("OrigTransparency", nil) end
					else
						part:SetAttribute("OrigTransparency", part.Transparency)
						part.Transparency = 1
					end
				elseif part:IsA("Decal") or part:IsA("Texture") then
					if isInvis then
						local orig = part:GetAttribute("OrigTransparency")
						if orig then part.Transparency = orig; part:SetAttribute("OrigTransparency", nil) end
					else
						part:SetAttribute("OrigTransparency", part.Transparency)
						part.Transparency = 1
					end
				end
			end
			for _, acc in pairs(myChar:GetChildren()) do
				if acc:IsA("Accessory") then
					for _, part in pairs(acc:GetDescendants()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") then
							if isInvis then
								local orig = part:GetAttribute("OrigTransparency")
								if orig then part.Transparency = orig; part:SetAttribute("OrigTransparency", nil) end
							else
								part:SetAttribute("OrigTransparency", part.Transparency)
								part.Transparency = 1
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			myChar:SetAttribute("SpideyInvis", not isInvis)
			showOutput(isInvis and "Visible" or "Invisible")
		end)
	end}
	commands["invis"] = commands["invisible"]

	commands["visible"] = {desc = "visible - Make character visible again", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			local myChar = player.Character
			if not myChar then return end
			for _, part in pairs(myChar:GetDescendants()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") or part:IsA("Decal") or part:IsA("Texture") then
					local orig = part:GetAttribute("OrigTransparency")
					if orig then part.Transparency = orig; part:SetAttribute("OrigTransparency", nil) end
				end
			end
			myChar:SetAttribute("SpideyInvis", nil)
			showOutput("Visible")
		end)
	end}
	commands["vis"] = commands["visible"]


	commands["clone"] = {desc = "clone - Spawn a fake clone of yourself", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			local myChar = player.Character
			if not myChar then return end
			local cl = myChar:Clone()
			cl.Name = "SpideyClone"
			for _, obj in pairs(cl:GetDescendants()) do
				if obj:IsA("Script") or obj:IsA("LocalScript") then obj:Destroy() end
			end
			local clHum = cl:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			if clHum then clHum.PlatformStand = false end
			local clRoot = cl:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if clRoot then clRoot.Anchored = true; clRoot.CFrame = myChar.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame end
			cl.Parent = workspace
			Debris:AddItem(cl, 60)
			showOutput("Clone spawned (60s)")
		end)
	end}

	commands["ghost"] = {desc = "ghost - Toggle ghost mode (invisible + noclip)", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			local myChar = player.Character
			if not myChar then return end
			local isGhost = myChar:GetAttribute("SpideyGhost")
			if isGhost then
				commands["visible"].fn({})

				myChar:SetAttribute("SpideyGhost", nil)
				showOutput("Ghost OFF")

				commands["invisible"].fn({})
				cmdBarNoclip = true
				myChar:SetAttribute("SpideyGhost", true)
				showOutput("Ghost ON (invisible + noclip)")
			end
		end)
	end}

		end)
		showOutput("Sent: " .. table.concat(args, " "))
	end}

	commands["spam"] = {desc = "spam [message] - Spam chat message 5 times", fn = function(args)

		task.spawn(function()
			for i = 1, 5 do
				pcall(function()
					game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents"):WaitForChild("SayMessageRequest"):FireServer(msg, "All")
				end)
				task.wait(0.5)
			end
		end)
		showOutput("Spamming: " .. msg)
	end}

		if getgenv()._stopwatchStart then
			local elapsed = tick() - getgenv()._stopwatchStart
			getgenv()._stopwatchStart = nil
			showOutput("Stopwatch: " .. string.format("%.1f", elapsed) .. "s")
		else
			getgenv()._stopwatchStart = tick()
			showOutput("Stopwatch started")
		end
	end}

		local mn = tonumber(args[1]) or 1

		showOutput("Random: " .. math.random(mn, mx) .. " (" .. mn .. "-" .. mx .. ")")

	commands["macro"] = {desc = "macro [name] [cmds] - Save command sequence (use | to separate)", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: macro [name] [cmd1 | cmd2 | cmd3]") end
		if not getgenv()._macros then getgenv()._macros = {} end
		local name = args[1]:lower()
		if not args[2] then
			if getgenv()._macros[name] then
				for _, cmd in pairs(getgenv()._macros[name]) do
					executeCmd(cmd)
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			else
				showOutput("Macro '" .. name .. "' not found")
			end
			return
		end
		local cmdStr = table.concat(args, " ", 2)
		local cmds = {}
		for cmd in cmdStr:gmatch("[^|]+") do
			table.insert(cmds, cmd:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"))
		end
		getgenv()._macros[name] = cmds
		showOutput("Macro '" .. name .. "' saved (" .. #cmds .. " commands)")
	end}

	commands["macros"] = {desc = "macros - List saved macros", fn = function()
		if not getgenv()._macros or not next(getgenv()._macros) then return showOutput("No macros saved") end
		local list = {}
		for name, cmds in pairs(getgenv()._macros) do table.insert(list, name .. " (" .. #cmds .. " cmds)") end
		showOutput(table.concat(list, ", "))
	end}

	commands["delmacro"] = {desc = "delmacro [name] - Delete a macro", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: delmacro [name]") end
		if getgenv()._macros then getgenv()._macros[args[1]:lower()] = nil end
		showOutput("Macro deleted")
	end}

	commands["serverage"] = {desc = "serverage - Average account age in server", fn = function()
		local total, count = 0, 0
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do total = total + p.AccountAge; count = count + 1 end
		showOutput("Average account age: " .. math.floor(total / math.max(count, 1)) .. " days (" .. count .. " players)")
	end}

	commands["oldest"] = {desc = "oldest - Find oldest account in server", fn = function()
		local best, bestAge = nil, 0
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p.AccountAge > bestAge then bestAge = p.AccountAge; best = p end end
		if best then showOutput("Oldest: " .. best.DisplayName .. " (" .. bestAge .. " days)") end
	end}

	commands["youngest"] = {desc = "youngest - Find newest account in server", fn = function()
		local best, bestAge = nil, math.huge
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p.AccountAge < bestAge then bestAge = p.AccountAge; best = p end end
		if best then showOutput("Youngest: " .. best.DisplayName .. " (" .. bestAge .. " days)") end
	end}

	commands["where"] = {desc = "where [player] - Show player's position", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: where [player]") end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t or not t.Character or not t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		local p = t.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
		showOutput(t.DisplayName .. ": X:" .. math.floor(p.X) .. " Y:" .. math.floor(p.Y) .. " Z:" .. math.floor(p.Z))
	end}

	commands["goto random"] = commands["random"]

	commands["copylook"] = {desc = "copylook [player] - Face same direction as player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: copylook [player]") end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t or not t.Character or not t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if myRoot then
			myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(myRoot.Position, myRoot.Position + t.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector)
			showOutput("Facing same direction as " .. t.DisplayName)
		end
	end}

	commands["behind"] = {desc = "behind [player] - Teleport behind a player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: behind [player]") end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t or not t.Character or not t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local tRoot = t.Character.HumanoidRootPart
		if myRoot then
			myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(tRoot.Position - tRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 5 + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), tRoot.Position)
			showOutput("Behind " .. t.DisplayName)
		end
	end}

	commands["above"] = {desc = "above [player] [height] - Teleport above a player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: above [player] [height]") end
		local height = tonumber(args[#args])
		local nameArgs = height and table.concat(args, " ", 1, #args - 1) or table.concat(args, " ")
		if not height then height = 20 end
		local t = findPlayer(nameArgs)
		if not t or not t.Character or not t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if myRoot then
			local tPos = t.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
			myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(tPos.X, tPos.Y + height, tPos.Z)
			showOutput("Above " .. t.DisplayName .. " (" .. height .. " studs)")
		end
	end}

	commands["attach"] = {desc = "attach [player] - Attach to a player (ride them)", fn = function(args)
		if getgenv()._attachConn then getgenv()._attachConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._attachConn = nil; showOutput("Detached"); return end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: attach [player]") end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		getgenv()._attachConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local tRoot = t.Character and t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if myRoot and tRoot then
					myRoot.CFrame = tRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
				end
			end)
		end)
		showOutput("Attached to " .. t.DisplayName .. " - type attach to stop")
	end}

	commands["detach"] = {desc = "detach - Detach from player", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._attachConn then getgenv()._attachConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._attachConn = nil end
		showOutput("Detached")
	end}
	commands["unattach"] = commands["detach"]

	commands["ride"] = {desc = "ride [player] - Piggyback ride a player", fn = function(args)
		if getgenv()._rideConn then
			if getgenv()._rideTrack then getgenv()._rideTrack:Stop(0.2); getgenv()._rideTrack = nil end
			getgenv()._rideConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._rideConn = nil
			showOutput("Stopped riding")
			return
		end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: ride [player]") end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		local myChar = player.Character
		local myAnimator = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") and myChar:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid"):FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Animator")
		if myAnimator then
			local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://107480602323379"
			getgenv()._rideTrack = myAnimator:LoadAnimation(anim)
			getgenv()._rideTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
			getgenv()._rideTrack.Looped = true
			getgenv()._rideTrack:Play(0.2)
		end
		getgenv()._rideConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local tRoot = t.Character and t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if myRoot and tRoot then
					myRoot.CFrame = tRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 1.5)
				end
			end)
		end)
		showOutput("Riding " .. t.DisplayName .. " - type unride to stop")
	end}

	commands["unride"] = {desc = "unride - Stop riding a player", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._rideTrack then getgenv()._rideTrack:Stop(0.2); getgenv()._rideTrack = nil end
		if getgenv()._rideConn then getgenv()._rideConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._rideConn = nil end
		showOutput("Stopped riding")
	end}

	commands["menu"] = {desc = "menu - Open/close the settings menu", fn = function()
		if toggleSettingsGui then toggleSettingsGui() end
	end}
	commands["config"] = commands["menu"]

	commands["rain"] = {desc = "rain - Toggle web rain effect", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			local existing = workspace:FindFirstChild("SpideyRain")
			if existing then existing:Destroy(); showOutput("Rain OFF"); return end
			local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not myRoot then return end
			local part = Instance.new("Part")
			part.Name = "SpideyRain"
			part.Anchored = true
			part.CanCollide = false
			part.Transparency = 1
			part.Size = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
			part.Parent = workspace
			local att = Instance.new("Attachment", part)
			local p = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", att)
			p.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
			p.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
			p.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.1)
			p.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
			p.Speed = NumberRange.new(30, 50)
			p.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1, 2)
			p.Rate = 100
			p.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(40, 40)
			p.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
			local conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				pcall(function()
					local r = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if r and part.Parent then part.CFrame = CFrame.new(r.Position + Vector3.new(0, 40, 0)) end
				end)
			end)
			part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function() if not part.Parent then conn:Disconnect() end end)
			showOutput("Web rain ON")
		end)
	end}

	commands["ambient"] = {desc = "ambient [r] [g] [b] - Set ambient color", fn = function(args)
		local r = tonumber(args[1]) or 128
		local g = tonumber(args[2]) or 128
		local b = tonumber(args[3]) or 128
		Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b)
		showOutput("Ambient set to " .. r .. "," .. g .. "," .. b)
	end}

		if #args == 0 then return showOutput("Usage: chat [message]") end
		pcall(function()
			game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents"):WaitForChild("SayMessageRequest"):FireServer(table.concat(args, " "), "All")
		end)
		showOutput("Sent: " .. table.concat(args, " "))
	end}

		local z = tonumber(args[3])

	commands["volume"] = {desc = "volume [0-10] - Set game volume", fn = function(args)
		local v = tonumber(args[1])
		if not v then return showOutput("Usage: volume [0-10]") end
		game:GetService("SoundService").AmbientReverb = Enum.ReverbType.NoReverb
		pcall(function()
			for _, s in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if s:IsA("Sound") then s.Volume = s.Volume * (v / 5) end
			end
		end)
		showOutput("Volume adjusted to " .. v)
	end}

	commands["mute"] = {desc = "mute - Mute all sounds", fn = function()
		pcall(function() game:GetService("SoundService").AmbientReverb = Enum.ReverbType.NoReverb end)
		for _, s in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if s:IsA("Sound") then pcall(function() s.Volume = 0 end) end
		end
		showOutput("All sounds muted")
	end}

	commands["highjump"] = {desc = "highjump [power] - Jump with power", fn = function(args)
		local p = tonumber(args[1]) or 200
		local old = humanoid.JumpPower
		humanoid.JumpPower = p; humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
		task.delay(0.5, function() humanoid.JumpPower = old end)
		showOutput("High jump: " .. p)
	end}
	commands["hj"] = commands["highjump"]

		if hrp then hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, p, 0); showOutput("Launched with " .. p .. " power") end
	end}

	commands["yeet"] = {desc = "yeet [power] - Yeet forward", fn = function(args)
		local p = tonumber(args[1]) or 200
		if hrp then hrp.Velocity = hrp.CFrame.LookVector * p + Vector3.new(0, p * 0.4, 0); showOutput("Yeeted with " .. p .. " power") end
	end}

	end}

	commands["lookat"] = {desc = "lookat [player] - Face toward player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: lookat [player]") end
		if args[1]:lower() == "random" then
			local others = {} for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= player and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then table.insert(others, p) end end
			if #others == 0 then return showOutput("No players") end
			local t = others[math.random(1,#others)]
			hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(t.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, hrp.Position.Y, t.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
			return showOutput("Looking at " .. t.DisplayName)
		end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t or not t.Character or not t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(t.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, hrp.Position.Y, t.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
		showOutput("Looking at " .. t.DisplayName)
	end}

	commands["btools"] = {desc = "btools - Give yourself build tools", fn = function()
		pcall(function()
			local t1 = Instance.new("HopperBin", player.Backpack); t1.BinType = Enum.BinType.Hammer; t1.Name = "Delete"
			local t2 = Instance.new("HopperBin", player.Backpack); t2.BinType = Enum.BinType.Clone; t2.Name = "Clone"
			local t3 = Instance.new("HopperBin", player.Backpack); t3.BinType = Enum.BinType.Grab; t3.Name = "Grab"
		end)
		showOutput("BTools added to backpack")
	end}

	commands["particles"] = {desc = "particles - Remove all particles in workspace", fn = function()
		local ct = 0
		for _, d in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("Smoke") or d:IsA("Sparkles") then pcall(function() d:Destroy(); ct = ct + 1 end) end
		end
		showOutput("Removed " .. ct .. " particle effects")
	end}

	commands["sounds"] = {desc = "sounds - List playing sounds", fn = function()
		local list = {}
		for _, s in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if s:IsA("Sound") and s.Playing then table.insert(list, s.Name .. " (Vol:" .. string.format("%.1f", s.Volume) .. ")") end
		end
		if #list == 0 then return showOutput("No sounds playing") end
		showOutput(table.concat(list, "\n"), 5)
	end}

		end

	end}

	commands["guis"] = {desc = "guis - List all ScreenGuis", fn = function()
		local list = {}
		for _, g in pairs(player.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do

		end

	commands["hidegui"] = {desc = "hidegui [name] - Hide a ScreenGui", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: hidegui [name]") end
		local name = table.concat(args, " "):lower()
		for _, g in pairs(player.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
			if g:IsA("ScreenGui") and g.Name:lower():find(name, 1, true) then
				g.Enabled = false; showOutput("Hidden: " .. g.Name); return
			end
		end
		showOutput("GUI not found")
	end}

	commands["showgui"] = {desc = "showgui [name] - Show a ScreenGui", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: showgui [name]") end
		local name = table.concat(args, " "):lower()
		for _, g in pairs(player.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
			if g:IsA("ScreenGui") and g.Name:lower():find(name, 1, true) then
				g.Enabled = true; showOutput("Shown: " .. g.Name); return
			end
		end
		showOutput("GUI not found")
	end}

	commands["hideallgui"] = {desc = "hideallgui - Hide all non-spidey GUIs", fn = function()
		for _, g in pairs(player.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
			if g:IsA("ScreenGui") and not g.Name:find("Spidey") then g.Enabled = false end
		end
		showOutput("All GUIs hidden")
	end}

	commands["showallgui"] = {desc = "showallgui - Show all GUIs", fn = function()
		for _, g in pairs(player.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
			if g:IsA("ScreenGui") then g.Enabled = true end
		end
		showOutput("All GUIs shown")
	end}

	commands["clean"] = {desc = "clean - Remove leftover web/vfx debris", fn = function()
		local ct = 0
		for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			pcall(function()
				if obj.Name == "WebAnchorClump" or obj.Name == "ImpactCrater" or obj.Name == "ImpactDebris" or obj.Name == "SonicBoomRing" then
					obj:Destroy(); ct = ct + 1
				end
			end)
		end
		showOutput("Cleaned " .. ct .. " debris")
	end}

	commands["spawnpoint"] = {desc = "spawnpoint - Set spawn to current position", fn = function()
		if not getgenv()._customSpawn then
			getgenv()._customSpawn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
				local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
				if newHrp and getgenv()._spawnPos then
					newHrp.CFrame = getgenv()._spawnPos
					task.defer(function()
						newHrp.CFrame = getgenv()._spawnPos
					end)
				end
			end)
		end
		getgenv()._spawnPos = hrp.CFrame
		showOutput("Spawn point set here")
	end}

	commands["voidre"] = {desc = "voidre - Void kill yourself and TP back instantly", fn = function()
		local savedPos = hrp.CFrame
		if getgenv()._voidConn then getgenv()._voidConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._voidConn = nil end
		getgenv()._voidConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
			local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
			if newHrp then
				newHrp.CFrame = savedPos
				task.defer(function() newHrp.CFrame = savedPos end)
			end
			getgenv()._voidConn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._voidConn = nil
		end)
		hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -500, 0)
		showOutput("Voiding...")
	end}

	commands["removespawnpoint"] = {desc = "removespawnpoint - Remove custom spawn", fn = function()
		if getgenv()._customSpawn then getgenv()._customSpawn:Disconnect(); getgenv()._customSpawn = nil end
		getgenv()._spawnPos = nil
		showOutput("Custom spawn removed")
	end}
	commands["rsp"] = commands["removespawnpoint"]

	commands["wall"] = {desc = "wall - Create a wall in front of you", fn = function()
		local w = Instance.new("Part")
		w.Size = Vector3.new(15, 12, 1); w.Anchored = true; w.CanCollide = true
		w.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + hrp.CFrame.LookVector * 8)
		w.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; w.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60); w.Transparency = 0.3
		w.Parent = workspace; Debris:AddItem(w, 30)
		showOutput("Wall created (30s)")
	end}

	commands["box"] = {desc = "box - Create a box around you", fn = function()
		local size = 12
		local pos = hrp.Position
		local faces = {
			{Vector3.new(size,size*2,1), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0,size/2,size/2))},
			{Vector3.new(size,size*2,1), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0,size/2,-size/2))},
			{Vector3.new(1,size*2,size), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(size/2,size/2,0))},
			{Vector3.new(1,size*2,size), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(-size/2,size/2,0))},
			{Vector3.new(size,1,size), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0,size,0))},
		}
		for _, f in ipairs(faces) do
			local p = Instance.new("Part"); p.Size = f[1]; p.CFrame = f[2]; p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = true
			p.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField; p.Transparency = 0.5; p.Parent = workspace; Debris:AddItem(p, 30)
		end
		showOutput("Box created (30s)")
	end}

	commands["bridge"] = {desc = "bridge [length] - Build bridge forward", fn = function(args)
		local length = tonumber(args[1]) or 50
		local b = Instance.new("Part")
		b.Size = Vector3.new(6, 0.5, length); b.Anchored = true; b.CanCollide = true
		local fwd = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
		b.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + fwd * (length / 2) - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
		b.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(b.Position, b.Position + fwd) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0)
		b.Material = Enum.Material.Neon; b.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40); b.Transparency = 0.4
		b.Parent = workspace; Debris:AddItem(b, 60)
		showOutput("Bridge (" .. length .. " studs, 60s)")
	end}

	commands["ramp"] = {desc = "ramp [length] - Build ramp forward", fn = function(args)
		local length = tonumber(args[1]) or 30
		local r = Instance.new("Part")
		r.Size = Vector3.new(6, 0.5, length); r.Anchored = true; r.CanCollide = true
		local fwd = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
		local startPos = hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
		r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(startPos, startPos + fwd) * CFrame.new(0, length * 0.25, -length * 0.5) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-30), 0, 0)
		r.Material = Enum.Material.Neon; r.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40); r.Transparency = 0.4
		r.Parent = workspace; Debris:AddItem(r, 60)
		showOutput("Ramp (" .. length .. " studs, 60s)")
	end}

	commands["roof"] = {desc = "roof - Create roof above you", fn = function()
		local r = Instance.new("Part")
		r.Size = Vector3.new(20, 1, 20); r.Anchored = true; r.CanCollide = true
		r.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 15, 0))
		r.Material = Enum.Material.Neon; r.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40); r.Transparency = 0.4
		r.Parent = workspace; Debris:AddItem(r, 30)
		showOutput("Roof created (30s)")
	end}

	commands["deletepart"] = {desc = "deletepart - Delete part you're looking at", fn = function()
		local ray = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, camera.CFrame.LookVector * 500)
		if ray and ray.Instance then
			local name = ray.Instance.Name
			ray.Instance:Destroy()
			showOutput("Deleted: " .. name)
		else
			showOutput("No part found")
		end
	end}
	commands["dpart"] = commands["deletepart"]

	commands["copypart"] = {desc = "copypart - Clone part you're looking at", fn = function()
		local ray = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, camera.CFrame.LookVector * 500)
		if ray and ray.Instance then
			local c = ray.Instance:Clone(); c.Parent = workspace; c.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + hrp.CFrame.LookVector * 10
			Debris:AddItem(c, 60)
			showOutput("Cloned: " .. c.Name .. " (60s)")
		else
			showOutput("No part found")
		end
	end}

	commands["partinfo"] = {desc = "partinfo - Info on part you're looking at", fn = function()
		local ray = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, camera.CFrame.LookVector * 500)
		if ray and ray.Instance then
			local p = ray.Instance
			showOutput(p.Name .. " | " .. p.ClassName .. " | Size: " .. tostring(p.Size) .. " | Material: " .. tostring(p.Material), 5)
		else
			showOutput("No part found")
		end
	end}

	commands["repeat"] = {desc = "repeat [n] [cmd] - Repeat a command N times", fn = function(args)
		local n = tonumber(args[1])
		if not n or not args[2] then return showOutput("Usage: repeat [count] [command]") end
		n = math.clamp(n, 1, 20)
		local cmdStr = table.concat(args, " ", 2)
		for i = 1, n do
			task.delay((i - 1) * 0.5, function() executeCmd(cmdStr) end)
		end
		showOutput("Repeating '" .. cmdStr .. "' x" .. n)
	end}

	commands["delay"] = {desc = "delay [sec] [cmd] - Run command after delay", fn = function(args)
		local sec = tonumber(args[1])
		if not sec or not args[2] then return showOutput("Usage: delay [seconds] [command]") end
		local cmdStr = table.concat(args, " ", 2)
		showOutput("Running '" .. cmdStr .. "' in " .. sec .. "s")
		task.delay(sec, function() executeCmd(cmdStr) end)
	end}

	commands["track"] = {desc = "track [player] - Track a player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: track [player]") end
		if args[1]:lower() == "random" then
			local others = {} for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= player then table.insert(others, p) end end
			if #others == 0 then return showOutput("No players") end
			local t = others[math.random(1,#others)]
			TrackedPlayers[t.Name] = true; saveConfig()
			return showOutput("Tracking " .. t.DisplayName)
		end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		TrackedPlayers[t.Name] = true; saveConfig()
		showOutput("Tracking " .. t.DisplayName)
	end}

	commands["untrack"] = {desc = "untrack [player] - Stop tracking a player", fn = function(args)
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: untrack [player]") end
		if args[1]:lower() == "all" then
			for k in pairs(TrackedPlayers) do TrackedPlayers[k] = nil end
			saveConfig()
			return showOutput("Untracked all players")
		end
		local t = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not t then
			local name = table.concat(args, " ")
			for tracked, _ in pairs(TrackedPlayers) do
				if tracked:lower():find(name:lower(), 1, true) then
					TrackedPlayers[tracked] = nil; saveConfig()
					return showOutput("Untracked " .. tracked)
				end
			end
			return showOutput("Player not found")
		end
		TrackedPlayers[t.Name] = nil; saveConfig()
		showOutput("Untracked " .. t.DisplayName)
	end}

	commands["trackall"] = {desc = "trackall - Track all players", fn = function()
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= player then TrackedPlayers[p.Name] = true end
		end
		saveConfig()
		showOutput("Tracking all players")
	end}

	commands["untrackall"] = {desc = "untrackall - Untrack all players", fn = function()
		for k in pairs(TrackedPlayers) do TrackedPlayers[k] = nil end
		saveConfig()
		showOutput("Untracked all players")
	end}

	commands["tracked"] = {desc = "tracked - List tracked players", fn = function()
		local list = {}
		for name, _ in pairs(TrackedPlayers) do table.insert(list, name) end
		if #list == 0 then return showOutput("Not tracking anyone") end
		showOutput("Tracking: " .. table.concat(list, ", "))
	end}

	commands["bring"] = {desc = "bring [player] - Bring a script user to you (admin)", fn = function(args)
		if player.UserId ~= ADMIN_USER_ID then return showOutput("Admin only") end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: bring [player]") end
		local target = findPlayer(table.concat(args, " "))
		if not target then return showOutput("Player not found") end
		if not KnownSpidermen[target.UserId] then return showOutput(target.DisplayName .. " is not using the script") end
		pcall(function()
			game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents"):WaitForChild("SayMessageRequest"):FireServer(BRING_PREFIX .. target.UserId, "All")
		end)
		showOutput("Bringing " .. target.DisplayName .. "...")
	end}

	commands["whitelist"] = {desc = "whitelist [player/id] - Allow a player to use the script (owner)", fn = function(args)
		if not OWNER_USER_IDS[player.UserId] then return showOutput("Owner only") end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: whitelist [player or userid]") end
		local input = table.concat(args, " ")
		local uid = tonumber(input)
		local name = input
		if not uid then
			local t = findPlayer(input)
			if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
			uid = t.UserId
			name = t.DisplayName
		end
		WHITELISTED_IDS[uid] = true
		BLACKLISTED_IDS[uid] = nil
		saveAccess()
		showOutput("Whitelisted " .. name .. " (" .. uid .. ")")
	end}

	commands["unwhitelist"] = {desc = "unwhitelist [player/id] - Remove a player from whitelist (owner)", fn = function(args)
		if not OWNER_USER_IDS[player.UserId] then return showOutput("Owner only") end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: unwhitelist [player or userid]") end
		local input = table.concat(args, " ")
		local uid = tonumber(input)
		local name = input
		if not uid then
			local t = findPlayer(input)
			if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
			uid = t.UserId
			name = t.DisplayName
		end
		if OWNER_USER_IDS[uid] then return showOutput("Cannot remove owner") end
		WHITELISTED_IDS[uid] = nil
		saveAccess()
		showOutput("Removed " .. name .. " (" .. uid .. ") from whitelist")
	end}

	commands["blacklist"] = {desc = "blacklist [player/id] - Block a player from using the script (owner)", fn = function(args)
		if not OWNER_USER_IDS[player.UserId] then return showOutput("Owner only") end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: blacklist [player or userid]") end
		local input = table.concat(args, " ")
		local uid = tonumber(input)
		local name = input
		if not uid then
			local t = findPlayer(input)
			if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
			uid = t.UserId
			name = t.DisplayName
		end
		if OWNER_USER_IDS[uid] then return showOutput("Cannot blacklist owner") end
		BLACKLISTED_IDS[uid] = true
		WHITELISTED_IDS[uid] = nil
		saveAccess()
		showOutput("Blacklisted " .. name .. " (" .. uid .. ")")
	end}

	commands["unblacklist"] = {desc = "unblacklist [player/id] - Remove a player from blacklist (owner)", fn = function(args)
		if not OWNER_USER_IDS[player.UserId] then return showOutput("Owner only") end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Usage: unblacklist [player or userid]") end
		local input = table.concat(args, " ")
		local uid = tonumber(input)
		local name = input
		if not uid then
			local t = findPlayer(input)
			if not t then return showOutput("Player not found") end
			uid = t.UserId
			name = t.DisplayName
		end
		BLACKLISTED_IDS[uid] = nil
		saveAccess()
		showOutput("Unblacklisted " .. name .. " (" .. uid .. ")")
	end}

	commands["accessmode"] = {desc = "accessmode [open/whitelist/blacklist] - Set script access mode (owner)", fn = function(args)
		if not OWNER_USER_IDS[player.UserId] then return showOutput("Owner only") end
		if not args[1] then return showOutput("Current mode: " .. SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE .. " | Usage: accessmode [open/whitelist/blacklist]") end
		local mode = args[1]:lower()
		if mode ~= "open" and mode ~= "whitelist" and mode ~= "blacklist" then
			return showOutput("Invalid mode. Use: open, whitelist, or blacklist")
		end
		SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE = mode
		saveAccess()
		showOutput("Access mode set to: " .. mode)
	end}

	commands["listaccess"] = {desc = "listaccess - Show whitelist and blacklist (owner)", fn = function()
		if not OWNER_USER_IDS[player.UserId] then return showOutput("Owner only") end
		local wl, bl = {}, {}
		for id, _ in pairs(WHITELISTED_IDS) do table.insert(wl, tostring(id)) end
		for id, _ in pairs(BLACKLISTED_IDS) do table.insert(bl, tostring(id)) end
		showOutput("Mode: " .. SCRIPT_ACCESS_MODE .. " | WL: " .. (#wl > 0 and table.concat(wl, ", ") or "none") .. " | BL: " .. (#bl > 0 and table.concat(bl, ", ") or "none"))
	end}

	local function executeCmd(input)
		local parts = {}
		for word in input:gmatch("%S+") do table.insert(parts, word) end
		if #parts == 0 then return end
		local cmdName = parts[1]:lower()
		local args = {}
		for i = 2, #parts do table.insert(args, parts[i]) end
		local cmd = commands[cmdName]
		if cmd then
			pcall(function() cmd.fn(args) end)
		else
			showOutput("Unknown command: " .. cmdName .. " (type 'cmds' for list)")
		end
	end

	getgenv()._toggleCmdBar = function()
		cmdBarOpen = not cmdBarOpen
		if cmdBarOpen then
			cmdBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -60)
			cmdBar.Visible = true
			cmdInput.Text = ""
			cmdInput:CaptureFocus()
			task.defer(function()
				cmdInput.Text = cmdInput.Text:gsub(";", "")
			end)
			task.delay(0.05, function()
				cmdInput.Text = cmdInput.Text:gsub(";", "")
			end)
		else
			cmdInput:ReleaseFocus()
			TweenService:Create(cmdBar, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 50)}):Play()
			task.delay(0.2, function() cmdBar.Visible = false end)
		end
	end

	cmdInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
		if enterPressed and cmdInput.Text ~= "" then
			executeCmd(cmdInput.Text)
		end
		cmdInput.Text = ""
		cmdInput:ReleaseFocus()
		cmdBarOpen = false
		TweenService:Create(cmdBar, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 50)}):Play()
		task.delay(0.2, function() cmdBar.Visible = false end)
	end)

	table.insert(connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		if cmdBarFlying and hrp and humanoid and isEquipped then
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6, 1e6, 1e6)
			local move = Vector3.zero
			local cf = camera.CFrame
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then move = move + cf.LookVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then move = move - cf.LookVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then move = move - cf.RightVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then move = move + cf.RightVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then move = move + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then move = move - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
			if move.Magnitude > 0 then
				bv.Velocity = move.Unit * cmdBarFlySpeed
			else
				bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		end
		if cmdBarNoclip and char then
			for _, part in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = false end
			end
		end
	end))
end)

getgenv()._forceResetSkipConfirm = false

getgenv()._doForceReset = function()
	pcall(function()
		local currentChar = player.Character
		local currentHrp = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local currentHum = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		local savedPos = (currentHrp or hrp) and (currentHrp or hrp).CFrame
		for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			pcall(function()
				if obj.Name == "WebAnchorClump" or obj.Name == "ImpactCrater" or obj.Name == "ImpactDebris" or obj.Name == "SonicBoomRing" or obj.Name == "SpideyChargeVFX" or obj.Name == "SpideyLungeBurst" then
					obj:Destroy()
				end
			end)
		end
		if currentChar then
			for _, obj in pairs(currentChar:GetDescendants()) do
				pcall(function()
					if obj.Name == "SpideyChargeAtt" or obj.Name == "SpideyLungeBurst" then obj:Destroy() end
				end)
			end
		end
		hideAllBeams()
		local humToKill = currentHum or humanoid
		if humToKill then humToKill.Health = 0 end
		task.spawn(function()
			local newChar = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
			local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
			local newHum = newChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
			if newHrp and savedPos then
				task.wait(0.3)
				newHrp.CFrame = savedPos
			end
			if newHum then
				newHum.PlatformStand = false
				newHum.AutoRotate = true
				newHum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			end
		end)
	end)
end

		local existing = player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SpideyResetConfirm")
		if existing then existing:Destroy() end

		local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

		dimBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		dimBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		dimBg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5

		title2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30)
		title2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		title2.Text = "Force Reset?"
		title2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		title2.TextSize = 15
		title2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack

		local yesBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", box)
		yesBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 32)
		yesBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 62)
		yesBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		yesBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
		yesBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		yesBtn.Text = "Yes, Reset"
		yesBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		yesBtn.TextSize = 13
		yesBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		Instance.new("UICorner", yesBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
		yesBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			gui:Destroy()
			getgenv()._doForceReset()
		end)

		local checkRow = Instance.new("Frame", box)
		checkRow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 22)
		checkRow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 102)
		checkRow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		checkRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1

		local checkBox = Instance.new("TextButton", checkRow)
		checkBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
		checkBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
		checkBox.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		checkBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
		checkBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
		checkBox.Text = ""
		checkBox.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		checkBox.TextSize = 11
		checkBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		Instance.new("UICorner", checkBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
		local cbStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", checkBox)
		cbStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 70)
		cbStroke.Thickness = 1

		local checkLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", checkRow)
		checkLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0)
		checkLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 0)
		checkLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		checkLabel.Text = "Don't show again"
		checkLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)
		checkLabel.TextSize = 10
		checkLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		checkLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

		local checked = false
		checkBox.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			checked = not checked
			checkBox.Text = checked and "✓" or ""
			checkBox.BackgroundColor3 = checked and Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
			getgenv()._forceResetSkipConfirm = checked
		end)

		box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 0)
		TweenService:Create(box, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 145)}):Play()
		dimBg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		TweenService:Create(dimBg, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5}):Play()
	end)
end

local function setup()
	char = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
	humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")
	hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	rightHand = char:FindFirstChild("RightHand") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
	leftHand = char:FindFirstChild("LeftHand") or char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
	if not rightHand then rightHand = char:WaitForChild("RightHand", 2) or char:WaitForChild("Right Arm", 2) end
	if not leftHand then leftHand = char:WaitForChild("LeftHand", 2) or char:WaitForChild("Left Arm", 2) end
	if hrp:FindFirstChild("SwingVelocity") then hrp.SwingVelocity:Destroy() end
	bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
	bv.Name = "SwingVelocity"
	bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
	bv.P = 3000
	bv.Parent = hrp
	humanoid.PlatformStand = false
	hrp.CanCollide = true
	if hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro") then hrp.WallGyro:Destroy() end
	local wg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	wg.Name = "WallGyro"
	wg.MaxTorque = Vector3.zero
	wg.P = 30000
	wg.D = 1000
	wg.Parent = hrp
	wallGyro = wg
	glideTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animGlide)
	glideTrack.Looped = true
	glideTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
	local animCrawl = Instance.new("Animation"); animCrawl.AnimationId = CRAWL_ANIM_ID
	crawlTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animCrawl); crawlTrack.Looped = true; crawlTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3
	local animVault = Instance.new("Animation"); animVault.AnimationId = VAULT_ANIM_ID
	vaultTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animVault); vaultTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
	local animMantle = Instance.new("Animation"); animMantle.AnimationId = MANTLE_ANIM_ID
	mantleTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animMantle); mantleTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
	local animRoll = Instance.new("Animation"); animRoll.AnimationId = ROLL_ANIM_ID
	rollTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animRoll); rollTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
	local animRun = Instance.new("Animation"); animRun.AnimationId = RUN_ANIM_ID
	runTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animRun); runTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement; runTrack.Looped = true
	local animBackDash = Instance.new("Animation"); animBackDash.AnimationId = BACK_DASH_ID
	backDashTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animBackDash); backDashTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4

	if rightHand then
		handAttachmentR = rightHand:FindFirstChild("WebHandAttR") or Instance.new("Attachment", rightHand)
		handAttachmentR.Name = "WebHandAttR"
		handAttachmentR.Position = Vector3.new(0,-0.1,0)
	end
	if leftHand then
		handAttachmentL = leftHand:FindFirstChild("WebHandAttL") or Instance.new("Attachment", leftHand)
		handAttachmentL.Name = "WebHandAttL"
		handAttachmentL.Position = Vector3.new(0,-0.1,0)
	else
		handAttachmentL = handAttachmentR
	end

	setupWebBeams(workspace.Terrain)
	if rightHand then handTrailR = createTrail(rightHand) end
	if leftHand then handTrailL = createTrail(leftHand) end
	for i = 1, 5 do
		local s = Instance.new("Sound")
		s.SoundId = SWING_SOUND_ID
		s.Volume = 0.4
		s.RollOffMaxDistance = 100
		s.RollOffMinDistance = 10
		s.RollOffMode = Enum.RollOffMode.Linear
		s:SetAttribute("BasePitch", 1)
		s.Parent = hrp
		table.insert(webSoundsPool, s)
	end
	if Lighting:FindFirstChild("SpideyCinematicCC") then Lighting.SpideyCinematicCC:Destroy() end
	ccCinematic = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
	ccCinematic.Name = "SpideyCinematicCC"
	ccCinematic.Parent = Lighting

	table.insert(connections, humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
		saveConfig()
		if settingsOpen and mainFrame then
			settingsOpen = false
			mainFrame.Visible = false
		end
		isCinematic = false
		slowMoAlpha = 0
		workspace.Gravity = 196.2
		if ccCinematic then ccCinematic:Destroy(); ccCinematic = nil end
		if ccSonar then ccSonar:Destroy(); ccSonar = nil end
		isEquipped = false
		StopAllAnimations()
		hideAllBeams()
		for beam, conn in pairs(webLaunchConnections) do
			if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
			if beam then beam.Enabled = false end
		end
		webLaunchConnections = {}
		camera.FieldOfView = 70
		pcall(function() humanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero end)
		shakeIntensity = 0
		cameraRoll = 0
		fovKick = 0
		currentMomentum = 0
		isZipping = false
		swinging = false
		isDashing = false
		gliding = false
		isSlingshotting = false
		isHoldingMouse = false
		isLooping = false
		isChargingJump = false
		isLaunching = false
		isVaulting = false
		isMantling = false
		isTricking = false
		isEmoting = false
		isHangingEmote = false
		isWallRunning = false
		stuckToWall = false
		wallDebounce = false
		slideLocked = false
		idlePlaying = false
		fallingAnimPlaying = false
		startFallPlaying = false
		stepFallPlaying = false
		runPlaying = false
		if handTrailR then handTrailR.Enabled = false end
		if handTrailL then handTrailL.Enabled = false end
		if photoModeActive then
			photoModeActive = false
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
		end
		pcall(function()
			for _, name in pairs({"SpiderWebBeamR","SpiderWebBeamL","WebWorldAttR","WebWorldAttL","WebMidAttR","WebMidAttL","WebLoopBeamR","WebLoopBeamL"}) do
				local obj = workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChild(name)
				if obj then obj:Destroy() end
			end
		end)
	end))
end

local function createTool()
	if player.Backpack:FindFirstChild("SpiderMan") then player.Backpack.SpiderMan:Destroy() end
	if char:FindFirstChild("SpiderMan") then char.SpiderMan:Destroy() end
	tool = Instance.new("Tool")
	tool.Name = "SpiderMan"
	tool.RequiresHandle = false
	tool.Parent = player.Backpack
end

local function isNearGround()
	local params = getIgnoreParams()
	local result = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0,-GLIDE_GROUND_CUTOFF,0), params)
	return result ~= nil
end

local function handleLedgeVault()
	if isVaulting then return end
	isVaulting = true
	local vaultNormal = smoothWallNormal
	local preVaultMomentum = currentMomentum
	stuckToWall = false
	wallClimbEnabled = false
	wallDebounce = true
	wallAttachDebounceTime = tick() + 1.5
	StopAllAnimations()
	humanoid.PlatformStand = true
	hrp.Anchored = true
	bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
	hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
	hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
	ensureCrawlTrack()
	if vaultTrack then vaultTrack:Play() end
	local forwardDir = -vaultNormal
	local flatForward = Vector3.new(forwardDir.X, 0, forwardDir.Z).Unit
	local upOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4.5, 0)
	local targetUpPos = hrp.Position + upOffset + (flatForward * 2)
	local targetForwardPos = hrp.Position + upOffset + (flatForward * 5)
	local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	activeWebHand = "Right"
	playInstantWebSound()
	if worldAttR then worldAttR.WorldPosition = targetUpPos + (flatForward * 5) end
	if webMidAttR then webMidAttR.WorldPosition = targetUpPos + (flatForward * 5) end
	if webBeamR then webBeamR.Enabled = true; webBeamR.CurveSize0 = 0; webBeamR.CurveSize1 = 0 end
	if webLoopBeamR then webLoopBeamR.Enabled = false end
	local moveUp = TweenService:Create(hrp, tweenInfo, {CFrame = hrp.CFrame + upOffset + (flatForward * 1.5)})
	moveUp:Play()
	moveUp.Completed:Wait()
	if not isEquipped then 
		if hrp then hrp.Anchored = false end
		humanoid.PlatformStand = false
		hideAllBeams()
		isVaulting = false
		wallDebounce = false
		return 
	end
	if webBeamR then webBeamR.Enabled = false end
	activeWebHand = "Left"
	playInstantWebSound()
	if worldAttL then worldAttL.WorldPosition = targetForwardPos + (flatForward * 15) end
	if webMidAttL then webMidAttL.WorldPosition = targetForwardPos + (flatForward * 15) end
	if webBeamL then webBeamL.Enabled = true; webBeamL.CurveSize0 = 0; webBeamL.CurveSize1 = 0 end
	if webLoopBeamL then webLoopBeamL.Enabled = false end
	local endCF = CFrame.new(targetForwardPos, targetForwardPos + flatForward)
	local moveForward = TweenService:Create(hrp, tweenInfo, {CFrame = endCF})
	moveForward:Play()
	moveForward.Completed:Wait()
	if not isEquipped then 
		if hrp then hrp.Anchored = false end
		humanoid.PlatformStand = false
		hideAllBeams()
		isVaulting = false
		wallDebounce = false
		return 
	end
	stopCrawlTrack()
	if vaultTrack then vaultTrack:Stop() end
	hideAllBeams()
	hrp.Anchored = false
	humanoid.PlatformStand = false
	humanoid.AutoRotate = true
	local wg = hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro")
	if wg then wg.MaxTorque = Vector3.zero end
	humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
	local lungeSpeed = (25 + (preVaultMomentum * 0.4)) * getMod()
	currentMomentum = lungeSpeed
	hrp.Velocity = (flatForward * lungeSpeed) + Vector3.new(0, 10 * getMod(), 0)
	task.delay(0.2, function()
		isVaulting = false
		wallDebounce = false
	end)
end

local function handleSlingshot()
	if not isEquipped or isSlingshotting or hrp.Velocity.Magnitude > 5 then return end
	isSlingshotting = true
	swinging, stuckToWall, gliding, isDashing, isZipping = false, false, false, false, false
	StopAllAnimations()
	local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
	anim.AnimationId = SLINGSHOT_PERCH_ID
	slingshotTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
	slingshotTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
	slingshotTrack:Play(0.1)
	slingshotStartCF = hrp.CFrame
	hrp.Anchored = true
	bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
	bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
	hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
	humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
	local chargeSound = playSound(SLINGSHOT_CHARGE_ID, 2, 1)

	task.wait(2)
	hrp.Anchored = false
	slingshotStartCF = nil
	if not isEquipped then
		isSlingshotting = false
		bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
		if chargeSound then chargeSound:Stop() end
		return
	end
	if slingshotTrack then slingshotTrack:Stop(0.1) end
	isSlingshotting = false
	humanoid.WalkSpeed = 30
	playSound(SLINGSHOT_BOOM_ID, 1, 1)
	camera.FieldOfView = 120
	bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
	hrp.Velocity = (camera.CFrame.LookVector * ((SLINGSHOT_FORCE * getMod()) + currentMomentum))
	applyShake(4.5)
end

local function handleZip()
	if isZipping or swinging or stuckToWall then return end
	local rayParams = getIgnoreParams()
	local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
	local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
	local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, look * ZIP_RANGE, rayParams)
	if result then
		activeWebHand = "Right"
		emitWebBurst(activeWebHand)
		local hitPos = result.Position
		local hitNormal = result.Normal
		zipTargetPoint = hitPos + (hitNormal * 3) + Vector3.new(0,2,0)
		currentWebNormal = hitNormal
		isZipping = true
		swinging, gliding, isDashing = false, false, false
		StopAllAnimations()

		local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
		anim.AnimationId = ZIP_ANIM_ID
		zipTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
		zipTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
		zipTrack:Play(0.1)
		zipTrack:AdjustSpeed(2)

		task.spawn(function()
			while isZipping do
				task.wait(0.8)
				if isZipping and zipTrack and zipTrack.IsPlaying then
					zipTrack.TimePosition = 0
				end
			end
		end)

		playSound(ZIP_SOUND_ID, 1.2, 1.1)


		if worldAttR then worldAttR.WorldPosition = hitPos end
		if webMidAttR then webMidAttR.WorldPosition = hitPos end
		if worldAttL then worldAttL.WorldPosition = hitPos end
		if webMidAttL then webMidAttL.WorldPosition = hitPos end

		if webBeamR then webBeamR.Enabled = true; webBeamR.CurveSize0 = 0; webBeamR.CurveSize1 = 0 end
		if webBeamL then webBeamL.Enabled = true; webBeamL.CurveSize0 = 0; webBeamL.CurveSize1 = 0 end
		if webLoopBeamR then webLoopBeamR.Enabled = false end
		if webLoopBeamL then webLoopBeamL.Enabled = false end

		bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
		fovKick = 10
		task.spawn(function()
			local startTime = tick()
			local function getZipSpeed()
				local speedScale = isCinematic and 0.3 or 1.0
				return ((ZIP_SPEED * getMod()) + (currentMomentum * 0.5)) * speedScale
			end
			while isZipping and (tick() - startTime) < 0.8 do
				if not isEquipped then break end
				local dist = (zipTargetPoint - hrp.Position).Magnitude
				local dir = (zipTargetPoint - hrp.Position).Unit
				bv.Velocity = dir * getZipSpeed()

				if worldAttR then worldAttR.WorldPosition = hitPos end
				if webMidAttR then webMidAttR.WorldPosition = hitPos end
				if worldAttL then worldAttL.WorldPosition = hitPos end
				if webMidAttL then webMidAttL.WorldPosition = hitPos end
				if webBeamR then webBeamR.CurveSize0 = 0; webBeamR.CurveSize1 = 0 end
				if webBeamL then webBeamL.CurveSize0 = 0; webBeamL.CurveSize1 = 0 end

				if dist < 4 then
					isZipping = false
					bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
					hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
					local zipHand = (activeWebHand == "Right") and rightHand or leftHand
					spawnWebResidue(hitPos, zipHand and zipHand.Position or hrp.Position, hitNormal)
					break
				end
				RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
			end
			isZipping = false
			if not isDashing and not isSlingshotting then bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero end
			hideAllBeams()
			if zipTrack then zipTrack:Stop(0.1) end
		end)
	end
end

local function handleDash()
	if isDashing or stuckToWall or isEmoting then return end
	if tick() - lastDashTime < 2 then return end
	lastDashTime = tick()
	activeWebHand = "Right"
	emitWebBurst("Right")
	emitWebBurst("Left")
	playSound(DASH_SOUND_ID, 2.5, 1, 0)

	isDashing = true
	swinging = false
	isZipping = false
	isLaunching = true
	if swingTrack then swingTrack:Stop(0.1) end
	camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(math.random(-4,4)), math.rad(math.random(-4,4)), math.rad(math.random(-2,2)))
	local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
	local rightDir = camera.CFrame.RightVector
	local origin = hrp.Position

	local webDist = 60
	local spreadAngle = 0.6
	local webDirR = (lookDir + rightDir * spreadAngle + Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0)).Unit
	local webDirL = (lookDir - rightDir * spreadAngle + Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0)).Unit
	local skyR = origin + webDirR * webDist
	local skyL = origin + webDirL * webDist

	if worldAttR then worldAttR.WorldPosition = skyR end
	if webMidAttR then webMidAttR.WorldPosition = skyR end
	if worldAttL then worldAttL.WorldPosition = skyL end
	if webMidAttL then webMidAttL.WorldPosition = skyL end

	if webBeamR then webBeamR.Enabled = true; webBeamR.CurveSize0 = 0; webBeamR.CurveSize1 = 0 end
	if webBeamL then webBeamL.Enabled = true; webBeamL.CurveSize0 = 0; webBeamL.CurveSize1 = 0 end
	if webLoopBeamR then webLoopBeamR.Enabled = false end
	if webLoopBeamL then webLoopBeamL.Enabled = false end

	local flagAnim = Instance.new("Animation")
	flagAnim.AnimationId = FLAG_DOUBLE_DASH
	local flagTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(flagAnim)
	flagTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
	flagTrack:Play(0, 0.001, 0)
	if fallTrack then fallTrack:Stop(0.1) end
	if startFallTrack then startFallTrack:Stop(0.1) end
	if glideTrack then glideTrack:Play(0.1) end
	if trickTrack then trickTrack:Stop(0.1) end
	isTricking = false
	humanoid.PlatformStand = true
	bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
	currentMomentum = math.min(currentMomentum + (80 * getMod()), 250 * getMod())
	local forwardPush = lookDir * ((DASH_FORCE * 1.4 * getMod()) + currentMomentum)
	bv.Velocity = forwardPush + Vector3.new(0, 35 * getMod(), 0)
	task.delay(0.4, function()
		isLaunching = false
		isDashing = false
		humanoid.PlatformStand = false
		if not isEquipped then return end
		if not gliding then
			humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
			if glideTrack then glideTrack:Stop(0.2) end
		end
		bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
		hideAllBeams()
		if flagTrack then flagTrack:Stop() end
	end)
end

local function attemptNewSwing(isAuto)
	local startPos = camera.CFrame.Position
	local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
	local rayParams = getIgnoreParams()
	local result

	if manualModeEnabled then
		result = workspace:Raycast(startPos, lookDir * MAX_SWING_DIST, rayParams)
	else
		local aimDirection = (lookDir + Vector3.new(0,0.4,0.0)).Unit * MAX_SWING_DIST
		result = workspace:Spherecast(startPos, AUTO_AIM_RADIUS, aimDirection, rayParams)
		local function isWeakPoint(r)
			if not r or not r.Instance then return false end
			if r.Normal.Y >= 0.7 or r.Position.Y <= (hrp.Position.Y - 5) then return true end
			local d = (r.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
			if d < 15 and r.Position.Y < (hrp.Position.Y + 5) then return true end
			return false
		end
		if isWeakPoint(result) then
			local higherAim = (aimDirection + Vector3.new(0,0.4,0)).Unit * MAX_SWING_DIST
			result = workspace:Spherecast(startPos + Vector3.new(0,5,0), AUTO_AIM_RADIUS, higherAim, rayParams)
			if isWeakPoint(result) then
				local steepAim = (lookDir + Vector3.new(0,1.0,0)).Unit * MAX_SWING_DIST
				result = workspace:Spherecast(startPos + Vector3.new(0,5,0), AUTO_AIM_RADIUS, steepAim, rayParams)
			end
		end
	end

	if result and result.Instance and result.Normal.Y < 0.7 and result.Position.Y > (hrp.Position.Y - 5) then
		currentWebPoint = result.Position
		currentWebNormal = result.Normal
		stuckToWall = false
		stopCrawlTrack()
		humanoid.PlatformStand = false
		humanoid.AutoRotate = true
		if xTrack then xTrack:Stop(0.1) end
		if emoteTrack then emoteTrack:Stop(0.1); isEmoting = false; isHangingEmote = false end
		if zipTrack then zipTrack:Stop(0.1) end
		if trickTrack then trickTrack:Stop(0.1) end
		isZipping = false
		isTricking = false
		if activeWebHand == "Right" then activeWebHand = "Left" else activeWebHand = "Right" end
		fovKick = 5
		gliding = false
		slideLocked = false
		
		if swingReleaseTrack then swingReleaseTrack:Stop(0.2) end
		if not swinging then
			StopAllAnimations()
			swinging = true
			totalSwings = totalSwings + 1
			local a = Instance.new("Animation")

			local isAltSwing = math.random(1, 4) == 1
			
			if isAltSwing then
				a.AnimationId = SWING_ANIM_ID_2
			else
				a.AnimationId = SWING_ANIM_ID
			end
			
			swingTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(a)
			swingTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
			swingTrack:Play(0.3)
			
			local targetSpeed = a.AnimationId == SWING_ANIM_ID_2 and 1.5 or 2.0
			swingTrack:AdjustSpeed(targetSpeed)
			
			if a.AnimationId == SWING_ANIM_ID_2 then
				swingTrack.TimePosition = 0.5
			else
				swingTrack.TimePosition = 1.0 
			end
		end
		
		if worldAttR then worldAttR.WorldPosition = currentWebPoint end
		if worldAttL then worldAttL.WorldPosition = currentWebPoint end
		local mainBeam = activeWebHand == "Right" and webBeamR or webBeamL
		local loopBeam = activeWebHand == "Right" and webLoopBeamR or webLoopBeamL
		local midAtt = activeWebHand == "Right" and webMidAttR or webMidAttL
		local handAtt = activeWebHand == "Right" and handAttachmentR or handAttachmentL
		if mainBeam and loopBeam and midAtt and handAtt then
			launchWebCoil(mainBeam, loopBeam, midAtt, handAtt, currentWebPoint)
		end
		return true
	end
	return false
end

table.insert(connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
	if photoModeActive then
		pcall(function()
			local camSpeed = 50 * dt
			local cf = camera.CFrame
			UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
			local delta = UIS:GetMouseDelta()
			local sensitivity = 0.3
			local rx = -delta.Y * sensitivity * dt * 60
			local ry = -delta.X * sensitivity * dt * 60
			cf = cf * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx), math.rad(ry), 0)
			local move = Vector3.zero
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then move = move + cf.LookVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then move = move - cf.LookVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then move = move - cf.RightVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then move = move + cf.RightVector end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then move = move + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then move = move - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then camSpeed = camSpeed * 3 end
			if move.Magnitude > 0 then
				cf = cf + (move.Unit * camSpeed)
			end
			camera.CFrame = cf
		end)
		return
	end
	local currentlyEquipped = char and char:FindFirstChild("SpiderMan") ~= nil
	if currentlyEquipped and not isEquipped then
		isEquipped = true
