		if bv then bv.Parent = hrp end
		local wg = hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro") or wallGyro
		if wg then wg.Parent = hrp end
		settingsWasOpen = false
		if not workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChild("SpiderWebBeamR") then
			pcall(function() setupWebBeams(workspace.Terrain); applyWebColor() end)
		end
	elseif not currentlyEquipped and isEquipped then
		if settingsOpen and mainFrame then
			settingsWasOpen = true
			pcall(toggleSettingsGui)
		end
		isEquipped = false
		if photoModeActive then
			photoModeActive = false
			if hrp then hrp.Anchored = false end
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
		end
		StopAllAnimations()
		if vaultTrack then vaultTrack:Stop() end
		humanoid.PlatformStand = false
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
		wallClimbEnabled = false
		swinging, stuckToWall, isSlingshotting, gliding, isDashing, isChargingJump, isZipping, isVaulting = false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false
		isHoldingMouse, isLooping, isEmoting, isHangingEmote, isTricking, isMantling = false, false, false, false, false, false
		isLaunching = false
		idlePlaying = false
		fallingAnimPlaying = false
		startFallPlaying = false
		isWallRunning = false
		slideLocked = false
		walkedOffLedge = false
		stepFallPlaying = false
		isSpeedBoosted = false
		runPlaying = false
		goingUp = false
		goingDown = false
		currentMomentum = 0
		fallTime = 0
		lastVerticalVelocity = 0
		cameraRoll = 0
		fovKick = 0
		shakeIntensity = 0
		if hrp then hrp.Anchored = false; hrp.CanCollide = true end
		slingshotStartCF = nil
		stopCrawlTrack()
		if bv then bv.Parent = nil end
		local wg = hrp and hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro") or wallGyro
		if wg then wg.Parent = nil end
		pcall(function() humanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero end)
		camera.FieldOfView = 70
		humanoid.AutoRotate = true
		isCinematic, slowMoAlpha = false, 0
		workspace.Gravity = 196.2
		if ccCinematic then ccCinematic.Saturation = 0; ccCinematic.Contrast = 0 end
		if ccSonar then ccSonar:Destroy(); ccSonar = nil end
		hideAllBeams()
		for beam, conn in pairs(webLaunchConnections) do
			if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
			if beam then beam.Enabled = false end
		end
		webLaunchConnections = {}
		if handTrailR then handTrailR.Enabled = false end
		if handTrailL then handTrailL.Enabled = false end
		if hangBeam then hangBeam:Destroy(); hangBeam = nil end
		if hangWorldAtt then hangWorldAtt:Destroy(); hangWorldAtt = nil end
		if hangMidAtt then hangMidAtt:Destroy(); hangMidAtt = nil end
		pcall(function()
			for _, name in pairs({"SpiderWebBeamR","SpiderWebBeamL","WebWorldAttR","WebWorldAttL","WebMidAttR","WebMidAttL","WebLoopBeamR","WebLoopBeamL"}) do
				local obj = workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChild(name)
				if obj then obj:Destroy() end
			end
		end)
		camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
	end

	UpdatePeers(dt)
	pcall(UpdateTrackerGUI)

	pcall(function()
		local hud = getgenv()._spideyHUD
		if hud and hrp then
			local speed = hrp.Velocity.Magnitude
			if speed > topSpeed then topSpeed = speed end
			totalDistance = totalDistance + (speed * dt)

			local groundHeight = 0
			local rp = RaycastParams.new()
			rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
			rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			local gRay = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0, -500, 0), rp)
			groundHeight = gRay and (hrp.Position.Y - gRay.Position.Y) or hrp.Position.Y

			if getgenv()._hudEditActive then return end
			if not getgenv()._hudPrevState then getgenv()._hudPrevState = {} end
			local ps = getgenv()._hudPrevState
			local tweenIn = TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			local tweenOut = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In)

			local function animCell(cell, wanted, key)
				if not cell then return end
				local constraint = cell:FindFirstChild("SizeLimit")
				if not constraint then return end
				if ps[key] == nil then ps[key] = wanted end
				if wanted and not ps[key] then
					ps[key] = true
					cell.Visible = true
					constraint.MaxSize = Vector2.new(0, 36)
					TweenService:Create(constraint, tweenIn, {MaxSize = Vector2.new(200, 36)}):Play()
				elseif not wanted and ps[key] then
					ps[key] = false
					TweenService:Create(constraint, tweenOut, {MaxSize = Vector2.new(0, 36)}):Play()
					task.delay(0.25, function()
						if not ps[key] then cell.Visible = false end
					end)
				end
			end

			animCell(hud.speedCell, hudSpeedometer, "speed")
			if hud.speedLabel then hud.speedLabel.Text = math.floor(speed) end
			animCell(hud.heightCell, hudHeightMeter, "height")
			if hud.heightLabel then hud.heightLabel.Text = math.floor(groundHeight) end
			if hud.momCell then
				animCell(hud.momCell, hudMomentumBar, "mom")
				if hudMomentumBar then
					local maxMom = 225 * getMod()
					local pct = math.clamp(currentMomentum / maxMom, 0, 1)
					hud.momentumFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
					hud.momentumText.Text = math.floor(currentMomentum)
				end
			end
			animCell(hud.swingCell, hudSwingCounter, "swing")
			if hud.swingLabel then hud.swingLabel.Text = totalSwings end

			local anyVisible = (hudSpeedometer or hudHeightMeter or hudMomentumBar or hudSwingCounter)
			local containerWant = isEquipped and anyVisible
			if hud.container then
				if ps.container == nil then ps.container = containerWant end
				if containerWant and not ps.container then
					ps.container = true
					hud.container.Visible = true
					local cp = hud.container.Position
					hud.container.Position = UDim2.new(cp.X.Scale, cp.X.Offset, cp.Y.Scale, cp.Y.Offset - 50)
					TweenService:Create(hud.container, tweenIn, {Position = UDim2.new(cp.X.Scale, cp.X.Offset, cp.Y.Scale, cp.Y.Offset)}):Play()
				elseif not containerWant and ps.container then
					ps.container = false
					local cp = hud.container.Position
					local tw = TweenService:Create(hud.container, tweenOut, {Position = UDim2.new(cp.X.Scale, cp.X.Offset, cp.Y.Scale, cp.Y.Offset - 50)})
					tw:Play()
					tw.Completed:Connect(function()
						if not ps.container then hud.container.Visible = false end
					end)
				end
			end

			for _, child in pairs(hud.container:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name:sub(1, 3) == "Div" then
					child.Visible = containerWant
				end
			end

			if hud.radarFrame then
				local radarWant = radarEnabled and isEquipped
				if ps.radar == nil then ps.radar = radarWant end
				if radarWant and not ps.radar then
					ps.radar = true
					hud.radarFrame.Visible = true
					hud.radarFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
					TweenService:Create(hud.radarFrame, tweenIn, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 100)}):Play()
				elseif not radarWant and ps.radar then
					ps.radar = false
					local tw = TweenService:Create(hud.radarFrame, tweenOut, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)})
					tw:Play()
					tw.Completed:Connect(function()
						if not ps.radar then hud.radarFrame.Visible = false end
					end)
				end
				if not getgenv()._radarPool then
					getgenv()._radarPool = {}
					for i = 1, 20 do
						local dot = Instance.new("Frame", hud.radarFrame)
						dot.Name = "PlayerDot"
						dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)
						dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
						dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
						dot.ZIndex = 3
						dot.Visible = false
						Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
						getgenv()._radarPool[i] = dot
					end
					getgenv()._radarTick = 0
				end
				getgenv()._radarTick = (getgenv()._radarTick or 0) + dt
				if radarEnabled and isEquipped and getgenv()._radarTick >= 0.1 then
					getgenv()._radarTick = 0
					local pool = getgenv()._radarPool
					local idx = 0
					local radarRadius = 200
					local halfSize = 46
					local myPos = hrp.Position
					local camLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
					local camAngle = math.atan2(camLook.X, camLook.Z)
					for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
						if p ~= player and p.Character then
							local pHrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if pHrp and idx < #pool then
								local offset = pHrp.Position - myPos
								local dist = Vector3.new(offset.X, 0, offset.Z).Magnitude
								if dist <= radarRadius then
									idx = idx + 1
									local angle = math.atan2(offset.X, offset.Z) - camAngle
									local normDist = dist / radarRadius
									local dx = math.sin(angle) * normDist * halfSize
									local dy = -math.cos(angle) * normDist * halfSize
									pool[idx].Position = UDim2.new(0.5, dx - 2, 0.5, dy - 2)
									pool[idx].Visible = true
								end
							end
						end
					end
					for i = idx + 1, #pool do pool[i].Visible = false end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	if not isEquipped or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not hrp or not humanoid or not bv then return end
	local targetAlpha = isCinematic and 1 or 0
	slowMoAlpha = slowMoAlpha + (targetAlpha - slowMoAlpha) * (dt * 10)
	if math.abs(slowMoAlpha - targetAlpha) < 0.01 then slowMoAlpha = targetAlpha end
	local speedScale = isCinematic and 0.3 or 1.0
	if not isSlingshotting and not isChargingJump then
		if isSpeedBoosted then humanoid.WalkSpeed = 50 else humanoid.WalkSpeed = 30 end
	end
	workspace.Gravity = (lowGravityEnabled and 50 or 196.2) * speedScale
	if slowMoAlpha == 0 then
		if ccCinematic then ccCinematic.Saturation = 0; ccCinematic.Contrast = 0 end
	else
		if ccCinematic then ccCinematic.Saturation = -0.4 * slowMoAlpha; ccCinematic.Contrast = 0.15 * slowMoAlpha end
	end
	for _, obj in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("Sound") then
			local bp = obj:GetAttribute("BasePitch") or obj.PlaybackSpeed
			if not obj:GetAttribute("BasePitch") then obj:SetAttribute("BasePitch", bp) end
			obj.PlaybackSpeed = bp * speedScale
		end
	end

	if swingTrack and swingTrack.IsPlaying then 
		local tSpeed = 2.0
		if swingTrack.Animation and swingTrack.Animation.AnimationId == SWING_ANIM_ID_2 then
			tSpeed = 1.5
		end
		swingTrack:AdjustSpeed(tSpeed * speedScale) 
		if swingTrack.TimePosition >= 3.0 then
			swingTrack.TimePosition = 1.0
		end
	end

	if fallTrack and fallTrack.IsPlaying then fallTrack:AdjustSpeed((currentFallAnimIndex == 2 and 2.0 or 1.0) * speedScale) end
	if glideTrack and glideTrack.IsPlaying then glideTrack:AdjustSpeed(1.0 * speedScale) end
	if trickTrack and trickTrack.IsPlaying then trickTrack:AdjustSpeed(1.0 * speedScale) end
	if stepFallTrack and stepFallTrack.IsPlaying then stepFallTrack:AdjustSpeed(1.0 * speedScale) end
	if startFallTrack and startFallTrack.IsPlaying then
		if startFallTrack.Length > 0 and startFallTrack.TimePosition >= (startFallTrack.Length - 0.05) then
			startFallTrack:AdjustSpeed(0)
		else
			startFallTrack:AdjustSpeed(1.5 * speedScale)
		end
	end
	local state = humanoid:GetState()
	if isCinematic and not swinging and not isDashing and not isVaulting and not isZipping and state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
		local v = hrp.Velocity
		hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(v.X * (1 - dt * 2 * slowMoAlpha), v.Y * (1 - dt * 2 * slowMoAlpha), v.Z * (1 - dt * 2 * slowMoAlpha))
	end
	if not isWallRunning and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
		local wg = hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro")
		if wg then wg.MaxTorque = Vector3.zero end
	end
	if isSlingshotting and slingshotStartCF then
		hrp.CFrame = slingshotStartCF
		bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
		bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
	end
	if isLaunching then return end
	local isTyping = UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= nil
	if (state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed or humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air) then
		slideLocked = false
	end

	local flatVel = Vector3.new(hrp.Velocity.X, 0, hrp.Velocity.Z).Magnitude
	if ((state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed) and flatVel > 1 and not swinging and not stuckToWall and not isZipping and not isSlingshotting and isEquipped) then
		if not runPlaying then
			if runTrack then runTrack:Play(0.1); runTrack:AdjustSpeed(1.5) end
			runPlaying = true
		end
	else
		if runPlaying then
			if runTrack then runTrack:Stop(0.1) end
			runPlaying = false
		end
	end

	if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and lastState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running and not swinging and not stuckToWall and not isZipping and not isSlingshotting and not isDashing and not isVaulting then
		if fallTime == 0 then walkedOffLedge = true end
	end
	if state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping or swinging or isZipping then
		walkedOffLedge = false
	end
	if isHangingEmote and hangWorldAtt and hangMidAtt then
		if rightHand and leftHand then hangMidAtt.WorldPosition = (rightHand.Position + leftHand.Position) / 2 else hangMidAtt.WorldPosition = hrp.Position end
		local rayParams = getIgnoreParams()
		local rayUp = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0, HANG_MAX_DIST + 10, 0), rayParams)
		local targetCeilingPos = rayUp and rayUp.Position or (hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, HANG_MAX_DIST, 0))
		hangWorldAtt.WorldPosition = targetCeilingPos
		local upMove = 0
		if not isTyping then
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then upMove = 1 end
			if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then upMove = -1 end
		end
		bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
		local moveSpeed = HANG_CLIMB_SPEED * 0.85
		local distToCeil = (hrp.Position - targetCeilingPos).Magnitude
		if upMove == 1 and distToCeil < 4 then upMove = 0 end
		bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, upMove * moveSpeed, 0)
		local distFromStart = 0
		if hangStartPos then distFromStart = (hrp.Position - hangStartPos).Magnitude end
		if distFromStart > HANG_MAX_DIST then
			StopAllAnimations()
			humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			humanoid.AutoRotate = true
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
		end
	end
	if not isMantling and state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running and not swinging and not isZipping and not isSlingshotting and not isVaulting and not isDashing and not isHangingEmote and (tick() - lastMantleTime > 0.5) then
		if humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then
			local flatLook = Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
			if flatLook.Magnitude > 0 then
				flatLook = flatLook.Unit
				local rp = getIgnoreParams()
				local hipHeight = humanoid.HipHeight > 0 and humanoid.HipHeight or 2
				local kneePos = hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, hipHeight * 0.5, 0)
				local hitTorso = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, flatLook * 2.5, rp)
				local hitKnee = workspace:Raycast(kneePos, flatLook * 2.5, rp)
				local hitRef = hitTorso or hitKnee
				if hitRef and math.abs(hitRef.Normal.Y) < 0.2 then
					local headPos = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
					local hitHead = workspace:Raycast(headPos, flatLook * 2.5, rp)
					if not hitHead then
						local topCheckOrigin = hitRef.Position + (flatLook * 1.5) + Vector3.new(0, 4.5, 0)
						local hit3 = workspace:Raycast(topCheckOrigin, Vector3.new(0,-6,0), rp)
						if hit3 and hit3.Normal.Y > 0.8 then
							local footY = hrp.Position.Y - hipHeight - (hrp.Size.Y/2)
							local blockHeight = hit3.Position.Y - footY
							if blockHeight > 0.5 and blockHeight <= 5.0 then
								isMantling = true
								lastMantleTime = tick()
								stuckToWall = false
								wallDebounce = true
								wallAttachDebounceTime = tick() + 0.5
								StopAllAnimations()
								isMantling = true
								if mantleTrack then mantleTrack:Play(0.1) end
								humanoid.PlatformStand = true
								hrp.Anchored = true
								bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
								hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
								hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
								local endPos = hit3.Position + Vector3.new(0, humanoid.HipHeight + (hrp.Size.Y/2) + 0.1, 0)
								local tInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
								local t = TweenService:Create(hrp, tInfo, {CFrame = CFrame.new(endPos, endPos + flatLook)})
								t:Play()
								task.delay(0.25, function()
									if isMantling then
										isMantling = false
										humanoid.PlatformStand = false
										hrp.Anchored = false
										if mantleTrack then mantleTrack:Stop(0.2) end
										if not isEquipped then return end
										humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
										wallDebounce = false
									end
								end)
								return
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if isMantling then return end
	local wrp = getIgnoreParams()
	if not isVaulting then
		local wallHit = nil
		local hitNormal = nil
		if stuckToWall and wallClimbEnabled then
			local CRAWL_CAST_DIST = 7
			local CRAWL_WRAP_DIST = 10
			local bestCrawlHit = nil
			local bestCrawlScore = -999
			local function scoreHit(hit, bonusForMovement)
				if not hit or not hit.Instance.CanCollide then return -999 end
				local nDot = smoothWallNormal:Dot(-hit.Normal)
				local dist = (hit.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
				local distPenalty = dist * 0.05
				local score = nDot - distPenalty + (bonusForMovement or 0)
				return score
			end
			local primaryHit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, -smoothWallNormal * CRAWL_CAST_DIST, wrp)
			if primaryHit then
				local s = scoreHit(primaryHit, 0.3)
				if s > bestCrawlScore then bestCrawlScore = s; bestCrawlHit = primaryHit end
			end
			local inX, inY = 0, 0
			if not isTyping then
				if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then inY = 1 end
				if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then inY = -1 end
				if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then inX = -1 end
				if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then inX = 1 end
			end
			local hasInput = (inX ~= 0 or inY ~= 0)
			if hasInput then
				local camLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
				local hitNmlTemp = smoothWallNormal
				local upOnWallTemp = (camLook - hitNmlTemp * camLook:Dot(hitNmlTemp))
				if upOnWallTemp.Magnitude < 0.001 then
					upOnWallTemp = (Vector3.new(0,1,0) - hitNmlTemp * hitNmlTemp.Y)
				end
				if upOnWallTemp.Magnitude > 0.001 then upOnWallTemp = upOnWallTemp.Unit else upOnWallTemp = Vector3.new(0,0,-1) end
				local rightOnWallTemp = upOnWallTemp:Cross(hitNmlTemp)
				if rightOnWallTemp.Magnitude > 0.001 then rightOnWallTemp = rightOnWallTemp.Unit else rightOnWallTemp = Vector3.new(1,0,0) end
				local inputDir = (upOnWallTemp * inY + rightOnWallTemp * inX).Unit
				local moveHit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, inputDir * CRAWL_CAST_DIST, wrp)
				if moveHit then
					local s = scoreHit(moveHit, 0.5)
					if s > bestCrawlScore then bestCrawlScore = s; bestCrawlHit = moveHit end
				end
				local diagDir = (inputDir + (-smoothWallNormal) * 0.5).Unit
				local diagHit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, diagDir * CRAWL_CAST_DIST, wrp)
				if diagHit then
					local s = scoreHit(diagHit, 0.4)
					if s > bestCrawlScore then bestCrawlScore = s; bestCrawlHit = diagHit end
				end
				local offsetOrigin = hrp.Position + inputDir * 3.5 + smoothWallNormal * 2
				local wrapHit = workspace:Raycast(offsetOrigin, -smoothWallNormal * CRAWL_WRAP_DIST, wrp)
				if wrapHit then
					local s = scoreHit(wrapHit, 0.3)
					if s > bestCrawlScore then bestCrawlScore = s; bestCrawlHit = wrapHit end
				end
			end
			local tangent1 = smoothWallNormal:Cross(Vector3.new(0,1,0))
			if tangent1.Magnitude < 0.01 then tangent1 = smoothWallNormal:Cross(Vector3.new(1,0,0)) end
			tangent1 = tangent1.Unit
			local tangent2 = smoothWallNormal:Cross(tangent1).Unit
			for i = 0, 5 do
				local angle = (i / 6) * math.pi * 2
				local dir = (tangent1 * math.cos(angle) + tangent2 * math.sin(angle)).Unit
				local rayDir = (-smoothWallNormal * 0.7 + dir * 0.8).Unit
				local hit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, rayDir * CRAWL_CAST_DIST, wrp)
				if hit then
					local s = scoreHit(hit, 0)
					if s > bestCrawlScore then bestCrawlScore = s; bestCrawlHit = hit end
				end
			end
			if not bestCrawlHit then
				local wrapOrigin = hrp.Position + (smoothWallNormal * 3)
				local wrapHit = workspace:Raycast(wrapOrigin, -smoothWallNormal * CRAWL_WRAP_DIST, wrp)
				if wrapHit and wrapHit.Instance.CanCollide then bestCrawlHit = wrapHit end
			end
			if not bestCrawlHit then
				local downHit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0,-CRAWL_CAST_DIST,0), wrp)
				if downHit and downHit.Instance.CanCollide then bestCrawlHit = downHit end
			end
			if not bestCrawlHit then
				local upHit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0,CRAWL_CAST_DIST,0), wrp)
				if upHit and upHit.Instance.CanCollide then bestCrawlHit = upHit end
			end
			local wallCheck = bestCrawlHit
			if wallCheck then
				humanoid.AutoRotate = false
				hrp.CanCollide = false
				local inX2, inY2 = 0, 0
				if not isTyping then
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then inY2 = 1 end
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then inY2 = -1 end
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then inX2 = -1 end
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then inX2 = 1 end
				end
				local hasInput2 = (inX2 ~= 0 or inY2 ~= 0)
				if hasInput2 and (isEmoting or isHangingEmote) then
					isEmoting = false; isHangingEmote = false
					if emoteTrack then emoteTrack:Stop(0.1) end
					if hangBeam then hangBeam:Destroy(); hangBeam = nil end
				end
				local camLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
				local hitNml = wallCheck.Normal
				local upOnWall = (camLook - hitNml * camLook:Dot(hitNml))
				if upOnWall.Magnitude < 0.001 then upOnWall = (Vector3.new(0,1,0) - hitNml * hitNml.Y) end
				if upOnWall.Magnitude > 0.001 then upOnWall = upOnWall.Unit else upOnWall = Vector3.new(0,0,-1) end
				local rightOnWall = upOnWall:Cross(hitNml)
				if rightOnWall.Magnitude > 0.001 then rightOnWall = rightOnWall.Unit else rightOnWall = Vector3.new(1,0,0) end
				local moveDir3D = hasInput2 and (upOnWall * inY2 + rightOnWall * inX2).Unit or lastMoveDirection
				if hasInput2 then lastMoveDirection = moveDir3D end
				local newNormal = hitNml
				if hasInput2 then
					local aheadHit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position + moveDir3D * 2.5 + smoothWallNormal * 1.0, -smoothWallNormal * 5, wrp)
					if aheadHit then newNormal = aheadHit.Normal end
				end
				local dotVal = math.clamp(smoothWallNormal:Dot(newNormal), -1, 1)
				local angleDiff = math.acos(dotVal)
				if angleDiff > 0.001 then
					local blendAlpha
					if angleDiff > math.rad(70) then blendAlpha = 0.7
					elseif angleDiff > math.rad(30) then blendAlpha = 0.4
					else blendAlpha = 0.2 end
					smoothWallNormal = smoothWallNormal:Lerp(newNormal, blendAlpha).Unit
				end
				local surfacePos = wallCheck.Position
				local surfaceNormal = smoothWallNormal
				local playerToSurface = hrp.Position - surfacePos
				local distAlongNormal = playerToSurface:Dot(surfaceNormal)
				local closestPointOnWall = hrp.Position - surfaceNormal * distAlongNormal
				local snapPos = closestPointOnWall + surfaceNormal * CRAWL_SNAP_DISTANCE
				local newCF = buildWallFrame(snapPos, smoothWallNormal, moveDir3D)
				local px, py, pz = newCF:GetComponents()
				if px == px and py == py and pz == pz then
					local lerpSpeed = hasInput2 and 0.45 or 0.25
					hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame:Lerp(newCF, lerpSpeed)
				end
				hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
				lastWallHitTime = tick()
				humanoid.PlatformStand = true
				bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
				if hasInput2 then
					bv.Velocity = moveDir3D * WALL_CRAWL_SPEED
					setCrawlSpeed(1)
				else
					bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
					setCrawlSpeed(0)
				end
				ensureCrawlTrack()
			else
				if (tick() - lastWallHitTime) > 0.35 then
					stuckToWall = false
					humanoid.PlatformStand = false
					humanoid.AutoRotate = true
					hrp.CanCollide = true
					stopCrawlTrack()
					bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
					hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
					local escapeDir = smoothWallNormal
					if escapeDir.Magnitude < 0.5 then escapeDir = Vector3.new(0,1,0) end
					hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position + (escapeDir * 3), hrp.Position + (escapeDir * 3) + Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z), Vector3.new(0,1,0))
				end
			end
		elseif stuckToWall and not wallClimbEnabled then
			local flatLook = Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
			if flatLook.Magnitude > 0 then flatLook = flatLook.Unit else flatLook = hrp.CFrame.LookVector end
			wallHit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, -smoothWallNormal * 5, wrp)
			if not wallHit then wallHit = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, flatLook * 5, wrp) end
			if not wallHit then
				local wrapOrigin = hrp.Position + (flatLook * 3) + (smoothWallNormal * 2)
				wallHit = workspace:Raycast(wrapOrigin, -smoothWallNormal * 6, wrp)
			end
			if wallHit then
				hitNormal = wallHit.Normal
				smoothWallNormal = hitNormal
			end
			hrp.CanCollide = false
			local isGrounded = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0,-3.2,0), wrp) ~= nil
			local invalidState = (state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding)
			if not wallHit or invalidState or isGrounded or swinging or wallDebounce or gliding or tick() < wallAttachDebounceTime then
				stuckToWall = false
				humanoid.PlatformStand = false
				humanoid.AutoRotate = true
				hrp.CanCollide = true
				local wg = hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro")
				if wg then wg.MaxTorque = Vector3.zero end
				if wallTrack then wallTrack:Stop(0.2) end
				if wallLeftTrack then wallLeftTrack:Stop(0.2) end
				if wallRunTrack then wallRunTrack:Stop(0.2) end
				if wallRunLeftTrack then wallRunLeftTrack:Stop(0.2) end
				isWallRunning = false
			else
				local isRightWall = false
				local isStrictLeft = false
				local isFrontWall = false
				if hitNormal then
					local camFlatLook = Vector3.new(camera.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, camera.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit
					local camFlatRight = Vector3.new(camera.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, camera.CFrame.RightVector.Z).Unit
					local frontDot = camFlatLook:Dot(-hitNormal)
					local rightDot = camFlatRight:Dot(-hitNormal)
					if frontDot > 0.5 then isFrontWall = true elseif rightDot > 0 then isRightWall = true else isStrictLeft = true end
				end
				local isHoldingW = not isTyping and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W)
				local targetPos = wallHit.Position + (hitNormal * SLIDE_SNAP_DISTANCE)
				local posDiff = targetPos - hrp.Position
				local inwardPull = posDiff * 15
				if isHoldingW and (isRightWall or isStrictLeft) and not isFrontWall then
					humanoid.PlatformStand = false
					humanoid.AutoRotate = true
					if not isWallRunning then
						isWallRunning = true
						currentWallRunSpeed = math.clamp(16 + (currentMomentum * 0.5), 16, 50)
						if wallTrack then wallTrack:Stop(0.2) end
						if wallLeftTrack then wallLeftTrack:Stop(0.2) end
						if wallRunTrack then wallRunTrack:Stop(0.2) end
						if wallRunLeftTrack then wallRunLeftTrack:Stop(0.2) end
						if isRightWall then
							if not wallRunTrack then
								local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
								anim.AnimationId = WALL_RUN_RIGHT_ID
								wallRunTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
								wallRunTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
								wallRunTrack.Looped = true
							end
							wallRunTrack:Play(0.2)
						else
							if not wallRunLeftTrack then
								local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
								anim.AnimationId = WALL_RUN_LEFT_ID
								wallRunLeftTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
								wallRunLeftTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
								wallRunLeftTrack.Looped = true
							end
							wallRunLeftTrack:Play(0.2)
						end
					end
					local dir = hitNormal:Cross(Vector3.new(0,1,0)).Unit
					if camera.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(dir) < 0 then dir = -dir end
					targetWallRunDir = dir
					bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
					bv.Velocity = (targetWallRunDir * currentWallRunSpeed) + inwardPull
					local snapCF = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + targetWallRunDir)
					local wg = hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro")
					if wg then wg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6); wg.CFrame = snapCF end
					if isRightWall and wallRunTrack and wallRunTrack.IsPlaying then wallRunTrack:AdjustSpeed(currentWallRunSpeed / 25)
					elseif isStrictLeft and wallRunLeftTrack and wallRunLeftTrack.IsPlaying then wallRunLeftTrack:AdjustSpeed(currentWallRunSpeed / 25) end
					if currentWallRunSpeed < 50 then currentWallRunSpeed = math.min(currentWallRunSpeed + (dt * 25), 50)
					elseif currentWallRunSpeed > 50 then currentWallRunSpeed = math.max(currentWallRunSpeed - (dt * 15), 50) end
					currentMomentum = currentWallRunSpeed * 1.5
				else
					humanoid.PlatformStand = false
					humanoid.AutoRotate = true
					bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
					bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, goingUp and HANG_CLIMB_SPEED or (goingDown and -HANG_CLIMB_SPEED or 0), 0) + inwardPull
					if isRightWall then
						local lookDir = hitNormal:Cross(Vector3.new(0,1,0)).Unit
						if hrp.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(lookDir) < 0 then lookDir = -lookDir end
						local snapCF = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + lookDir)
						local wg = hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro")
						if wg then wg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6); wg.CFrame = snapCF end
					elseif isStrictLeft or isFrontWall then
						local lookDir = Vector3.new(0,1,0):Cross(hitNormal).Unit
						if hrp.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(lookDir) < 0 then lookDir = -lookDir end
						local snapCF = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + lookDir)
						local wg = hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro")
						if wg then wg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6); wg.CFrame = snapCF end
					end
					if isWallRunning then
						isWallRunning = false
						if wallRunTrack then wallRunTrack:Stop(0.2) end
						if wallRunLeftTrack then wallRunLeftTrack:Stop(0.2) end
						if isStrictLeft and not isRightWall and not isFrontWall then if wallLeftTrack then wallLeftTrack:Play(0.2) end else if wallTrack then wallTrack:Play(0.2) end end
					end
				end
			end
		else
			if not swinging and not gliding and not isChargingJump and not isEmoting and not isZipping and not isSlingshotting and not isDashing then
				bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
			end
			if hrp.CanCollide == false then hrp.CanCollide = true end
			if not wallHit then
				local speedBonus = math.clamp(hrp.Velocity.Magnitude / 25, 0, 10)
				local catchDist = 1.5 + speedBonus
				local cLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
				local cRight = camera.CFrame.RightVector
				local fVec = Vector3.new(cLook.X, 0, cLook.Z).Unit
				local rVec = Vector3.new(cRight.X, 0, cRight.Z).Unit
				local lVec = -rVec
				local bVec = -fVec
				local wallLeft = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, lVec * catchDist, wrp)
				local wallRight = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, rVec * catchDist, wrp)
				local wallForward = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, fVec * catchDist, wrp)
				local wallBackLeft = nil
				local wallBackRight = nil
				if not (wallLeft or wallRight or wallForward) then
					wallBackLeft = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, (lVec + bVec).Unit * catchDist, wrp)
					wallBackRight = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, (rVec + bVec).Unit * catchDist, wrp)
				end
				wallHit = wallLeft or wallRight or wallForward or wallBackLeft or wallBackRight
				if wallHit then
					if math.abs(wallHit.Normal.Y) > 0.85 then
						wallHit = nil
					else
						hitNormal = wallHit.Normal
						smoothWallNormal = hitNormal
					end
				end
			end
			if tick() > wallAttachDebounceTime then
				local validState = (state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall) or (wallClimbEnabled and state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) or (state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding)
				if wallHit and wallHit.Instance.CanCollide and validState and not wallDebounce and not gliding and not isZipping and not slideLocked and not isHangingEmote and not UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
					if swinging then
						swinging = false
						isHoldingMouse = false
						currentWebPoint = nil
						if swingTrack then swingTrack:Stop(0.2) end
						hideAllBeams()
					end
					if wallClimbEnabled then
						playSound(WALL_IMPACT_SOUND_ID, 1, 1)
						spawnImpactDust(wallHit.Position, wallHit.Normal)
						stuckToWall = true
						goingUp = false
						humanoid.PlatformStand = true
						smoothWallNormal = hitNormal
						targetWallNormal = hitNormal
						local toSurface = hrp.Position - wallHit.Position
						local distToWall = toSurface:Dot(hitNormal)
						local onWall = hrp.Position - hitNormal * distToWall
						local snapPos = onWall + hitNormal * CRAWL_SNAP_DISTANCE
						hrp.CFrame = buildWallFrame(snapPos, hitNormal, hrp.CFrame.LookVector)
						lastWallHitTime = tick()
						ensureCrawlTrack()
						setCrawlSpeed(0)
						humanoid.AutoRotate = false
					elseif state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
						playSound(WALL_IMPACT_SOUND_ID, 1, 1)
						spawnImpactDust(wallHit.Position, wallHit.Normal)
						stuckToWall = true
						smoothWallNormal = hitNormal
						goingUp = false
						StopAllAnimations()
						fallingAnimPlaying = false
						startFallPlaying = false
						stepFallPlaying = false
						local newPos = wallHit.Position + (hitNormal * SLIDE_SNAP_DISTANCE)
						hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
						local isRightWall = false
						local isStrictLeft = false
						local isFrontWall = false
						if hitNormal then
							local camFlatLook = Vector3.new(camera.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, camera.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit
							local camFlatRight = Vector3.new(camera.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, camera.CFrame.RightVector.Z).Unit
							local frontDot = camFlatLook:Dot(-hitNormal)
							local rightDot = camFlatRight:Dot(-hitNormal)
							if frontDot > 0.5 then isFrontWall = true elseif rightDot > 0 then isRightWall = true else isStrictLeft = true end
						end
						local lookDir = Vector3.new(0,1,0):Cross(hitNormal).Unit
						if hrp.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(lookDir) < 0 then lookDir = -lookDir end
						hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(newPos, newPos + lookDir)
						local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
						if isStrictLeft and not isRightWall and not isFrontWall then
							if wallLeftTrack then wallLeftTrack:Stop() end
							anim.AnimationId = WALL_LEFT_ID
							wallLeftTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
							wallLeftTrack.Looped = true
							wallLeftTrack:Play(0.2)
						else
							if wallTrack then wallTrack:Stop() end
							anim.AnimationId = WALL_RIGHT_ID
							wallTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
							wallTrack.Looped = true
							wallTrack:Play(0.2)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if tick() > wallAttachDebounceTime then
			if wallClimbEnabled and not stuckToWall and not swinging and not isEmoting and not isZipping and not isSlingshotting and not isDashing then
				local floorRay = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0,-4,0), wrp)
				if floorRay and floorRay.Instance.CanCollide then
					stuckToWall = true
					smoothWallNormal = floorRay.Normal
					if smoothWallNormal.Y < 0.5 then smoothWallNormal = Vector3.new(0,1,0) end
					lastMoveDirection = Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
					if lastMoveDirection.Magnitude < 0.01 then lastMoveDirection = Vector3.new(0,0,-1) else lastMoveDirection = lastMoveDirection.Unit end
					lastWallHitTime = tick()
					ensureCrawlTrack()
					setCrawlSpeed(humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 and 1 or 0)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if isHoldingMouse then
		local nTime = tick()
		if swinging and (nTime - swingStartTime >= 2.0) then
			isHoldingMouse = false
			local spawnPos = currentWebPoint
			local spawnHand = (activeWebHand == "Right") and rightHand or leftHand
			local spawnNormal = currentWebNormal
			local wasSwinging = swinging
			swinging = false
			currentWebPoint = nil
			if swingTrack then swingTrack:Stop(0.3) end
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
			hideAllBeams()
			if wasSwinging and spawnPos and spawnHand then task.spawn(function() pcall(function() spawnWebResidue(spawnPos, spawnHand.Position, spawnNormal) end) end) end
		else
			if (not swinging) or (nTime - lastWebTime >= WEB_FIRE_RATE) then
				if (not swinging and nTime - lastWebTime >= 0.2) or swinging then
					if swinging and currentWebPoint then
						local spawnPos = currentWebPoint
						local spawnHand = (activeWebHand == "Right") and rightHand or leftHand
						local spawnNormal = currentWebNormal
						task.spawn(function() pcall(function() spawnWebResidue(spawnPos, spawnHand.Position, spawnNormal) end) end)
					end
					local success = attemptNewSwing(true)
					if success then
						lastWebTime = nTime
						if not swingStartTime or swingStartTime == 0 then swingStartTime = nTime end
					end
				end
			end
			if swinging and nTime - lastSoundTime >= SOUND_FIRE_RATE then
				playInstantWebSound()
				lastSoundTime = nTime
			end
		end
	end
	local vel = hrp.Velocity.Magnitude
	local verticalVel = hrp.Velocity.Y
	local isSpecialFall = (fallingAnimPlaying and fallTrack and fallTrack.Animation and fallTrack.Animation.AnimationId == FALL_MAIN_ID_2) or isTricking

	if handTrailR and handTrailL then
		local trailEnabled = (vel > 60) and not isSpecialFall
		handTrailR.Enabled = trailEnabled
		handTrailL.Enabled = trailEnabled
	end

	if gliding then
		if (isNearGround() or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed) then
			gliding = false
			humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
			if glideTrack then glideTrack:Stop(0.15) end
		else
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
			local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
			local flatLook = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
			if flatLook.Magnitude > 0.001 then
				flatLook = flatLook.Unit
				local targetCF = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + flatLook)
				hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame:Lerp(targetCF, 0.4)
			else
				flatLook = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
			end
			local forward = flatLook * (GLIDE_FORWARD_SPEED + currentMomentum)
			bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(forward.X, GLIDE_FALL_SPEED, forward.Z) * speedScale
			currentMomentum = currentMomentum * GLIDE_DRAG
		end
	end
	if isChargingJump then
		bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,0,1e6); bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero; humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
		pcall(function()
			local pct = math.clamp((tick() - chargeStartTime) / MAX_CHARGE_TIME, 0, 1)
			updateChargeVFX(getgenv()._chargeVFX, pct)
		end)
	end

	local baseFov = (isSlingshotting) and 60 or 70
	local momentumFov = currentMomentum / 6
	local fovGoal = baseFov + momentumFov + fovKick
	if isCinematic then fovGoal = fovGoal - (15 * slowMoAlpha) end
	if vel > 100 then fovGoal = fovGoal + 20 end
	fovKick = fovKick * 0.85

	camera.FieldOfView = camera.FieldOfView + (fovGoal - camera.FieldOfView) * 0.2
	local sideOffset = 0
	if swinging then sideOffset = (activeWebHand == "Right") and 1.5 or -1.5 end
	humanoid.CameraOffset = humanoid.CameraOffset:Lerp(Vector3.new(sideOffset,0,0), 0.1)
	local shakeOffset = Vector3.new((math.sin(tick()*55)*shakeIntensity),(math.cos(tick()*50)*shakeIntensity),(math.sin(tick()*45)*shakeIntensity))
	shakeIntensity = shakeIntensity * shakeDamping
	impactKick = impactKick:Lerp(Vector3.zero, 0.2)
	local targetCamRoll = 0
	if stuckToWall and wallClimbEnabled then
		targetCamRoll = 0
	elseif not gliding and (swinging or fallingAnimPlaying or stuckToWall or isSlingshotting or isDashing or startFallPlaying or isZipping) then
		local moveDir = hrp.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(hrp.Velocity)
		targetCamRoll = math.clamp(-moveDir.X / 20, -10, 10)
	end
	local rollDiff = (targetCamRoll - cameraRoll)
	rollDiff = (rollDiff + 180) % 360 - 180
	cameraRoll = cameraRoll + (rollDiff * 0.1)
	camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(cameraRoll)) * CFrame.new(shakeOffset.X + impactKick.X, shakeOffset.Y + impactKick.Y, shakeOffset.Z + impactKick.Z)

	local isLandedNow = (state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed)
	if isLandedNow and lastState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
		lastLandTime = tick()
		if isLunging then isLunging = false; if lungeTrack then lungeTrack:Stop(0.2) end end
		if not landDebounce and lastVerticalVelocity < LANDING_THRESHOLD then
			landDebounce = true
			local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
			rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
			rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			local groundRay = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0,-10,0), rayParams)
			if groundRay then
				spawnImpactDust(groundRay.Position, groundRay.Normal)
			end
			task.delay(0.5, function() landDebounce = false end)
		end

		if lastVerticalVelocity < -120 or fallTime > 1.8 then
			StopAllAnimations()
			isEmoting = true
			hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			anim.AnimationId = LANDING_EMOTE_ID
			emoteTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
			emoteTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
			emoteTrack.Looped = false
			emoteTrack:Play(0.1)

			local rayP = RaycastParams.new()
			rayP.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
			rayP.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			local gRay = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0,-10,0), rayP)
			if gRay then
				local hitPos = gRay.Position
				local hitNormal = gRay.Normal
				local impactStrength = math.clamp(math.abs(lastVerticalVelocity) / 100, 0.5, 1.5) * (vfxIntensity or 1)

				local crater = Instance.new("Part")
				crater.Name = "ImpactCrater"
				crater.Transparency = 1
				crater.Anchored = true
				crater.CanCollide = false
				crater.Size = Vector3.new(12,0.1,12)
				crater.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hitPos + (hitNormal * 0.2), hitPos + hitNormal) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90),0,0)
				crater.Parent = workspace
				local decal = Instance.new("Decal")
				decal.Texture = CRATER_DECAL_ID
				decal.Face = Enum.NormalId.Top
				decal.Transparency = 0
				decal.Parent = crater
				Debris:AddItem(crater, 5)
				spawnImpactDust(hitPos, hitNormal)

				local dustRing = Instance.new("Part")
				dustRing.Anchored = true
				dustRing.CanCollide = false
				dustRing.Transparency = 1
				dustRing.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hitPos, hitPos + hitNormal)
				dustRing.Parent = workspace
				local dustAtt = Instance.new("Attachment", dustRing)
				local dustEmit = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
				dustEmit.Texture = "rbxassetid://283437152"
				dustEmit.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(180, 170, 155))
				dustEmit.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 6 * impactStrength), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 10 * impactStrength)})
				dustEmit.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.6), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
				dustEmit.Speed = NumberRange.new(20 * impactStrength, 40 * impactStrength)
				dustEmit.Drag = 5
				dustEmit.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.5)
				dustEmit.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(80, 80)
				dustEmit.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Front
				dustEmit.Rate = 0
				dustEmit.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-60, 60)
				dustEmit.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
				dustEmit.Parent = dustAtt
				dustEmit:Emit(math.floor(35 * impactStrength))
				Debris:AddItem(dustRing, 2.5)

				for i = 1, math.floor(6 * impactStrength) do
					task.spawn(function()
						local chunk = Instance.new("Part")
						chunk.Name = "ImpactDebris"
						chunk.Size = Vector3.new(math.random() * 0.6 + 0.2, math.random() * 0.4 + 0.1, math.random() * 0.6 + 0.2)
						chunk.Color = gRay.Instance.Color
						chunk.Material = gRay.Instance.Material
						chunk.CFrame = CFrame.new(hitPos + Vector3.new(math.random() * 2 - 1, 0.5, math.random() * 2 - 1))
						chunk.Anchored = false
						chunk.CanCollide = true
						chunk.Parent = workspace
						local angle = math.rad(math.random() * 360)
						local power = (10 + math.random() * 15) * impactStrength
						chunk.Velocity = Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * power, 20 + math.random() * 20 * impactStrength, math.sin(angle) * power)
						chunk.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random() * 20 - 10, math.random() * 20 - 10, math.random() * 20 - 10)
						task.delay(0.8, function()
							if chunk and chunk.Parent then
								TweenService:Create(chunk, TweenInfo.new(1.5), {Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05)}):Play()
								Debris:AddItem(chunk, 1.6)
							end
						end)
					end)
				end

				shakeIntensity = 3 * impactStrength
				impactKick = Vector3.new(0, -0.3 * impactStrength, 0)

				task.spawn(function()
					pcall(function()
						local flash = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
						flash.Name = "LandingFlash"
						flash.Brightness = 0.15 * impactStrength
						flash.Parent = Lighting
						TweenService:Create(flash, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Brightness = 0}):Play()
						Debris:AddItem(flash, 0.5)
					end)
				end)
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				emoteTrack.Stopped:Wait()
				if isEmoting then
					isEmoting = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	lastState = state
	lastVerticalVelocity = verticalVel
	if isSlingshotting or isDashing or isEmoting then return end

	if swinging and currentWebPoint then
		if isLooping then
			currentMomentum = math.min(currentMomentum + (dt * 60 * speedScale), 225 * getMod())
		else
			if swingTrack and swingTrack.TimePosition >= SWING_LOOP_POINT then swingTrack.TimePosition = 1.0 end
		end

		local isCoilingR = webLaunchConnections[webBeamR] ~= nil
		local isCoilingL = webLaunchConnections[webBeamL] ~= nil

		if activeWebHand == "Right" then
			webBeamR.Enabled = true
			webBeamL.Enabled = false
			if webLoopBeamR then webLoopBeamR.Enabled = isCoilingR end
			if webLoopBeamL then webLoopBeamL.Enabled = false end
			if not isCoilingR and webMidAttR then webMidAttR.WorldPosition = currentWebPoint end
		else
			webBeamL.Enabled = true
			webBeamR.Enabled = false
			if webLoopBeamL then webLoopBeamL.Enabled = isCoilingL end
			if webLoopBeamR then webLoopBeamR.Enabled = false end
			if not isCoilingL and webMidAttL then webMidAttL.WorldPosition = currentWebPoint end
		end

		applyWebColor()
		if worldAttR then worldAttR.WorldPosition = currentWebPoint end
		if worldAttL then worldAttL.WorldPosition = currentWebPoint end

		local webDist = (currentWebPoint - hrp.Position).Magnitude
		local sag = math.clamp(webDist * 0.12, 1, 15)
		local activeBeam = activeWebHand == "Right" and webBeamR or webBeamL
		if activeBeam and not webLaunchConnections[activeBeam] then
			activeBeam.CurveSize0 = -sag
			activeBeam.CurveSize1 = sag * 0.5
		end

		bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
		local direction = (currentWebPoint - hrp.Position).Unit
		local moveVec = camera.CFrame.LookVector
			local rawVel = (moveVec * ((SWING_SPEED * getMod()) + currentMomentum)) + (direction * 20)
		if isLooping then
			bv.Velocity = (rawVel + Vector3.new(0,80,0)) * speedScale
		else
			bv.Velocity = (rawVel + Vector3.new(0, goingUp and (VERTICAL_BOOST * getMod()) or (goingDown and -(VERTICAL_BOOST * getMod()) or (math.sin(tick()*4)*20)), 0)) * speedScale
		end
	elseif not isZipping and not isVaulting and not isDashing then
		hideAllBeams()
	end

	if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and not swinging and not stuckToWall and not gliding and not isDashing and not isChargingJump and not isEmoting and not isZipping and not (swingReleaseTrack and swingReleaseTrack.IsPlaying) then
		fallTime = fallTime + (dt * speedScale)
		local accel = 25 * getMod()
		local cap = 120 * getMod()
		if fallingAnimPlaying or isTricking then accel = 60 * getMod(); cap = 350 * getMod() end
		currentMomentum = math.min(currentMomentum + (dt * accel * speedScale), cap)
		if not isTricking then
			local isStepPhase = walkedOffLedge and fallTime < 0.2
			local isStartPhase = fallTime < (walkedOffLedge and 0.7 or 0.5)
			if isStepPhase then
				if not stepFallPlaying then
					if stepFallTrack then stepFallTrack:Play(0.1) end
					stepFallPlaying = true
				end
			elseif isStartPhase then
				if stepFallPlaying then if stepFallTrack then stepFallTrack:Stop(0.2) end; stepFallPlaying = false end
				if not startFallPlaying then
					local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
					anim.AnimationId = FALL_START_ID
					startFallTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
					startFallTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
					startFallTrack.Looped = false
					startFallTrack:Play(0.2)
					startFallPlaying = true
				end
			else
				if stepFallPlaying then if stepFallTrack then stepFallTrack:Stop(0.2) end; stepFallPlaying = false end
				if startFallPlaying then if startFallTrack then startFallTrack:Stop(0.2) end; startFallPlaying = false end
				if not fallingAnimPlaying then
					local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
					currentFallAnimIndex = math.random(1,2)
					if currentFallAnimIndex == 1 then anim.AnimationId = FALL_MAIN_ID else anim.AnimationId = FALL_MAIN_ID_2 end
					fallTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
					fallTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
					fallTrack.Looped = true
					fallTrack:Play(0.2)
					if currentFallAnimIndex == 2 then fallTrack:AdjustSpeed(2 * speedScale) else fallTrack:AdjustSpeed(1 * speedScale) end
					fallingAnimPlaying = true
				end
			end
		end
	else
		fallTime = 0
		if state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and not gliding and not isWallRunning then currentMomentum = currentMomentum * 0.98 end
		if startFallPlaying then if startFallTrack then startFallTrack:Stop(0.1) end; startFallPlaying = false end
		if fallingAnimPlaying then if fallTrack then fallTrack:Stop(0.1) end; fallingAnimPlaying = false end
		if stepFallPlaying then if stepFallTrack then stepFallTrack:Stop(0.1) end; stepFallPlaying = false end
		if isTricking then if trickTrack then trickTrack:Stop(0.1) end; isTricking = false end
	end
	if hrp.Velocity.Magnitude < 2 and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude == 0 and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and not swinging and not stuckToWall and not isChargingJump and not isEmoting and (xTrack == nil or not xTrack.IsPlaying) and not isZipping and not isMantling then
		if not idlePlaying then
			local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			anim.AnimationId = currentIdleId
			idleTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
			idleTrack.Looped = true
			idleTrack:Play(0.5)
			idlePlaying = true
		end
	else
		if idlePlaying then if idleTrack then idleTrack:Stop(0.3) end; idlePlaying = false end
	end
end))

table.insert(connections, UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
	if not isWindowFocused then return end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.cmdBar] and not gpe and (UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() == nil or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() == getgenv()._cmdInput) then
		pcall(function() getgenv()._toggleCmdBar() end)
		return
	end
	if UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= nil then return end
	if gpe or not isEquipped or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
		if mainFrame and mainFrame.Visible then
			local mp = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
			local ap = mainFrame.AbsolutePosition
			local as = mainFrame.AbsoluteSize
			if mp.X >= ap.X and mp.X <= ap.X + as.X and mp.Y >= ap.Y and mp.Y <= ap.Y + as.Y then return end
		end
	end
	if getgenv()._hudEditActive then return end

	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		if not isSlingshotting and not isDashing and not isChargingJump and not isZipping then
			isHoldingMouse = true; activeWebHand = "Left"; lastWebTime = tick(); lastSoundTime = tick(); swingStartTime = tick()
			local success = attemptNewSwing(false)
			if success then
				playInstantWebSound()
			end
		end
	end

	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then 
		isLooping = true 
	end

	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.U and UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() == nil then
		manualModeEnabled = not manualModeEnabled
		saveConfig()
		pcall(function()
			game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
				Title = "by Xurco",
				Text = manualModeEnabled and "Manual Mode Active" or "Manual Mode Off",
				Duration = 3
			})
		end)
	end

	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then isCinematic = true end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S and UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() == nil then
		if tick() - lastSTapTime < 0.3 then
			if tick() - lastSBackDashTime >= 2.0 then
				local isGrounded = (humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed)
				if isGrounded then
					lastSBackDashTime = tick()
					if backDashTrack then backDashTrack:Play(0.1); backDashTrack:AdjustSpeed(1.5) end
					bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,0,1e6)
					bv.Velocity = -hrp.CFrame.LookVector * 100
					isSpeedBoosted = true
					humanoid.WalkSpeed = 50
					task.delay(0.3, function() if not swinging and not isDashing and not isSlingshotting then bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero end end)
					task.delay(1, function() isSpeedBoosted = false end)
				end
			end
		end
		lastSTapTime = tick()
	end
	if (input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftAlt or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightAlt) then
		isHoldingAlt = true
		local state = humanoid:GetState()
		if (state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) and not swinging and not gliding and not isZipping and not isDashing then
			if not isTricking then
				isTricking = true
				if startFallTrack then startFallTrack:Stop(0.2) end
				if fallTrack then fallTrack:Stop(0.2) end
				startFallPlaying = false; fallingAnimPlaying = false
				local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
				anim.AnimationId = TRICK_ANIM_ID
				trickTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
				trickTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
				trickTrack.Looped = true
				trickTrack:Play(0.2)
				local isReverse = math.random(1,2) == 1
				task.spawn(function()
					while trickTrack.Length == 0 do RunService.RenderStepped:Wait() end
					if isTricking and trickTrack.IsPlaying then
						if isReverse then trickTrack:AdjustSpeed(-1); trickTrack.TimePosition = trickTrack.Length - 0.01 else trickTrack:AdjustSpeed(1); if trickTrack.Length > 1 then trickTrack.TimePosition = 1 end end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end
	if not photoModeActive and isEmoting and (input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D) then
		StopAllAnimations(); humanoid.PlatformStand = false; humanoid.AutoRotate = true; bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
		if hangWorldAtt then hangWorldAtt:Destroy(); hangWorldAtt = nil end
		if hangMidAtt then hangMidAtt:Destroy(); hangMidAtt = nil end
		return
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.menu] and UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() == nil then toggleSettingsGui(); return end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.forceReset] and UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() == nil then
		if getgenv()._forceResetSkipConfirm then
			getgenv()._doForceReset()
		else
			getgenv()._showResetConfirm()
		end
		return
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.photoMode] and UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() == nil then
		if not photoModeActive then
			photoModeActive = true
			photoModeSavedCF = hrp.CFrame
			photoModeChar = char
			hrp.Anchored = true
			if swingTrack then swingTrack:AdjustSpeed(0) end
			if fallTrack then fallTrack:AdjustSpeed(0) end
			if emoteTrack then emoteTrack:AdjustSpeed(0) end
			if glideTrack then glideTrack:AdjustSpeed(0) end
			if trickTrack then trickTrack:AdjustSpeed(0) end
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
			camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame
			pcall(function()
				local hud = getgenv()._spideyHUD
				if hud and hud.container then hud.container.Visible = false end
				if hud and hud.radarFrame then hud.radarFrame.Visible = false end
				if getgenv()._hudPrevState then
					getgenv()._hudPrevState.container = false
					getgenv()._hudPrevState.radar = false
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Photo Mode",
					Text = "WASD/QE to fly camera. Press L to exit.",
					Duration = 3
				})
			end)
		else
			photoModeActive = false
			if photoModeChar and photoModeChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				photoModeChar.HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
			end
			if swingTrack and swingTrack.IsPlaying then swingTrack:AdjustSpeed(1) end
			if fallTrack and fallTrack.IsPlaying then fallTrack:AdjustSpeed(1) end
			if emoteTrack and emoteTrack.IsPlaying then emoteTrack:AdjustSpeed(1) end
			if glideTrack and glideTrack.IsPlaying then glideTrack:AdjustSpeed(1) end
			if trickTrack and trickTrack.IsPlaying then trickTrack:AdjustSpeed(1) end
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
		end
		return
	end
	if photoModeActive then return end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.sonar] then if tick() - lastSonarTime >= SONAR_COOLDOWN then lastSonarTime = tick(); ActivateSonar() end end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl then
		if not isHangingEmote then
			wallClimbEnabled = not wallClimbEnabled
			saveConfig()
			if not wallClimbEnabled and stuckToWall then
				stuckToWall = false
				stopCrawlTrack()
				StopAllAnimations()
				humanoid.PlatformStand = false
				humanoid.AutoRotate = true
				hrp.CanCollide = true
				hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position + (smoothWallNormal * 3), hrp.Position + (smoothWallNormal * 3) + Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z), Vector3.new(0,1,0))
				slideLocked = true
				wallAttachDebounceTime = tick() + 2
			end
		end
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.perch] then
		if not swinging and not stuckToWall and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude == 0 then
			StopAllAnimations()
			isEmoting = true
			local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			anim.AnimationId = PERCH_EMOTES[math.random(1,#PERCH_EMOTES)]
			emoteTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
			emoteTrack.Looped = true
			emoteTrack:Play(0.2)
		end
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.hang] then
		if not swinging and not stuckToWall and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude == 0 then
			local rp = getIgnoreParams()
			local ray = workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0,HANG_MAX_DIST,0), rp)
			hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6,1e6,1e6)
			bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			StopAllAnimations()
			isEmoting = true
			isHangingEmote = true
			humanoid.PlatformStand = true
			local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			local chosenAnim = 1
			local targetPos
			if ray then
				targetPos = ray.Position
				local rand = math.random(1,3)
				if rand == 1 then anim.AnimationId = N_ANIM_ID; chosenAnim = 1 elseif rand == 2 then anim.AnimationId = N_ANIM_ID_2; chosenAnim = 2 else anim.AnimationId = N_ANIM_ID_3; chosenAnim = 3 end
			else
				targetPos = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0,HANG_MAX_DIST,0)
				anim.AnimationId = N_ANIM_ID
				chosenAnim = 1
			end
			hangStartPos = hrp.Position
			emoteTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
			emoteTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
			emoteTrack.Looped = true
			emoteTrack:Play(0.2)
			if not hangBeam then
				hangWorldAtt = Instance.new("Attachment", workspace.Terrain)
				hangWorldAtt.WorldPosition = targetPos
				hangMidAtt = Instance.new("Attachment", workspace.Terrain)
				if rightHand and leftHand then hangMidAtt.WorldPosition = (rightHand.Position + leftHand.Position) / 2 else hangMidAtt.WorldPosition = hrp.Position end
				if chosenAnim == 1 then
					hangBeam = Instance.new("Beam", workspace.Terrain)
					hangBeam.Texture = WEB_BEAM_TEXTURE; hangBeam.Width0 = 0.08; hangBeam.Width1 = 0.08; hangBeam.FaceCamera = true
					hangBeam.Attachment0 = hangWorldAtt; hangBeam.Attachment1 = hangMidAtt
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.J then
		local originalPos = hrp.CFrame
		local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local mySpeed = hrp.Velocity.Magnitude
		local combinedMomentum = (mySpeed + currentMomentum) * 3.5
		local totalThrowForce = math.clamp(1500 + combinedMomentum, 1500, 6000)
		local throwVel = (lookDir * totalThrowForce) + Vector3.new(0, totalThrowForce * 0.4, 0)
		local throwBv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
		throwBv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9,1e9,1e9)
		throwBv.Velocity = throwVel
		throwBv.Parent = hrp
		hrp.Velocity = throwVel
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(0.2)
			if not isEquipped then if throwBv then throwBv:Destroy() end return end
			pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.G, false, game) end)
			task.wait(0.05)
			if not isEquipped then if throwBv then throwBv:Destroy() end return end
			pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.G, false, game) end)
			task.wait(0.3)
			if not isEquipped then if throwBv then throwBv:Destroy() end return end
			if throwBv then throwBv:Destroy() end
			hrp.Anchored = true; hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero; hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
			for i = 1, 5 do hrp.CFrame = originalPos; RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
			hrp.Anchored = false
		end)
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.H then
		local originalPos = hrp.CFrame
		local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local mySpeed = hrp.Velocity.Magnitude
		local combinedMomentum = (mySpeed + currentMomentum) * 0.8
		local totalThrowForce = math.clamp(400 + combinedMomentum, 400, 1000)
		local throwVel = lookDir * totalThrowForce
		local throwBv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
		throwBv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9,1e9,1e9)
		throwBv.Velocity = throwVel
		throwBv.Parent = hrp
		hrp.Velocity = throwVel
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(0.2)
			if not isEquipped then if throwBv then throwBv:Destroy() end return end
			pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.G, false, game) end)
			task.wait(0.05)
			if not isEquipped then if throwBv then throwBv:Destroy() end return end
			pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.G, false, game) end)
			task.wait(0.3)
			if not isEquipped then if throwBv then throwBv:Destroy() end return end
			if throwBv then throwBv:Destroy() end
			hrp.Anchored = true; hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero; hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
			for i = 1, 5 do hrp.CFrame = originalPos; RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
			hrp.Anchored = false
		end)
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.slingshot] then handleSlingshot() end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.dash] then
		isHoldingR = true
		if swinging then
			local spawnPos = currentWebPoint
			local spawnHand = (activeWebHand == "Right") and rightHand or leftHand
			local spawnNormal = currentWebNormal
			swinging = false
			currentWebPoint = nil
			if swingTrack then swingTrack:Stop(0.3) end
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
			hideAllBeams()
			if spawnPos and spawnHand then task.spawn(function() pcall(function() spawnWebResidue(spawnPos, spawnHand.Position, spawnNormal) end) end) end
			local a = Instance.new("Animation")
			a.AnimationId = SWING_RELEASE_ANIM_ID
			swingReleaseTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(a)
			swingReleaseTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
			swingReleaseTrack:Play(0.2)
			swingReleaseTrack:AdjustSpeed(2)
		else
			handleDash()
		end
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.zip] then handleZip() end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.lunge] then
		if tick() - lastLandTime <= 2.0 and tick() - (lastLungeTime or 0) >= 2.0 then
			lastLungeTime = tick()
			isLunging = true
			playSound(DASH_SOUND_ID, 0.4, 1.5)
			StopAllAnimations()
			if not lungeTrack then
				local a = Instance.new("Animation")
				a.AnimationId = LUNGE_ANIM_ID
				lungeTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(a)
				lungeTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
				lungeTrack.Looped = false
			end
			lungeTrack:Play(0.1)
			lungeTrack:AdjustSpeed(1.2)
			local lungeDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
			hrp.Velocity = (lungeDir * 210) + Vector3.new(0, 70, 0)
			currentMomentum = currentMomentum + (65 * getMod())
			humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
			pcall(function() spawnLungeBurst(hrp) end)
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6, 1e6, 1e6)
			bv.Velocity = (lungeDir * 115) + Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)
			task.delay(0.3, function()
				if not swinging and not isSlingshotting and not isDashing and not isZipping then bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero end
			end)
			task.delay(1.0, function()
				if isLunging then
					isLunging = false
					if lungeTrack then lungeTrack:Stop(0.2) end
				end
			end)
		elseif not isLunging then
			if not isChargingJump and humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
				isChargingJump = true
				swinging = false
				stuckToWall = false
				StopAllAnimations()
				if not chargeTrack then
					local a = Instance.new("Animation")
					a.AnimationId = CHARGE_JUMP_ID
					chargeTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(a)
					chargeTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
				end
				chargeTrack:Play(0.1)
				chargeStartTime = tick()
				pcall(function()
					getgenv()._chargeVFX = createChargeVFX(hrp)
				end)
			end
		end
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.glide] then
		if stuckToWall and not isVaulting then
			handleLedgeVault()
		else
			gliding = true
			swinging = false
			stuckToWall = false
			StopAllAnimations()
			humanoid.PlatformStand = true
			if glideTrack then glideTrack:Play(0.1) end
		end
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
		if isHangingEmote then return end
		if stuckToWall then
			wallDebounce = true
			stuckToWall = false
			humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			hrp.CanCollide = true
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
			StopAllAnimations()
			if wallClimbEnabled then
				stopCrawlTrack()
				humanoid.AutoRotate = true
				hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position + (smoothWallNormal * 3), hrp.Position + (smoothWallNormal * 3) + Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z), Vector3.new(0,1,0))
				wallClimbEnabled = false
				slideLocked = true
				wallAttachDebounceTime = tick() + 0.35
				local wg = hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro")
				if wg then wg.MaxTorque = Vector3.zero end
			else
				humanoid.AutoRotate = true
				playSound(WALL_LUNGE_SOUND_ID, 1.0, 1)
				local camLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
				local intoWall = math.clamp(-camLook:Dot(smoothWallNormal), 0, 1)
				local lungeDir = (camLook + smoothWallNormal * intoWall * 0.7).Unit
				hrp.Velocity = (lungeDir * ((LUNGE_FORCE * getMod()) + currentMomentum)) + (Vector3.new(0, 50 * getMod(), 0)) + (smoothWallNormal * (30 * getMod()))
				hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + Vector3.new(lungeDir.X, 0, lungeDir.Z))
				applyShake(0.8)
				slideLocked = true
				wallAttachDebounceTime = tick() + 0.35
				local wg = hrp:FindFirstChild("WallGyro")
				if wg then wg.MaxTorque = Vector3.zero end
			end
			task.delay(0.35, function() wallDebounce = false end)
		else
			goingUp = true
		end
	end
end))

table.insert(connections, UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gpe)
	if not isWindowFocused then return end
	if UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= nil then return end
	if gpe or not isEquipped or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		if isHoldingMouse then
			isHoldingMouse = false
			local spawnPos = currentWebPoint
			local spawnHand = (activeWebHand == "Right") and rightHand or leftHand
			local spawnNormal = currentWebNormal
			local wasSwinging = swinging
			swinging = false
			currentWebPoint = nil
			if swingTrack then swingTrack:Stop(0.3) end
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
			hideAllBeams()
			if wasSwinging and spawnPos and spawnHand then task.spawn(function() pcall(function() spawnWebResidue(spawnPos, spawnHand.Position, spawnNormal) end) end) end
		end
	end

	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then 
		isLooping = false 
	end

	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.dash] then isHoldingR = false end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then isCinematic = false end
	if (input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftAlt or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightAlt) then
		isHoldingAlt = false
		if isTricking then isTricking = false; if trickTrack then trickTrack:Stop(0.2) end end
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then goingUp = false end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.lunge] and isLunging then
	elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.lunge] and isChargingJump then
		isChargingJump = false
		bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
		if chargeTrack then chargeTrack:Stop(0.1) end
		local heldTime = tick() - chargeStartTime
		local powerPercent = math.clamp(heldTime / MAX_CHARGE_TIME, 0, 1)
		local totalPower = (CHARGE_MIN_POWER * getMod()) + (powerPercent * ((CHARGE_MAX_POWER * getMod()) - (CHARGE_MIN_POWER * getMod())))
		hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(hrp.Velocity.X, totalPower, hrp.Velocity.Z)
		playSound(SLINGSHOT_BOOM_ID, 2.5, 1, 0)
		humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
		pcall(function() destroyChargeVFX(getgenv()._chargeVFX); getgenv()._chargeVFX = nil end)
		pcall(function() spawnLungeBurst(hrp) end)
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[keybinds.glide] then
		if gliding then
			gliding = false
			humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
			if glideTrack then glideTrack:Stop(0.15) end
			bv.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
		end
	end
end))

if SCRIPT_ACCESS_GRANTED then
	setup()
	createTool()
end

table.insert(connections, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
	task.spawn(function()
		local newHum = newChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
		local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
		if not newHum then return end
		for i = 1, 10 do
			task.wait(0.15)
			pcall(function()
				newHum.PlatformStand = false
				newHum.AutoRotate = true
				newHum.Sit = false
				newHum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
				if newHrp then newHrp.Anchored = false end
			end)
			if newHum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running then break end
		end
	end)
end))

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(10) and getgenv().SpideyRunId == runId do
		pcall(saveConfig)
	end
end)
